      o.wellFrame = mg(P);
      const R = []; for (const s of [-1, 1]) R.push([uvScale(new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.3, 0.06, 1.25), 2.5, 1), mat4(0, 2.78, s * 0.52, 0, 1, 1, 1, s * 0.62)]);
      o.wellRoof = mg(R); o.rope = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.015, 0.015, 1.25, 4).translate(0, 1.1, 0); }
    for (const k in o) SHARED.add(o[k]); return o; })();

  // ── مواد الأرض: الطريق (تراب + حصى بتجمعات طبيعية، كثافة أعلى بالنص وتخف للأطراف، حواف تذوب بالعشب) والساحة (تراب مداس + بقع حصى وتآكل) ──
  function groundMat(kind) { const m = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xffffff, roughness: 0.96, metalness: 0, transparent: true, depthWrite: false, polygonOffset: true,
      polygonOffsetFactor: kind === 'plaza' ? -3 : -2, polygonOffsetUnits: kind === 'plaza' ? -3 : -2, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
    if (OFF) return m; m.envMapIntensity = 0.25;
    m.onBeforeCompile = sh => { Object.assign(sh.uniforms, { gD: { value: TX.dirt }, gG: { value: TX.gravel }, gP: { value: TX.plaza }, gDN: { value: TX.dirtN }, gGN: { value: TX.gravelN }, gPN: { value: TX.plazaN }, gNz: { value: ROADTEX ? ROADTEX.nz : TX.dirtN } });
      sh.vertexShader = sh.vertexShader.replace('#include <common>', '#include <common>\nattribute vec3 rq;\nvarying vec3 vRq;\nvarying vec2 vWp;')
        .replace('#include <begin_vertex>', '#include <begin_vertex>\nvRq = rq; vWp = (modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0)).xz;');
      sh.fragmentShader = sh.fragmentShader.replace('#include <common>', '#include <common>\nvarying vec3 vRq;\nvarying vec2 vWp;\nuniform sampler2D gD, gG, gP, gDN, gGN, gPN, gNz;')
        .replace('#include <map_fragment>', kind === 'plaza' ? `
    vec2 wp = vWp; vec3 n1 = texture2D(gNz, wp * 0.031).rgb, n2 = texture2D(gNz, wp * 0.093).rgb, n3 = texture2D(gNz, wp * 0.29).rgb;
    float rr = vRq.x, R = vRq.z;                                   // rq.x = البعد عن مركز الساحة، rq.z = نصف القطر
    vec3 cP = texture2D(gP, wp * 0.21).rgb, cD = texture2D(gD, wp * 0.27 + 0.37).rgb, cG = texture2D(gG, wp * 0.38).rgb * vec3(0.8, 0.74, 0.65);
    float wear = smoothstep(0.45, 0.72, n1.b * 0.7 + n2.r * 0.3);   // بقع تراب مداس داكن
    float grv = smoothstep(0.55, 0.78, n1.g * 0.6 + n3.b * 0.4) * (1.0 - smoothstep(R * 0.55, R * 0.95, rr));
    vec3 col = mix(cP, cD * vec3(0.92, 0.9, 0.88), wear * 0.8); col = mix(col, cG, grv * 0.85);
    col *= 0.88 + 0.22 * n2.g; col *= mix(0.86, 1.0, smoothstep(1.2, 3.2, rr));   // أغمق قرب البئر (رطوبة)
    float gmS = grv; float edge = smoothstep(R - 3.2, R, rr + (n2.b - 0.5) * 3.4 + (n3.r - 0.5) * 1.2);
    diffuseColor.rgb *= col; diffuseColor.a *= 1.0 - edge;` : `
    float ax = vRq.x, al = vRq.y, hw = max(vRq.z, 0.5), aa = abs(ax); vec2 wp = vWp;
    vec3 n1 = texture2D(gNz, wp * 0.043).rgb, n2 = texture2D(gNz, wp * 0.127).rgb, n3 = texture2D(gNz, wp * 0.41).rgb;
    vec3 cD = texture2D(gD, wp * 0.30).rgb, cG = texture2D(gG, wp * 0.36).rgb * vec3(0.8, 0.74, 0.65), cG2 = texture2D(gG, wp * 0.11 + 0.5).rgb * vec3(0.74, 0.68, 0.6);
    float centre = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.1 * hw, 0.92 * hw, aa + (n2.r - 0.5) * 1.1 * hw);          // حصى أكثر بالنص
    float clus = smoothstep(0.38, 0.7, n1.g * 0.6 + n2.b * 0.3 + n3.r * 0.25 - 0.08);              // تجمعات غير منتظمة
    float gm = clamp(centre * (0.25 + 0.95 * clus) + (n3.g - 0.6) * 0.5, 0.0, 1.0);
    float rut = 1.0 - smoothstep(0.08, 0.3, abs(aa - hw * 0.42 + (n1.r - 0.5) * 0.4));             // أثر عجلات خفيف ومتعرج
    vec3 col = mix(cD, mix(cG, cG2, 0.3), gm); col *= 1.0 - rut * 0.1 * (1.0 - gm); col *= 0.88 + 0.24 * n1.r;
    col = mix(col, col * vec3(0.95, 1.0, 0.86), smoothstep(hw - 1.0, hw, aa) * 0.6);
    float gmS = gm; float edge = smoothstep(hw - 0.95, hw + 0.05, aa + (n2.g - 0.5) * 0.9 + (n3.b - 0.5) * 0.4);
    diffuseColor.rgb *= col; diffuseColor.a *= 1.0 - edge;`)
        .replace('#include <normal_fragment_maps>', kind === 'plaza' ? `
    { vec3 a = texture2D(gPN, wp * 0.21).xyz * 2.0 - 1.0, b = texture2D(gGN, wp * 0.38).xyz * 2.0 - 1.0; vec3 tn = normalize(mix(a, b, gmS)); tn.xy *= 1.3;
      vec3 nw = normalize(vec3(tn.x, tn.z, tn.y)); normal = normalize((viewMatrix * vec4(nw, 0.0)).xyz); }` : `
    { vec3 a = texture2D(gDN, wp * 0.30).xyz * 2.0 - 1.0, b = texture2D(gGN, wp * 0.36).xyz * 2.0 - 1.0; vec3 tn = normalize(mix(a, b, gmS)); tn.xy *= 1.35;
      vec3 nw = normalize(vec3(tn.x, tn.z, tn.y)); normal = normalize((viewMatrix * vec4(nw, 0.0)).xyz); }`); };
    m.customProgramCacheKey = () => 'vil4-' + kind; return m; }
  const LANE = groundMat('lane'), PLAZA = groundMat('plaza'); GLB_MAT.add(LANE); GLB_MAT.add(PLAZA);
  // قرص الساحة يتبع التضاريس: شبكة حلقات، rq = (البعد، 0، نصف القطر)
  function plazaMesh(L, v, R) { const P = [], Q = [], I = [], NR = 14, NA = 48; P.push(0, LIB.height(v.x, v.z) + 0.1, 0); Q.push(0, 0, R);
    for (let i = 1; i <= NR; i++) { const rr = R * i / NR; for (let k = 0; k < NA; k++) { const a = k / NA * 6.283, x = v.x + Math.cos(a) * rr, z = v.z + Math.sin(a) * rr; P.push(x - v.x, LIB.height(x, z) + 0.1, z - v.z); Q.push(rr, 0, R); } }
    for (let k = 0; k < NA; k++) I.push(0, 1 + (k + 1) % NA, 1 + k);
    for (let i = 1; i < NR; i++) for (let k = 0; k < NA; k++) { const a = 1 + (i - 1) * NA + k, b = 1 + (i - 1) * NA + (k + 1) % NA, c = a + NA, d = b + NA; I.push(a, b, c, b, d, c); }
    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(), nm = new Float32Array(P.length); for (let i = 1; i < nm.length; i += 3) nm[i] = 1;
    g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(P, 3)); g.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(nm, 3)); g.setAttribute('rq', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(Q, 3)); g.setIndex(I);
    const m = new THREE.Mesh(g, PLAZA); m.receiveShadow = true; m.renderOrder = 1; L.g.add(m); return m; }

  // ── النماذج: أغراض Poly Haven (CC0) + عربة وصخور ممسوحة + سنديانة + شجيرات ──
  const K = {}, waitQ = [];
  const fixMat = m => { if (m.map) { m.map.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; m.map.anisotropy = aniso(); } m.envMap = CHARENV; m.envMapIntensity = 0.35; GLB_MAT.add(m); };
  // كل عقدة علوية → قائمة أجزاء {geo, mat, m} مطبّعة: القاعدة y=0 والمركز x,z=0 (القياس الحقيقي محفوظ)
  const kitOf = (sc, filter, pre) => { sc.updateMatrixWorld(true); for (const top of sc.children) { const nm = (pre || '') + top.name; if (filter && !filter(top.name)) continue;
      const B = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(top), c = B.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3()), sh = new THREE.Matrix4().makeTranslation(-c.x, -B.min.y, -c.z), out = [];
      top.traverse(o => { if (o.isMesh) { fixMat(o.material); GLB_GEO.add(o.geometry); out.push({ geo: o.geometry, mat: o.material, m: sh.clone().multiply(o.matrixWorld) }); } });
      if (out.length) { out.size = B.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()); K[nm] = out; } } };
  const done = () => { st.loaded++; if (st.loaded >= st.need) { st.ms = Math.round(performance.now() - st.t0); for (const f of waitQ.splice(0)) try { f(); } catch (e) { console.warn('[vil4] late', e); } } };
  if (!OFF) { const ld = new GLTFLoader(); ld.setMeshoptDecoder(MeshoptDecoder); const LD = (url, ok) => { const bad = () => { st.failed.push(url); console.warn('[vil4] تعذّر ' + url); done(); };
      try { (window.__qLoad || ((l, u, a, p, b) => l.load(u, a, p, b)))(ld, url, g => { try { ok(g.scene); } catch (e) { st.failed.push(url); console.warn('[vil4]', e); } done(); }, undefined, bad); } catch (e) { bad(); } };
    LD('village/v4/village-props-v4' + (PH ? '-lite' : '') + '.glb', sc => kitOf(sc));
    LD('village/v4/road-props' + (PH ? '-lite' : '') + '.glb', sc => kitOf(sc, n => /^(cart|rk\d|bd\d)$/.test(n), 'R_'));
    LD('trees33/oak-large-mid.glb?k=oak3', sc => { sc.name = 'oak'; const g = new THREE.Group(); g.add(sc); sc.name = 'OAK'; kitOf(g); });
    LD('nature/nature-plants-lite.glb', sc => kitOf(sc, n => /^bush_(myrtle|broom)/.test(n), 'B_'));
    // v45g: بوابة v5 — نموذج واحد بإحداثياته الأصلية (X على امتداد السور، +Z نحو داخل القرية، y=0 الأرض)
    LD('village/v4/gate-v5' + (PH && !/[?&]gatehi=1/.test(location.search) ? '-lite' : '') + '.glb', sc => { sc.updateMatrixWorld(true); const out = [], B = new THREE.Box3();
      sc.traverse(o => { if (o.isMesh) { fixMat(o.material); GLB_GEO.add(o.geometry); out.push({ geo: o.geometry, mat: o.material, m: o.matrixWorld.clone() }); B.union(new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(o)); } });
      if (out.length) { out.size = B.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()); K.H_gate = out; } }); }
  const ready = () => OFF || st.loaded >= st.need || performance.now() - st.t0 > 90000;
  // ── مجموعات النسخ لكل قرية: L.v4.inst[name] = [{m4}], تُنشأ InstancedMesh عند جاهزية النموذج ──
  function V(L) { return L.v4 || (L.v4 = { inst: {}, made: false, chunks: [], meshes: [] }); }
  function put(L, v, name, x, z, y, yaw, sc, opt) { if (PH) { if (name === 'P_wine') return; if (name === 'P_crate1') name = 'P_crate2'; } const s = Array.isArray(sc) ? sc : [sc, sc, sc]; const o = opt || {};
    (V(L).inst[name] || (V(L).inst[name] = [])).push({ m: mat4(x - v.x, y, z - v.z, yaw, s[0], s[1], s[2], o.rx, o.rz), sh: o.shadow !== false, fit: o.fit, col: o.col }); }
  function makeInst(L) { const S = V(L); if (S.made) return; S.made = true; let draws = 0, tris = 0;
    for (const name in S.inst) { const parts = K[name], list = S.inst[name]; if (!parts || !list.length) { if (!parts) st.failed.push('kit:' + name); continue; }
      const sh = list.some(it => it.sh);
      for (const pt of parts) { const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(pt.geo, pt.mat, list.length); im.castShadow = sh && !/^P_peb|^R_rk/.test(name) && (!PH || /^H_|OAK|cart|barrel|bale|^B_/i.test(name)); im.receiveShadow = true; im.userData.vil4 = name;
        list.forEach((it, i) => { let m4 = it.m; if (it.fit) { const z0 = parts.size; const k = it.fit / Math.max(z0.x, z0.z, 1e-3); m4 = m4.clone().multiply(new THREE.Matrix4().makeScale(k, k, k)); }
          im.setMatrixAt(i, _m.multiplyMatrices(m4, pt.m)); if (it.col) im.setColorAt(i, typeof it.col === 'function' ? (it.col(pt.mat.name || '') || WHITE) : it.col); });
        im.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; if (im.instanceColor) im.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true; im.computeBoundingSphere(); L.g.add(im); S.meshes.push(im);
        draws++; tris += (pt.geo.index ? pt.geo.index.count : pt.geo.attributes.position.count) / 3 * list.length; } }
    S.draws = draws; S.tris = Math.round(tris); }
  // هندسة مصنوعة مكرّرة (أوتاد، عوارض، أعمدة، مقاعد...)
  function addG(L, v, key, mat, x, y, z, ry, sx, sy, sz, opt) { const S = V(L), k = key + '|' + mat; (S.g || (S.g = {}))[k] || (S.g[k] = { key, mat, items: [], shadow: !(opt && opt.noShadow) });
    S.g[k].items.push({ m: mat4(x - v.x, y, z - v.z, ry, sx, sy, sz, opt && opt.rx, opt && opt.rz), col: opt && opt.col }); }
  function makeG(L) { const S = V(L); if (!S.g) return; for (const k in S.g) { const E = S.g[k], geo = G[E.key]; const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(geo, M[E.mat], E.items.length);
      E.items.forEach((it, i) => { im.setMatrixAt(i, it.m); if (it.col) im.setColorAt(i, it.col); }); im.instanceMatrix.needsUpdate = true; if (im.instanceColor) im.instanceColor.needsUpdate = true;
      im.castShadow = E.shadow && (!PH || /stake|post|cap|box|rail|foot|well|bale|lathe|roof/.test(E.key)); im.receiveShadow = true; im.computeBoundingSphere(); im.userData.vil4 = E.key; L.g.add(im); S.meshes.push(im);
      S.draws = (S.draws || 0) + 1; S.tris = (S.tris || 0) + Math.round((geo.index ? geo.index.count : geo.attributes.position.count) / 3 * E.items.length); } S.g = null; }
  const ao = (L, v, x, z, w, d, yaw, k) => addG(L, v, 'ao', 'ao', x, LIB.height(x, z) + 0.06, z, yaw || 0, w, 1, d, { noShadow: true });
  const tint = (r, g, b) => new THREE.Color(r, g, b);

  // ── ضجيج قيم بسيط (للتجمعات الطبيعية) ──
  const hsh = (x, z) => { const s = Math.sin(x * 127.1 + z * 311.7) * 43758.5453; return s - Math.floor(s); };
  const vn = (x, z) => { const xi = Math.floor(x), zi = Math.floor(z), xf = x - xi, zf = z - zi, u = xf * xf * (3 - 2 * xf), w = zf * zf * (3 - 2 * zf);
    return (hsh(xi, zi) * (1 - u) + hsh(xi + 1, zi) * u) * (1 - w) + (hsh(xi, zi + 1) * (1 - u) + hsh(xi + 1, zi + 1) * u) * w; };
  const fbm = (x, z) => vn(x, z) * 0.6 + vn(x * 2.3 + 7, z * 2.3 + 3) * 0.3 + vn(x * 5.1 + 1, z * 5.1 + 9) * 0.1;
  const H = (x, z) => LIB.height(x, z);
  const hv = (x, z) => { const a = hsh(Math.round(x * 3), Math.round(z * 3)), b = hsh(Math.round(z * 7) + 3, Math.round(x * 5) + 1);
    return { s: 0.86 + a * 0.28, sy: 0.94 + b * 0.14, wood: [tint(0.86, 0.86, 0.9), tint(1.06, 0.96, 0.86), tint(0.8, 0.74, 0.68), tint(0.96, 0.92, 0.88)][Math.floor(b * 4) % 4],
      roof: [tint(1.08, 1.0, 0.86), tint(0.84, 0.82, 0.77), tint(0.72, 0.68, 0.62), tint(0.98, 0.9, 0.78)][Math.floor(a * 4.7) % 4], k: Math.floor(a * 1000) }; };
  // ── الساحة: أصغر ومعاشة ──
  const KSLOT = [[1, 1.05], [2, 2.15], [3, -1.05], [4, -2.15]], KR = 10.2, SQR = 12.5;
  function slotPos(v, ga, a, r) { return [v.x + Math.cos(ga + a) * r, v.z + Math.sin(ga + a) * r]; }
  function square(L, v, ga) { if (OFF) return; const P = (a, r) => slotPos(v, ga, a, r);
    plazaMesh(L, v, SQR);
    // البئر الحجري بسقف خشبي
    { const y0 = H(v.x, v.z) - 0.04, yaw = -ga; addG(L, v, 'wellWall', 'stone', v.x, y0, v.z, 0, 1, 1, 1); addG(L, v, 'wellWater', 'water', v.x, y0, v.z, 0, 1, 1, 1, { noShadow: true });
      addG(L, v, 'wellFrame', 'bark', v.x, y0, v.z, yaw, 1, 1, 1); addG(L, v, 'wellRoof', 'plank', v.x, y0, v.z, yaw, 1, 1, 1); addG(L, v, 'rope', 'rope', v.x, y0, v.z, 0, 1, 1, 1, { noShadow: true });
      put(L, v, 'P_bucket', v.x, v.z, y0 + 0.62, 0.3, 1, { fit: 0.36 }); ao(L, v, v.x, v.z, 4.2, 4.2); L.solid[0] && topOf(L.solid[0], y0 + 0.96); }
    // السنديانة الكبيرة مع ظلها
    { const [x, z] = P(Math.PI, 7.7); put(L, v, 'OAK', x, z, H(x, z) - 0.15, hsh(x, z) * 6.28, 1, { fit: 12.5 }); ao(L, v, x, z, 3.2, 3.2);
      L.solid.push(topOf([x, z, 0.62], H(x, z) + 12)); L.obst.push([x, z, 1.1]);
      for (const [da, rr] of [[0, 2.7], [2.1, 2.6], [-2.1, 2.7]]) { const a = ga + Math.PI + Math.PI + da, bx = x + Math.cos(a) * rr, bz = z + Math.sin(a) * rr, yaw = Math.atan2(bx - x, bz - z);
        addG(L, v, 'bench', 'plank', bx, H(bx, bz) - 0.02, bz, yaw + Math.PI, 1, 1, 1); ao(L, v, bx, bz, 2.4, 0.9, yaw); L.obst.push([bx, bz, 1.0]);
        L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(bx, bz, 1.9, 0.5, yaw), H(bx, bz) + 0.9)); L.poi.bench.push({ x: bx + Math.sin(yaw) * 0.9, z: bz + Math.cos(yaw) * 0.9, type: 'bench' }); } }
    // مقعدان قرب البئر
    for (const a of [Math.PI / 2, -Math.PI / 2]) { const [bx, bz] = P(a, 3.9), yaw = Math.atan2(v.x - bx, v.z - bz); addG(L, v, 'bench', 'plank', bx, H(bx, bz) - 0.02, bz, yaw + Math.PI, 1, 1, 1);
      ao(L, v, bx, bz, 2.4, 0.9, yaw); L.obst.push([bx, bz, 1.0]); L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(bx, bz, 1.9, 0.5, yaw), H(bx, bz) + 0.9)); L.poi.bench.push({ x: bx + Math.sin(yaw) * 0.9, z: bz + Math.cos(yaw) * 0.9, type: 'bench' }); }
    // تبن: بالات مكدسة + كومة
    { const [x, z] = P(Math.PI - 0.45, 13.2), yaw = ga + 1.1, y = H(x, z) - 0.03; const o = (lx, lz) => { const q = rotL(lx, lz, yaw); return [x + q[0], z + q[1]]; };
      for (const [lx, lz, ly, ry] of [[-0.6, 0, 0, 0], [0.6, 0.05, 0, 0.05], [0, 0.02, 0.5, -0.08], [1.7, 0.6, 0, 1.4]]) { const [bx, bz] = o(lx, lz); addG(L, v, 'bale', 'straw', bx, y + ly, bz, yaw + ry, 1.1, 0.5, 0.62); }
      const [mx, mz] = o(-1.6, 0.9); addG(L, v, 'mound', 'straw', mx, y - 0.05, mz, 0.3, 0.9, 0.45, 0.8); ao(L, v, x, z, 4.4, 2.6, yaw); L.obst.push([x, z, 1.8]); L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(x, z, 2.6, 0.8, yaw), y + 1.0)); }
    // عربة خشبية + كومة تبن صغيرة
    { const [x, z] = P(Math.PI + 0.5, 12.8), yaw = ga + 0.5 + Math.PI / 2 + 0.3; put(L, v, 'R_cart', x, z, H(x, z) - 0.02, yaw, 1, { fit: 3.1 }); ao(L, v, x, z, 3.4, 2.0, yaw);
      L.obst.push([x, z, 1.7]); L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(x, z, 1.5, 3.0, yaw), H(x, z) + 1.0)); const q = rotL(1.4, -0.9, yaw); addG(L, v, 'mound', 'straw', x + q[0], H(x + q[0], z + q[1]) - 0.05, z + q[1], 0, 0.7, 0.35, 0.6); }
    // جذع تقطيع + فأس + حطب
    { const [x, z] = P(Math.PI - 0.2, 13.7); put(L, v, 'P_stump', x, z, H(x, z) - 0.05, hsh(x, z) * 6, 1, { fit: 0.75 }); ao(L, v, x, z, 1.4, 1.4); L.obst.push([x, z, 0.7]);
      const yaw = ga + Math.PI / 2; const q = rotL(1.3, 0.2, yaw); addG(L, v, 'woodpile', 'bark', x + q[0], H(x + q[0], z + q[1]) - 0.03, z + q[1], yaw, 1, 1, 1); ao(L, v, x + q[0], z + q[1], 1.8, 1.0, yaw); }
    // مجموعتا صناديق وبراميل وسلال بين الأكشاك
    for (const sd of [1, -1]) { const [x, z] = P(sd * 1.6, 12.3), yaw = ga + sd * 1.6 + Math.PI / 2, o = (lx, lz) => { const q = rotL(lx, lz, yaw); return [x + q[0], z + q[1]]; }, y = H(x, z) - 0.02;
      const items = sd > 0 ? [['P_crate1', -0.7, 0, 0, 0.8, 0.1], ['P_crate2', -0.7, 0.05, 0.62, 0.68, -0.2], ['P_barrel', 0.45, 0.1, 0, 0.62, 0.3], ['P_basket', 1.2, 0.5, 0, 0.5, 1], ['P_wine', 0.2, 0.9, 0, 0.75, 1.57]]
        : [['P_barrel2', -0.5, 0, 0, 0.75, 0.5], ['P_barrel', 0.35, 0.15, 0, 0.62, 2], ['P_crate1', 1.2, -0.1, 0, 0.75, 0.6], ['P_bucket', 0.4, 0.85, 0, 0.36, 0], ['P_basket', -1.3, 0.5, 0, 0.5, 2]];
      for (const [nm, lx, lz, ly, fit, ry] of items) { const [px, pz] = o(lx, lz); put(L, v, nm, px, pz, y + ly, yaw + ry, 1, { fit }); }
      ao(L, v, x, z, 3.6, 2.0, yaw); L.obst.push([x, z, 1.4]); L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(x, z, 2.6, 1.0, yaw), y + 0.95)); }
    // سلة ودلو قرب البئر
    { const [x, z] = P(0.35, 2.0); put(L, v, 'P_basket', x, z, H(x, z) - 0.02, 0.5, 1, { fit: 0.48 }); const [x2, z2] = P(-0.5, 1.9); put(L, v, 'P_bucket', x2, z2, H(x2, z2) - 0.02, 1, 1, { fit: 0.36 }); }
    // حصى ممسوح متناثر بتجمعات حول الساحة
    pebbles(L, v, (cb) => { for (let i = 0; i < (PH ? 160 : 380); i++) { const a = hsh(i, 3.3) * 6.283, rr = 2 + Math.sqrt(hsh(i, 9.1)) * (SQR - 3); cb(v.x + Math.cos(a) * rr, v.z + Math.sin(a) * rr, 0.6); } });
    (L._foot || (L._foot = [])).push({ x: v.x, z: v.z, c: 1, s: 0, hw: 7.5, hd: 7.5 });   // لا عشب في قلب الساحة
  }
  // ── حصى حقيقي: نسخ صغيرة غاطسة جزئياً، أحجام متفاوتة، كثافة حسب ضجيج التجمعات ──
  const PEB = ['P_peb_a', 'P_peb_b', 'P_peb_c', 'P_peb_d', 'P_peb_e'];
  function pebbles(L, v, gen) { gen((x, z, k) => { const n = fbm(x * 0.12, z * 0.12); if (n < 0.42 && hsh(x, z) > 0.25) return;
      const nn = 1 + Math.floor(hsh(z, x) * 3 * k * (n + 0.2)); for (let j = 0; j < nn; j++) { const px = x + (hsh(x + j, z) - 0.5) * 0.9, pz = z + (hsh(z - j, x) - 0.5) * 0.9, sz = 0.05 + Math.pow(hsh(px, pz), 2.2) * 0.17;
        put(L, v, PEB[Math.floor(hsh(px * 3, pz) * 5) % 5], px, pz, H(px, pz) - sz * 0.32, hsh(pz, px) * 6.28, 1, { fit: sz, shadow: false, rx: (hsh(px, 1) - 0.5) * 0.6, rz: (hsh(1, pz) - 0.5) * 0.6 }); } }); }
  // طرق القرية: الحصى على امتداد كل شريط، وأحجار أكبر على الحواف
  function lanes(L, v) { if (OFF || !L._lanes) return; let n = 0, cap = PH ? 700 : 1600;
    for (const [pts, w] of L._lanes) { for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) { const a = pts[i], b = pts[i + 1], len = Math.hypot(b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]), ux = (b[0] - a[0]) / len, uz = (b[1] - a[1]) / len;
        for (let t = 0; t < len && n < cap; t += 0.7) { const cx = a[0] + ux * t, cz = a[1] + uz * t; const lat = (hsh(cx, cz) - 0.5) * w * 0.75 * (0.5 + hsh(cz, cx));
          pebbles(L, v, cb => { cb(cx - uz * lat, cz + ux * lat, 1); n++; });
          if (hsh(cx * 0.7, cz * 1.3) > 0.93) { const s = hsh(cz, 2) > 0.5 ? 1 : -1, ex = cx - uz * s * (w / 2 + 0.2), ez = cz + ux * s * (w / 2 + 0.2), sz = 0.22 + hsh(ex, ez) * 0.3;
            put(L, v, hsh(ex, 5) > 0.5 ? 'R_rk' + (Math.floor(hsh(ez, 7) * 5) % 5) : 'P_stone', ex, ez, H(ex, ez) - sz * 0.3, hsh(ez, ex) * 6.28, 1, { fit: sz, shadow: false }); } } } }
    st.pebbleSteps = n; }

  // ── حدود القرية: سور أوتاد مدببة متين (2.5–3.6م) بأساس حجري على مقاطع، عوارض داخلية، أبراج زوايا، وبوابة جذوع ──
  function palisade(L, v, ga) { if (OFF) return false; const R = 62, g0 = 4.1 / R, da = 0.31 / R, N = Math.floor((6.283 - 2 * g0) / da), RUN = 9; let runs = 0, stakes = 0;
    const P = (a, r) => [v.x + Math.cos(a) * (r || R), v.z + Math.sin(a) * (r || R)];
    for (let i0 = 0; i0 < N; i0 += RUN) { const aA = ga + g0 + i0 * da, aB = ga + g0 + Math.min(N, i0 + RUN) * da, am = (aA + aB) / 2, p = P(aA), q = P(aB), hp = H(p[0], p[1]), hq = H(q[0], q[1]);
      if (Math.min(hp, hq) < 0.4) continue; runs++;
      const nn = fbm(am * 9.1, 3.7), foot = nn > 0.52 || Math.abs(hq - hp) > 0.45, hs = 0.88 + fbm(am * 4.3, 1.1) * 0.42, tang = -am - Math.PI / 2;   // ارتفاع المقطع يتغير
      const [mx, mz] = P(am), hm = Math.min(hp, hq), len = Math.hypot(q[0] - p[0], q[1] - p[1]) + 0.08;
      if (foot) { addG(L, v, 'foot', 'stone', mx, hm - 0.35, mz, tang, len, 0.95, 0.78); }
      for (let i = i0; i < Math.min(N, i0 + RUN); i++) { const a = ga + g0 + (i + 0.5) * da, [x, z] = P(a), h0 = H(x, z), r = 0.13 + hsh(i, 1.7) * 0.05, h = hs * (0.9 + hsh(i, 4.2) * 0.2) * (hsh(i, 8.8) > 0.96 ? 0.72 : 1);
        const k = 0.78 + hsh(i, 2.9) * 0.3, gr = hsh(i, 6.1); addG(L, v, 'stake', 'bark', x, (foot ? hm + 0.5 : h0 - 0.35), z, hsh(i, 3.3) * 6.28, r, h * (foot ? 0.92 : 1.05), r,
          { rx: (hsh(i, 5.5) - 0.5) * 0.05, rz: (hsh(i, 7.7) - 0.5) * 0.05, col: tint(k * (1 + gr * 0.08), k, k * (1 - gr * 0.06)) }); stakes++; }
      for (const ry of [0.95, 2.15]) { const [rx, rz] = P(am, R - 0.24); addG(L, v, 'rail', 'bark', rx, hm + ry + (foot ? 0.4 : 0), rz, tang, len + 0.05, 0.075, 0.075); }
      if (runs % 8 === 4) { const [tx, tz] = P(aA, R - 0.15), th = H(tx, tz); addG(L, v, 'post', 'bark', tx, th - 0.4, tz, am, 0.3, 4.6, 0.3); addG(L, v, 'cap', 'plank', tx, th + 4.15, tz, am, 0.55, 0.55, 0.55);
        for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const [bx, bz] = P(aA + s * 0.9 / R, R - 0.9); addG(L, v, 'rail', 'bark', bx, th + 1.2, bz, tang + (s > 0 ? 0 : Math.PI), 1.0, 0.07, 0.07, { rz: 0.9, rx: 0 }); }
        L.solid.push(topOf([tx, tz, 0.4], th + 4.2)); }
      ao(L, v, mx, mz, len + 0.4, 1.3, tang);
      L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(mx, mz, len, 0.42, -am + Math.PI / 2 - Math.PI / 2 + Math.PI / 2), hm + 2.5)); }
    // البوابة v5 (v45g): نموذج GLB منحوت؛ نفس المواضع والتصادم. إن تعذّر تحميله تُبنى البوابة الإجرائية القديمة.
    { const [gx, gz] = P(ga), ux = Math.cos(ga), uz = Math.sin(ga), nx = -uz, nz = ux, yaw = -ga - Math.PI / 2, fb = L._gfb = [];
      const hs = [-1, 0, 1].map(s => H(gx + nx * s * 3.4, gz + nz * s * 3.4)), gy = Math.min(...hs) - 0.03;
      put(L, v, 'H_gate', gx, gz, gy, yaw, 1);
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const px = gx + nx * s * 3.4, pz = gz + nz * s * 3.4, py = H(px, pz);
        fb.push(['post', 'bark', px, py - 0.5, pz, s, 0.36, 6.0, 0.36], ['cap', 'plank', px, py + 5.45, pz, 0, 0.62, 0.6, 0.62]);
        L.solid.push(topOf([px, pz, 0.5], gy + 6.1));
        // درفة ألواح مفتوحة ~92° نحو الداخل (محورها عند زاوية العمود الداخلية)
        const lx = gx + nx * s * 3.21 - ux * 1.82, lz = gz + nz * s * 3.21 - uz * 1.82;
        const ox = gx + nx * s * 3.05 - ux * 1.45, oz = gz + nz * s * 3.05 - uz * 1.45;
        fb.push(['box', 'plank', ox - nx * s * 0.05, H(ox, oz) + 0.12, oz - nz * s * 0.05, yaw + Math.PI / 2, 2.8, 2.7, 0.1]);
        L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(lx, lz, 3.2, 0.28, yaw + Math.PI / 2), H(lx, lz) + 3.1)); ao(L, v, px, pz, 1.5, 1.5); }
      { const bx = gx - nx * 3.7 - ux * 0.75, bz = gz - nz * 3.7 - uz * 0.75; L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(bx, bz, 0.35, 1.1, yaw), gy + 2.5)); }   // العارضة المسنودة
      for (const [yy, rr] of [[4.65, 0.2], [4.05, 0.15]]) fb.push(['rail', 'bark', gx, gy + yy, gz, yaw, 7.9, rr, rr]);
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) fb.push(['box', 'plank', gx - ux * s * 0.45, gy + 5.05 + 0.18, gz - uz * s * 0.45, yaw, 8.6, 0.07, 1.2, { rx: s * 0.42 }]);
      st.gate = 5; }
    st.palisade = { runs, stakes }; return true; }
  // ── البستان: حوض تراب بأثلام + صفوف ملفوف/خضار + سياج قصب (عقدة FenceWattle من سور v3b) ──
  function garden(L, v, x, z, y) { if (OFF) return false; const yaw = Math.atan2(x - v.x, z - v.z), W = 5.4, D = 4.6;
    addG(L, v, 'box', 'soil', x, y - 0.18, z, yaw, W, 0.3, D, { noShadow: true });
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { const lz = -D / 2 + 0.45 + i * (D - 0.9) / 5; for (let j = 0; j < 7; j++) { const lx = -W / 2 + 0.45 + j * (W - 0.9) / 6 + (hsh(i, j) - 0.5) * 0.15, q = rotL(lx, lz, yaw);
      if (hsh(j, i) < 0.08) continue; const s = 0.17 + hsh(i + 3, j) * 0.12; addG(L, v, 'cabbage', 'crop', x + q[0], y + 0.1, z + q[1], hsh(i, j + 5) * 6, s, s, s, { noShadow: PH }); } }
    (L._wat || (L._wat = [])).push({ x, z, y, yaw, W, D }); ao(L, v, x, z, W + 0.8, D + 0.8, yaw); return true; }
  // ── تنويع البيوت: أغراض حول كل بيت (حطب، براميل، صناديق، تبن، عربة، جذع) ──
  function houseProps(L, v, key, x, z, y, yaw, sc) { if (OFF) return; const D = HOUSE_DEFS[key]; if (!D) return; const h = hv(x, z), w2 = D.w / 2 * sc, d2 = D.d / 2 * sc, kinds = ['wood', 'barrels', 'crates', 'hay', 'cart', 'stump', 'basket'];
    const pick = [kinds[h.k % 7], kinds[(h.k >> 3) % 7], kinds[(h.k >> 6) % 7]].filter((k, i, a) => a.indexOf(k) === i).slice(0, 2 + (h.k % 2));
    const spots = [[w2 + 0.75, -d2 * 0.25, Math.PI / 2], [-w2 - 0.75, d2 * 0.1, -Math.PI / 2], [w2 * 0.2, -d2 - 0.8, Math.PI], [-w2 * 0.5, -d2 - 0.8, Math.PI]];
    pick.forEach((kind, i) => { const [lx, lz, ry] = spots[(i + h.k) % 4], q = rotL(lx, lz, yaw), px = x + q[0], pz = z + q[1], py = H(px, pz) - 0.03, a = yaw + ry;
      if (L.obst.some(o => Math.hypot(px - o[0], pz - o[1]) < o[2] + 0.5 && Math.hypot(x - o[0], z - o[1]) > Math.max(w2, d2) + 0.5)) return;
      const o2 = (dx, dz) => { const r = rotL(dx, dz, a); return [px + r[0], pz + r[1]]; };
      if (kind === 'wood') { addG(L, v, 'woodpile', 'bark', px, py, pz, a + Math.PI / 2, 1, 1, 1); const [s1, s2] = o2(0.9, 0.3); addG(L, v, 'woodpile', 'bark', s1, H(s1, s2) - 0.03, s2, a + Math.PI / 2 + 0.2, 0.8, 0.7, 1); }
      else if (kind === 'barrels') { for (const [dx, dz, nm, f] of [[-0.4, 0, 'P_barrel', 0.62], [0.35, 0.1, 'P_barrel', 0.6], [0, 0.65, 'P_bucket', 0.34]]) { const [bx, bz] = o2(dx, dz); put(L, v, nm, bx, bz, H(bx, bz) - 0.02, hsh(bx, bz) * 6, 1, { fit: f }); } }
      else if (kind === 'crates') { for (const [dx, dz, dy, nm, f] of [[-0.45, 0, 0, 'P_crate1', 0.8], [0.45, 0.05, 0, 'P_crate2', 0.7], [-0.4, 0.02, 0.62, 'P_crate2', 0.66]]) { const [bx, bz] = o2(dx, dz); put(L, v, nm, bx, bz, py + dy, a + (hsh(bx, 2) - 0.5) * 0.4, 1, { fit: f }); } }
      else if (kind === 'hay') { for (const [dx, dz, dy] of [[-0.6, 0, 0], [0.6, 0, 0], [0, 0.05, 0.5]]) { const [bx, bz] = o2(dx, dz); addG(L, v, 'bale', 'straw', bx, py + dy, bz, a + (hsh(bx, bz) - 0.5) * 0.2, 1.1, 0.5, 0.62); } }
      else if (kind === 'cart') { const [cx, cz] = o2(0, 0.6); put(L, v, 'R_cart', cx, cz, H(cx, cz) - 0.02, a + 0.3, 1, { fit: 3.0 }); L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(cx, cz, 1.5, 3.0, a + 0.3), py + 1.0)); }
      else if (kind === 'stump') { put(L, v, 'P_stump', px, pz, py - 0.03, h.k, 1, { fit: 0.72 }); const [wx, wz] = o2(0.9, 0); addG(L, v, 'woodpile', 'bark', wx, H(wx, wz) - 0.03, wz, a, 0.7, 0.6, 0.9); }
      else { const [bx, bz] = o2(-0.3, 0); put(L, v, 'P_basket', bx, bz, py, h.k, 1, { fit: 0.5 }); const [cx, cz] = o2(0.4, 0.1); put(L, v, 'P_wine', cx, cz, py, a + 1.57, 1, { fit: 0.75 }); }
      ao(L, v, px, pz, kind === 'cart' ? 3.2 : 2.0, kind === 'cart' ? 2.0 : 1.4, a); const ob = [px, pz, kind === 'cart' ? 1.7 : 1.0]; L.obst.push(ob); });
    // ظل تلامس حول قاعدة البيت
    ao(L, v, x, z, D.w * sc + 1.8, D.d * sc + 1.8, yaw); }
  function woodSpot(L, v, x, z, y) { if (OFF) return false; put(L, v, 'P_stump', x, z, y - 0.05, hsh(x, z) * 6, 1, { fit: 0.78 }); const a = hsh(z, x) * 6.28, q = rotL(1.2, 0.3, a);
    addG(L, v, 'woodpile', 'bark', x + q[0], H(x + q[0], z + q[1]) - 0.03, z + q[1], a, 1, 1, 1); const q2 = rotL(-0.9, 0.8, a); addG(L, v, 'woodpile', 'bark', x + q2[0], H(x + q2[0], z + q2[1]) - 0.03, z + q2[1], a + 0.5, 0.7, 0.55, 0.9);
    ao(L, v, x, z, 3.2, 2.4, a); return true; }
  // ── سياج نباتي (شجيرات) على الجهة الداخلية للسور، على مقاطع متقطعة ──
  function hedges(L, v, ga) { if (OFF) return; const R = 60.2; let n = 0;
    for (let a = ga + 0.25; a < ga + 6.283 - 0.25; a += 0.045) { const f = fbm(a * 6.0, 9.3); if (f < 0.5) continue; const x = v.x + Math.cos(a) * (R - hsh(a, 1) * 0.6), z = v.z + Math.sin(a) * (R - hsh(a, 1) * 0.6), y = H(x, z);
      if (y < 0.6 || L.obst.some(o => Math.hypot(x - o[0], z - o[1]) < o[2] + 0.4)) continue;
      put(L, v, hsh(a, 3) > 0.45 ? 'B_bush_myrtle_lite' : 'B_bush_broom_lite', x, z, y - 0.05, hsh(a, 7) * 6.28, 1, { fit: 1.5 + hsh(a, 9) * 1.1 }); n++; }
    // سنديان متفرّق داخل السور يملأ الحزام بين البيوت والسور (بلوط فقط)
    let nt = 0; for (let a = ga + 0.38; a < ga + 6.283 - 0.38; a += 0.31) { const aa = a + (hsh(a, 2.2) - 0.5) * 0.16; if (fbm(aa * 3.1, 4.4) < 0.42) continue; const rr = 50.5 + hsh(aa, 5.5) * 5.5, x = v.x + Math.cos(aa) * rr, z = v.z + Math.sin(aa) * rr, y = H(x, z);
      if (y < 0.6 || L.obst.some(o => Math.hypot(x - o[0], z - o[1]) < o[2] + 2.2)) continue;
      put(L, v, 'OAK', x, z, y - 0.15, hsh(aa, 8) * 6.28, 1, { fit: 7.5 + hsh(aa, 6) * 4 }); ao(L, v, x, z, 2.6, 2.6); L.obst.push([x, z, 1.2]); L.solid.push(topOf([x, z, 0.5], y + 9)); nt++; }
    st.hedges = (st.hedges || 0) + n; st.bandOaks = nt; }

  // ── بيوت خشب بسيطة (تصاميم مصطفى ١ و٣ و٤ — village/simple-houses) ──
  const SHK0 = (window.__VIL4_SH || []).slice(), SHD = {};
  if (!OFF) for (const k of SHK0) { st.need++; const ld = new GLTFLoader(); ld.setMeshoptDecoder(MeshoptDecoder); const url = 'village/v4/house-' + k + (PH ? '-lite' : '') + '.glb';
    const ok = g => { try { const sc = g.scene; sc.updateMatrixWorld(true); const B = new THREE.Box3(); let door = null;
        sc.traverse(o => { if (o.isMesh) { const bb = new THREE.Box3().setFromObject(o); if (/door/i.test(o.name)) door = bb.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3()); B.union(bb); } });
        // بصمة الجدران: رؤوس بين 0.3 و1.7م فوق القاعدة
        const c = B.getCenter(new THREE.Vector3()), y0 = B.min.y, sh = new THREE.Matrix4().makeTranslation(-c.x, -y0, -c.z), out = [], F = [1e9, -1e9, 1e9, -1e9], _v = new THREE.Vector3();
        sc.traverse(o => { if (o.isMesh) { fixMat(o.material); GLB_GEO.add(o.geometry); out.push({ geo: o.geometry, mat: o.material, m: sh.clone().multiply(o.matrixWorld) });
          const P = o.geometry.attributes.position; for (let i = 0; i < P.count; i += 3) { _v.fromBufferAttribute(P, i).applyMatrix4(o.matrixWorld); if (_v.y - y0 < 0.3 || _v.y - y0 > 1.7) continue; F[0] = Math.min(F[0], _v.x - c.x); F[1] = Math.max(F[1], _v.x - c.x); F[2] = Math.min(F[2], _v.z - c.z); F[3] = Math.max(F[3], _v.z - c.z); } } });
        out.size = B.getSize(new THREE.Vector3()); K['H_' + k] = out;
        const w = out.size.x, d = out.size.z, dx = door ? door.x - c.x : 0, area = (F[1] - F[0]) * (F[3] - F[2]);
        SHD[k] = { key: 'sw' + k.slice(1), w, d, h: out.size.y, foot: F, doorSide: '+z', doorDist: (door ? door.z - c.z : d / 2) + 0.15, doorX: -dx, size: area > 45 ? 'medium' : 'small', cap: area > 45 ? 3 : 2 };
        HOUSE_DEFS[SHD[k].key] = SHD[k]; } catch (e) { st.failed.push(url); console.warn('[vil4] بيت ' + k, e); } done(); };
    const bad = () => { st.failed.push(url); console.warn('[vil4] تعذّر ' + url); done(); };
    try { (window.__qLoad || ((l, u, a, p, b) => l.load(u, a, p, b)))(ld, url, ok, undefined, bad); } catch (e) { bad(); } }
  const SHK = () => SHK0.filter(k => SHD[k]).map(k => SHD[k].key), isSH = key => /^sw\d$/.test(key);
  function shHouse(L, v, key, x, z, y, yaw, h) { const k = 'w' + key.slice(2), D = SHD[k], s = h ? h.s : 1, sy = h ? h.sy : 1;
    put(L, v, 'H_' + k, x, z, y, yaw, [s, s * sy, s], { col: nm => /wood|end/i.test(nm) ? h.wood : /roof|shingle|thatch|misc/i.test(nm) ? h.roof : WHITE });
    const F = D.foot, cx = (F[0] + F[1]) / 2 * s, cz = (F[2] + F[3]) / 2 * s, fw = (F[1] - F[0]) * s, fd = (F[3] - F[2]) * s, o = rotL(cx, cz, yaw);
    L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(x + o[0], z + o[1], fw, fd, yaw, 0.02), y + D.h * s * sy));
    addG(L, v, 'box', 'stone', x + o[0], y - 2.0, z + o[1], yaw, fw + 0.25, 2.06, fd + 0.25);   // أساس حجري يخفي ميلان الأرض
    (L._foot || (L._foot = [])).push({ x, z, c: Math.cos(yaw), s: Math.sin(yaw), hw: D.w * s / 2 + 1, hd: D.d * s / 2 + 1 }); }
  const WHITE = new THREE.Color(1, 1, 1);
  // ── الإنهاء: النسخ المصنوعة فوراً، ونماذج GLB عند تحميلها ──
  function finish(L, v) { if (OFF || !L.v4) return; const t0 = performance.now();
    if (L._wat) { const KW = V3B && V3B.T && (V3B.T.fenceL || V3B.T.fence); const wp = KW && KW.FenceWattle; if (wp) { if (!K.W_wattle) { K.W_wattle = wp; }
        for (const g of L._wat) for (const [lx, lz, ry, len] of [[-g.W / 2 - 0.2, 0, Math.PI / 2, g.D + 0.4], [g.W / 2 + 0.2, 0, -Math.PI / 2, g.D + 0.4], [0, -g.D / 2 - 0.2, 0, g.W + 0.4]]) {
          const q = rotL(lx, lz, g.yaw), px = g.x + q[0], pz = g.z + q[1], a = g.yaw + ry; const e = rotL(-len / 2, 0, a);
          (V(L).inst.W_wattle || (V(L).inst.W_wattle = [])).push({ m: mat4(px + e[0] - v.x, H(px, pz) - 0.05, pz + e[1] - v.z, a, len / 2.8, 0.75, 1), sh: true }); } } }
    makeG(L); const go = () => { if (v.live !== L) return;
      if (!K.H_gate && L._gfb) { const S = V(L), keep = S.g; S.g = {}; for (const a of L._gfb) addG(L, v, ...a); makeG(L); S.g = keep; st.gateFallback = 1; }   // v45g: احتياط
      makeInst(L); st.built.push({ v: v.name, draws: L.v4.draws, tris: L.v4.tris, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) });
      console.info('[vil4] ' + v.name + ': ' + L.v4.draws + ' draw, ' + L.v4.tris + ' tris'); try { if (window.V19 && V19.NS && V19.NS.nat && V19.NS.nat.refreshGrass) V19.NS.nat.refreshGrass(); } catch (e) {} };
    if (ready()) go(); else waitQ.push(go); }
  function stat() { let d = 0, t = 0; for (const v of VILLAGES) if (v.live && v.live.v4) { d += v.live.v4.draws || 0; t += v.live.v4.tris || 0; } return { draws: d, tris: t, ...st, kits: Object.keys(K).length }; }
  const api = { SHK, isSH, shHouse, SHD, st, K, OFF, PH, LANE, KSLOT, KR, SQR, slotPos, hv, square, palisade, garden, houseProps, hedges, woodSpot, lanes, finish, stat, ready, active: () => !OFF };
  window.VIL4 = api; return api;
})();
function houseBox(geo, x, z, yaw, s, pad) { const F = wallFoot(geo), o = rotL(F[2] * s, F[3] * s, yaw); return solidBox(x + o[0], z + o[1], F[0] * s, F[1] * s, yaw, pad); }
function houseTpl(key) {                          // هندسة مشتركة لكل نوع بيت (مقياس موحّد، القاعدة y=0) + أساس حجري يخفي ميلان الأرض
  if (HCACHE[key]) return HCACHE[key];
  const D = HOUSE_DEFS[key], M = MODELS[key]; let geo, bd = D.d;
  if (M && M.geo) { const k = D.w / (M.bb.max.x - M.bb.min.x); geo = M.geo.clone().scale(k, k, k); bd = (M.bb.max.z - M.bb.min.z) * k; }
  else geo = mergeGeo([[new THREE.BoxGeometry(D.w, 3.4, D.d).translate(0, 1.7, 0), [0.5, 0.4, 0.28]], [gableGeo(D.w + 0.8, D.d + 0.8, 2.4).translate(0, 3.4, 0), [0.32, 0.1, 0.06]]]);   // بديل: صندوق بسقف
  geo.computeBoundingSphere();
  const fgeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(D.w + 0.4, 3, bd + 0.4).translate(0, -1.48, 0); SHARED.add(geo); SHARED.add(fgeo);
  return HCACHE[key] = { geo, fgeo, bd };
}
const BENCHG = (() => { const P = [], b = (w, h, d, x, y, z, c) => P.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d).translate(x, y, z), colA(c)]);
  b(2.0, 0.12, 0.55, 0, 0.5, 0, 0x7a5530); b(2.0, 0.5, 0.1, 0, 0.85, -0.25, 0x6a4a2c); for (const sx of [-0.8, 0.8]) b(0.12, 0.5, 0.5, sx, 0.25, 0, 0x4a3524);
  const g = mergeGeo(P); SHARED.add(g); return g; })();
const DBGM = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: 0xff2020, depthTest: false, transparent: true });
function stallGeo(type) {
  if (SCACHE[type]) return SCACHE[type];
  const sh = SHOPS[type], P = [], box = (w, h, d, x, y, z, c, rx) => P.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d).rotateX(rx || 0).translate(x, y, z), colA(c)]);
  box(3.4, 1, 1.1, 0, 0.5, 0.25, 0x6a4a2c); box(3.7, 0.1, 1.4, 0, 1.05, 0.25, 0x8a6a40);
  for (const sx of [-1.7, 1.7]) for (const sz of [-0.9, 1.0]) box(0.12, 2.8, 0.12, sx, 1.4, sz, 0x4a3524);
  box(3.2, 1.8, 0.25, 0, 0.9, -0.95, 0x5a3a22);
  for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) box(0.5, 0.08, 2.8, -1.75 + i * 0.5, 2.95, 0.05, i % 2 ? 0xf2ead8 : sh.c1, 0.14);
  if (type === 'general') { [[-1, 0xcc3333], [-0.4, 0x3a8acc], [0.2, 0x4aa84a], [0.8, 0xcc3333]].forEach(([x, c]) => P.push([new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.1, 0.12, 0.3, 8).translate(x, 1.25, 0.2), colA(c)])); box(0.6, 0.35, 0.4, 1.3, 1.25, 0.2, 0x9a7a4a); }
  else if (type === 'bakery') { for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) box(0.4, 0.18, 0.22, -1.2 + i * 0.6, 1.2, 0.1 + (i % 2) * 0.3, 0xc8924a); }
  else { box(0.7, 0.35, 0.4, -0.9, 1.25, 0.2, 0x303030); box(0.35, 0.2, 0.3, -0.9, 1.52, 0.2, 0x404040); for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) box(0.06, 0.9, 0.02, 0.2 + i * 0.3, 1.55, 0.2, 0xc9ced6); }
  const g = mergeGeo(P); SHARED.add(g); return SCACHE[type] = g;
}
const hmat = (c, o = {}) => new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: c, roughness: 0.95, ...o });
const HM1 = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.9, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
const ROADM = hmat(0x8a7656, { polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: -2, polygonOffsetUnits: -2, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
const PLAZAM = hmat(0x8c8982, { polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: -3, polygonOffsetUnits: -3, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
const FIELDM = hmat(0x5a4a2a), STONEM = hmat(0x6f6a60), POSTM = hmat(0x6a4a2c);
const HG = { field: new THREE.BoxGeometry(6, 0.25, 6).translate(0, 0.1, 0), well: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.3, 1.4, 1.1, 14).translate(0, 0.55, 0), stump: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 8).translate(0, 0.35, 0), post: new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.13, 0.17, 2.6, 6).translate(0, 1.3, 0) };
Object.values(HG).forEach(x => SHARED.add(x));
function signTex(txt) {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = 512; c.height = 128; const x = c.getContext('2d');
  x.fillStyle = '#5a3a22'; x.fillRect(0, 0, 512, 128); x.strokeStyle = '#c9a46a'; x.lineWidth = 8; x.strokeRect(6, 6, 500, 116);
  x.fillStyle = '#f2e6c8'; x.font = 'bold 54px Tahoma, sans-serif'; x.textAlign = 'center'; x.textBaseline = 'middle'; x.direction = 'rtl'; x.fillText(txt, 256, 68);
  const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; return t;
}
function buildVillage(v) {
  const r = rng(v.seed), g = new THREE.Group(), L = { g, houses: [], obst: [[v.x, v.z, 1.9]], solid: [[v.x, v.z, 1.45]], npcs: [], work: [], wood: [], stalls: [], gatePos: [], alarm: 0, ga: 0, animals: [], sus: null,
    poi: { plaza: [], well: [], bench: [], cross: [], stall: [], field: [], wood: [] } };
  v.live = L; scene.add(g);
  const ga0 = r() * 6.283; const ga = (() => { for (const pl of WORLD.roads) { if (Math.abs(pl[0] - v.x) > 0.6 || Math.abs(pl[1] - v.z) > 0.6) continue; for (let i = 2; i + 1 < pl.length; i += 2) { const dx = pl[i] - v.x, dz = pl[i + 1] - v.z; if (Math.hypot(dx, dz) >= 45) return Math.atan2(dz, dx); } } return ga0; })(); L.ga = ga; const pol = (rad, a) => [v.x + Math.cos(a) * rad, v.z + Math.sin(a) * rad];
  // ── البئر والساحة والطرق (شرائط تتبع التضاريس) ──
  const well = new THREE.Mesh(HG.well, STONEM); well.position.set(0, LIB.height(v.x, v.z), 0); if (!VIL4.active()) g.add(well); topOf(L.solid[0], well.position.y + geoTop(HG.well));   // v10: سطح البئر
  const RP = [], RI = [], RQ = []; let rv = 0;
  const ribbon = (pts, w) => { (L._lanes || (L._lanes = [])).push([pts, w]);
    const sm = []; for (let i = 0; i < pts.length - 1; i++) { const a = pts[i], b = pts[i + 1], n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.hypot(b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1]) / 2.5));
      for (let k = i ? 1 : 0; k <= n; k++) sm.push([a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * k / n, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * k / n]); }
    const b0 = rv; let al = 0; for (let i = 0; i < sm.length; i++) { if (i) al += Math.hypot(sm[i][0] - sm[i - 1][0], sm[i][1] - sm[i - 1][1]); const a = sm[Math.max(0, i - 1)], b = sm[Math.min(sm.length - 1, i + 1)], dx = b[0] - a[0], dz = b[1] - a[1], l = Math.hypot(dx, dz) || 1;
      for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const x = sm[i][0] - dz / l * w / 2 * s, z = sm[i][1] + dx / l * w / 2 * s; RP.push(x - v.x, LIB.height(x, z) + 0.12, z - v.z); RQ.push(s * w / 2, al, w / 2); rv++; } }
    for (let i = 0; i < sm.length - 1; i++) { const a = b0 + i * 2; RI.push(a, a + 1, a + 2, a + 1, a + 3, a + 2); } };
  const circle = (rad, n) => Array.from({ length: n + 1 }, (_, i) => pol(rad, i / n * 6.283));
  const RING = VIL4.active() ? 15 : 18; ribbon([pol(62, ga), pol(VIL4.active() ? 13 : 9, ga)], 4.4); ribbon(circle(RING, 48), 2.6); ribbon(circle(34, 64), 3);
  const fits = (x, z, rad) => Math.hypot(x - v.x, z - v.z) + rad < 59.5 && !L.obst.some(o => Math.hypot(x - o[0], z - o[1]) < rad + o[2] + 1.0);
  // ── البيوت: حلقتان حول الساحة مع ممر للبوابة؛ يواجه كل باب الساحة، والبصمة الحقيقية من HOUSE_DEFS ──
  const V3H = V3B.has('v3b1') && V3B.has('v3b3'); let HK = V3H ? ['v3b1', 'v3b3', 'v3b1'] : ['houseA', 'houseE', 'houseF'], HW = V3H ? [0.5, 0.5, 0] : [0.36, 0.32, 0.32];
  if (VIL4.active() && VIL4.SHK().length) { const sk = VIL4.SHK(); if (sk.length >= 3) { HK = sk.slice(0, 3); HW = [0.36, 0.3, 0.34]; } else if (sk.length === 2 || !V3H) { HK = [sk[0], sk[1] || sk[0], V3H ? 'v3b1' : sk[0]]; HW = [0.4, 0.4, 0.2]; } else { HK = [sk[0], 'v3b1', 'v3b3']; HW = [0.5, 0.25, 0.25]; } }   // v45v: بيوت خشب بسيطة (تصاميم ١ و٣ و٤)   // v41b: بيوت v3b بدل القديمة      // القرى: بيوت خشبية بالكامل فقط
  const pickKey = () => { let q = r(), a = 0; for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { a += HW[i]; if (q < a) return HK[i]; } return 'houseA'; };
  const place = (key, rad, ang) => {
    const D = HOUSE_DEFS[key], x = v.x + Math.cos(ang) * rad, z = v.z + Math.sin(ang) * rad, fr = Math.hypot(D.w, D.d) / 2 * 0.88 * (VIL4.active() ? VIL4.hv(x, z).s : 1);
    if (!fits(x, z, fr)) return false;
    let mn = 1e9, mxh = -1e9; for (const [ox, oz] of [[0, 0], [D.w / 2, D.d / 2], [-D.w / 2, D.d / 2], [D.w / 2, -D.d / 2], [-D.w / 2, -D.d / 2]]) { const q = LIB.height(x + ox, z + oz); mn = Math.min(mn, q); mxh = Math.max(mxh, q); }
    if (mn < 2 || mxh - mn > 3.2) return false;
    const dl = DIRS[D.doorSide] || DIRS['+z'], yaw = Math.atan2(v.x - x, v.z - z) + (r() - 0.5) * (VIL4.active() ? 0.55 : 0.12) - Math.atan2(dl[0], dl[1]);   // الباب نحو الساحة
    const SH = VIL4.isSH(key), V3 = key === 'v3b1' || key === 'v3b3' || SH, T = V3 ? null : houseTpl(key);
    if (V3) (SH ? VIL4.shHouse : V3B.house)(L, v, key, x, z, mxh - Math.min(0.3, (mxh - mn) * 0.5) - 0.02, yaw, VIL4.active() ? VIL4.hv(x, z) : null);   // v41b: نسخة مكرّرة (InstancedMesh) + صناديق تصادم
    else { const h = new THREE.Mesh(T.geo, HM1); h.position.set(x - v.x, mxh - 0.05, z - v.z); h.rotation.y = yaw; h.castShadow = true; h.receiveShadow = true;
    const f = new THREE.Mesh(T.fgeo, STONEM); f.receiveShadow = true; h.add(f); g.add(h); }
    const dw = rotL(dl[0], dl[1], yaw), pw = rotL(-dl[1], dl[0], yaw), dd = D.doorDist, dx0 = D.doorX || 0;
    const door = [x + dw[0] * (dd + 1.3) + pw[0] * dx0, z + dw[1] * (dd + 1.3) + pw[1] * dx0];
    const al = D.w >= D.d ? [1, 0] : [0, 1], rr = Math.min(D.w, D.d) / 2, len = Math.max(D.w, D.d), nC = Math.max(1, Math.round(len / (rr * 1.3))), cir = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < nC; i++) { const t = nC === 1 ? 0 : -(len / 2 - rr) + (len - 2 * rr) * i / (nC - 1), q = rotL(al[0] * t, al[1] * t, yaw), c = [x + q[0], z + q[1], rr]; c.chain = 1; L.obst.push(c); cir.push(c); }   // دوائر للتخطيط فقط (تُحذف من الملاحة)
    if (!V3) L.solid.push(topOf(houseBox(T.geo, x, z, yaw, 1, 0.02), mxh - 0.05 + geoTop(T.geo)));   // التصادم: صندوق بالبصمة الحقيقية للنموذج
    L.houses.push({ x, z, door, def: D, key, cap: D.cap, yaw, T: { W: D.w, D: D.d } });
    const rd = Math.hypot(x - v.x, z - v.z); ribbon([door, pol(rd < 35 ? 18 : 34, Math.atan2(door[1] - v.z, door[0] - v.x))], 1.8);
    if (DBG) {                                    // #debug: سهم أحمر عند الباب ودائرة العائق
      const ar = new THREE.ArrowHelper(new THREE.Vector3(dw[0], 0, dw[1]), new THREE.Vector3(x + dw[0] * dd + pw[0] * dx0 - v.x, LIB.height(x, z) + 1.5, z + dw[1] * dd + pw[1] * dx0 - v.z), 3.4, 0xff0000, 1.1, 0.8);
      ar.line.material.depthTest = false; ar.cone.material.depthTest = false; ar.renderOrder = 999; g.add(ar);
      for (const c of cir) { const pts = []; for (let i = 0; i < 28; i++) { const a = i / 28 * 6.283, px = c[0] + Math.cos(a) * c[2], pz = c[1] + Math.sin(a) * c[2]; pts.push(new THREE.Vector3(px - v.x, LIB.height(px, pz) + 0.5, pz - v.z)); }
        const lp = new THREE.LineLoop(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints(pts), DBGM); lp.renderOrder = 998; g.add(lp); } }
    if (V3 && VIL4.active()) try { VIL4.houseProps(L, v, key, x, z, 0, yaw, VIL4.hv(x, z).s); } catch (e) { console.warn('[vil4] props', e); }
    return true; };
  const n1 = VIL4.active() ? 10 : 8, a1 = [], n2 = VIL4.active() ? 15 : 10;   // v45v: قرية أكثف
  for (let k = 0; k < n1; k++) { const ang = ga + 0.5 + (6.283 - 1.0) * (k + 0.5) / n1; a1.push(ang); place(pickKey(), (VIL4.active() ? 23.5 : 27) + r() * 1.5, ang + (r() - 0.5) * 0.05); }
  for (let k = 0; k < n2; k++) place(pickKey(), (VIL4.active() ? 40.5 + r() * 3.5 : 44 + r() * 2), ga + 0.35 + (6.283 - 0.7) * (k + 0.5) / n2 + (r() - 0.5) * 0.04);
  for (let t = 0; t < 120 && L.houses.length < 12; t++) place(pickKey(), 25 + r() * 24, ga + 0.45 + r() * (6.283 - 0.9));   // ضمان ١٢ بيتاً على الأقل
  for (const i of [1, n1 >> 1, n1 - 2]) { const m = (a1[i] + a1[i + 1]) / 2; ribbon([pol(RING, m), pol(34, m)], 2.4); L.poi.cross.push({ x: pol(RING, m)[0], z: pol(RING, m)[1], type: 'cross' }, { x: pol(34, m)[0], z: pol(34, m)[1], type: 'cross' }); }
  { const rg = new THREE.BufferGeometry(), nm = new Float32Array(RP.length); for (let i = 1; i < nm.length; i += 3) nm[i] = 1;
    rg.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(RP, 3)); rg.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(nm, 3)); rg.setIndex(RI);
    rg.setAttribute('rq', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(RQ, 3)); const m = new THREE.Mesh(rg, VIL4.active() ? VIL4.LANE : ROAD_VIL); m.receiveShadow = true; m.renderOrder = 1; g.add(m); }
  if (!VIL4.active()) { const P = [0, LIB.height(v.x, v.z) + 0.14, 0], I = [];
    for (const rr of [4.5, 9]) for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) { const a = i / 24 * 6.283, x = v.x + Math.cos(a) * rr, z = v.z + Math.sin(a) * rr; P.push(x - v.x, LIB.height(x, z) + 0.14, z - v.z); }
    for (let i = 0; i < 24; i++) { const j = (i + 1) % 24; I.push(0, 1 + i, 1 + j, 1 + i, 25 + i, 1 + j, 1 + j, 25 + i, 25 + j); }
    const pg = new THREE.BufferGeometry(), nm = new Float32Array(P.length); for (let i = 1; i < nm.length; i += 3) nm[i] = 1;
    pg.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(P, 3)); pg.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(nm, 3)); pg.setIndex(I);
    const m = new THREE.Mesh(pg, PLAZAM); m.receiveShadow = true; g.add(m); }
  // ── السور الخشبي (نموذج GLB مكرّر بـInstancedMesh) والبوابة ──
  if (!(VIL4.active() ? VIL4.palisade(L, v, ga) : V3B.fence(L, v, ga))) { const WT = MODELS.wall && MODELS.wall.geo ? MODELS.wall : null, bx = WT ? WT.bb.max.x - WT.bb.min.x : 1.35, SY = 4.3;
    const wgeo = WT ? WT.geo : mergeGeo([[new THREE.BoxGeometry(1.35, 0.53, 0.06).translate(0, 0.265, 0), [0.14, 0.08, 0.03]]]), NW = Math.round(6.283 * 62 / 4.6), pts = [], keep = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= NW; i++) { const a = ga + i / NW * 6.283, [x, z] = pol(62, a); pts.push([x, z, LIB.height(x, z), a]); }
    for (let i = 0; i < NW; i++) { const p = pts[i], q = pts[i + 1], am = (p[3] + q[3]) / 2; if (Math.abs(Math.atan2(sn(am - ga), cs(am - ga))) < 0.1 || Math.min(p[2], q[2]) < 0.4) continue; keep.push([p, q]); }
    const wm = new THREE.InstancedMesh(wgeo, HM1, keep.length);
    const wth = Math.max(0.3, WT ? (WT.bb.max.z - WT.bb.min.z) * SY : 0.26);
    keep.forEach(([p, q], i) => { const len = Math.hypot(q[0] - p[0], q[1] - p[1]), ang = Math.atan2(q[1] - p[1], q[0] - p[0]);
      L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox((p[0] + q[0]) / 2, (p[1] + q[1]) / 2, len * 1.04, wth, -ang), Math.min(p[2], q[2]) - 0.35 + (WT ? WT.bb.max.y : 0.53) * SY));   // جزء السور: صندوق مطابق للقطعة
      wm.setMatrixAt(i, _M.compose(_P.set((p[0] + q[0]) / 2 - v.x, Math.min(p[2], q[2]) - 0.35, (p[1] + q[1]) / 2 - v.z), _Q.setFromAxisAngle(_UP, -ang), _S.set(len / bx * 1.02, SY, SY))); });
    wm.castShadow = true; wm.receiveShadow = true; wm.computeBoundingSphere(); g.add(wm); }
  const gp = pol(62, ga), gate = new THREE.Group(); gate.position.set(gp[0] - v.x, LIB.height(gp[0], gp[1]), gp[1] - v.z); gate.rotation.y = Math.atan2(Math.cos(ga), Math.sin(ga)); g.add(gate);
  { const P = [], box = (w, h, d, x, y, z, c) => P.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d).translate(x, y, z), colA(c)]);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) { box(0.8, 4.6, 0.8, s * 4.8, 2.3, 0, 0x5a3a22); box(1.0, 0.3, 1.0, s * 4.8, 4.7, 0, 0x4a3524);
      P.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(3.6, 3.4, 0.2).translate(-s * 1.8, 1.7, 0).rotateY(s * 1.15).translate(s * 4.4, 0, 0), colA(0x6a4428)]); }
    box(10.6, 0.6, 0.7, 0, 4.3, 0, 0x5a3a22); box(11, 0.15, 1.4, 0, 4.75, 0, 0x4a3524);
    const gm = new THREE.Mesh(mergeGeo(P), HM1); gm.castShadow = true; if (!VIL4.active()) gate.add(gm);
    const sm = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: signTex(v.name), side: THREE.FrontSide }), s1 = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(4.4, 1.1), sm), s2 = s1.clone();
    s1.position.set(0, 3.45, 0.42); s2.position.set(0, 3.45, -0.42); s2.rotation.y = PI; gate.add(s1, s2); }
  L.gatePos = [pol(57, ga - 0.05), pol(57, ga + 0.05)];
  for (const s of (VIL4.active() ? [] : [-1, 1])) { const q = pol(62, ga + s * 4.8 / 62); L.solid.push(topOf([q[0], q[1], 0.6], gate.position.y + 4.85));    // أعمدة البوابة
    const gy = gate.rotation.y, lc = rotL(-s * 1.8, 0, s * 1.15), wc = rotL(lc[0] + s * 4.4, lc[1], gy); L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(gp[0] + wc[0], gp[1] + wc[1], 3.6, 0.3, gy + s * 1.15), gate.position.y + 3.4)); }   // ضلفتا البوابة
  // ── المحلات ──
  const nSt = S.pop > 0.5 ? 3 : 2, types = ['general', 'bakery', 'smith'], sa = VIL4.active() ? [ga + 1.05, ga + 2.15, ga - 1.05] : [ga + 2.0, ga + 3.14, ga + 4.3], KV4 = VIL4.active() && typeof KIOSK !== 'undefined' && /تل الأمل/.test(v.name || '');
  for (let i = 0; i < nSt; i++) { const [sx, sz] = pol(VIL4.active() ? VIL4.KR : 13, sa[i]), yaw = Math.atan2(v.x - sx, v.z - sz), m = new THREE.Mesh(stallGeo(types[i]), HM1);
    m.position.set(sx - v.x, LIB.height(sx, sz), sz - v.z); m.rotation.y = yaw; m.castShadow = true; if (!KV4) g.add(m);
    const dx = Math.sin(yaw), dz = Math.cos(yaw); L.stalls.push({ type: types[i], x: sx, z: sz, face: yaw, keeper: [sx - dx * (KV4 ? 0.9 : 1.4), sz - dz * (KV4 ? 0.9 : 1.4)], front: [sx + dx * 2.3, sz + dz * 2.3], lat: [dz, -dx] });
    { const ob = [sx, sz, 1.9]; if (KV4) ob.kiosk = 1; L.obst.push(ob); } if (!KV4) L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(sx, sz, 3.7, 1.95, yaw), m.position.y + geoTop(m.geometry))); L.poi.stall.push({ x: sx + dx * 2.6, z: sz + dz * 2.6, type: 'stall' }); }
  if (VIL4.active()) try { VIL4.square(L, v, ga); } catch (e) { console.warn('[vil4] square', e); }
  // ── نقاط الاهتمام: الساحة، البئر، الدكك ──
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { const p = pol(6.8, i * 1.047 + 0.3); L.poi.plaza.push({ x: p[0], z: p[1], type: 'plaza' }); }
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const p = pol(3.4, i * 1.571); L.poi.well.push({ x: p[0], z: p[1], type: 'well' }); }
  for (const da of (VIL4.active() ? [] : [0.9, 2.55, 3.7, 5.0])) { const [bx, bz] = pol(16.5, ga + da), yaw = Math.atan2(v.x - bx, v.z - bz), m = new THREE.Mesh(BENCHG, HM1);
    m.position.set(bx - v.x, LIB.height(bx, bz), bz - v.z); m.rotation.y = yaw + PI; g.add(m); L.obst.push([bx, bz, 1.1]); L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(bx, bz, 2.0, 0.6, yaw), m.position.y + geoTop(BENCHG)));
    L.poi.bench.push({ x: bx + Math.sin(yaw) * 1.4, z: bz + Math.cos(yaw) * 1.4, type: 'bench' }); }
  // ── الحقول وأماكن قطع الأخشاب (داخل السور) ──
  for (let i = 0, t = 0; i < 3 && t < 60; t++) { const [x, z] = pol(53 + r() * 3, ga + 0.5 + r() * 5.2), y = LIB.height(x, z); if (y < 2 || !fits(x, z, 3.6)) continue;
    if (!(VIL4.active() && VIL4.garden(L, v, x, z, y))) { const f = new THREE.Mesh(HG.field, FIELDM); f.position.set(x - v.x, y, z - v.z); g.add(f); } L.obst.push([x, z, 3.2]); L.work.push([x, z]); L.poi.field.push({ x: x + 0.6, z: z + 0.6, type: 'field' }); i++; }
  for (let i = 0, t = 0; i < 2 && t < 60; t++) { const [x, z] = pol(52 + r() * 4, ga + 0.5 + r() * 5.2), y = LIB.height(x, z); if (y < 2 || !fits(x, z, 1.6)) continue;
    const f = new THREE.Mesh(HG.stump, POSTM); f.position.set(x - v.x, y, z - v.z); if (!(VIL4.active() && VIL4.woodSpot(L, v, x, z, y))) g.add(f); L.obst.push([x, z, 0.8]); L.solid.push(topOf([x, z, 0.6], y + geoTop(HG.stump))); L.wood.push([x + 1.2, z]); L.poi.wood.push({ x: x + 1.2, z, type: 'wood' }); i++; }
  if (MODELS.vMill && MODELS.vMill.geo) for (let t = 0; t < 60; t++) {                      // طاحونة خشبية لكل قرية
    const [x, z] = pol(49 + r() * 6, ga + 0.5 + r() * 5.2); if (LIB.height(x, z) < 1.8 || !fits(x, z, 5.5)) continue;
    const M = MODELS.vMill, k = 8.8 / (M.bb.max.x - M.bb.min.x), gm = HCACHE.vMill || (HCACHE.vMill = M.geo.clone().scale(k, k, k)); SHARED.add(gm); const yaw = r() * 6.283;
    const m = new THREE.Mesh(gm, HM1); m.position.set(x - v.x, LIB.height(x, z) - 0.05, z - v.z); m.rotation.y = yaw; m.castShadow = true; g.add(m);
    for (const s of [-2.3, 2.3]) { const q = rotL(s, 0, yaw), c = [x + q[0], z + q[1], 2.4]; c.chain = 1; L.obst.push(c); }
    L.solid.push(topOf(solidBox(x, z, 8.8, Math.max(2, (M.bb.max.z - M.bb.min.z) * k), yaw), m.position.y + geoTop(gm))); break; }
  if (!L.work.length) { L.work.push(pol(30, ga + 1)); L.poi.field.push({ x: L.work[0][0], z: L.work[0][1], type: 'field' }); }
  if (!L.wood.length) { L.wood.push(...L.work); L.poi.wood.push(...L.poi.field); }
  if (!L.houses.length) { const p = pol(25, ga + 1); L.houses.push({ x: p[0], z: p[1], door: pol(19, ga + 1), def: HOUSE_DEFS.houseB, key: 'houseB', cap: 3, yaw: 0, T: { W: 7, D: 7 } }); }
  if (VIL4.active()) try { VIL4.hedges(L, v, ga); VIL4.lanes(L, v); } catch (e) { console.warn('[vil4] edge', e); }
  finishNav(L);
  L.roadF = (x, z) => { const rx = x - v.x, rz = z - v.z, rd = Math.hypot(rx, rz); if (rd < 9.5 || Math.abs(rd - (VIL4.active() ? 15 : 18)) < 1.6 || Math.abs(rd - 34) < 1.8) return 1;   // تفضيل الطرق والممرات
    const da = Math.atan2(Math.sin(Math.atan2(rz, rx) - ga), Math.cos(Math.atan2(rz, rx) - ga)); return Math.abs(da) * rd < 2.4 && rd < 63 ? 1 : 1.4; };
  // ── السكان: متناسبون مع عدد البيوت وأحجامها (صغير ٢، متوسط ٣، كبير ٤)، بلا بيت فارغ ولا بيت فوق ٤ ──
  const hs = L.houses, total = hs.reduce((a, h) => a + h.cap, 0), nv = Math.min(total, Math.max(6, Math.round(total * S.pop * 0.45))), occ = hs.map(() => 0), asg = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < hs.length && asg.length < nv; i++) { asg.push(i); occ[i]++; }
  while (asg.length < nv) { let b = -1, bs = 0; for (let i = 0; i < hs.length; i++) { const sp = hs[i].cap - occ[i]; if (sp > bs) { bs = sp; b = i; } } if (b < 0) break; occ[b]++; asg.push(b); }
  asg.forEach(i => makeNPC(v, L, r, { house: hs[i] }));
  for (let i = 0; i < nSt; i++) makeNPC(v, L, r, { house: hs[(i * 3) % hs.length], shop: L.stalls[i] });
  const ng = Math.min(4, Math.max(2, Math.round(hs.length / 6))); for (let i = 0; i < ng; i++) makeNPC(v, L, r, { house: hs[0], guard: true, slot: i, gate: i < 2 });
  L.stat = { houses: hs.length, pop: total, villagers: nv, guards: ng, traders: nSt };
  // ── حظيرة المواشي: سياج دائري + حمار وخيول (داخل السور وبعيدة عن البيوت) ──
  if (MODELS.ready && MODELS.donkey) {   // v14: بلا خيول في الحظائر (الحمار فقط)
    let px = 0, pz = 0, ok = false; const RAD = 6;   // v15: أُعيد التصريح إلى سطره (كان داخل التعليق فتعطّل بناء القرى)
    for (let t = 0; t < 160 && !ok; t++) { const [x, z] = pol(48 + r() * 5, ga + 0.6 + r() * 5.1); if (LIB.height(x, z) > 2.5 && fits(x, z, RAD + 0.5)) { px = x; pz = z; ok = true; } }
    if (ok && V3B.corral(L, v, px, pz, RAD)) { L.obst.push([px, pz, RAD * 0.85]);   // v41b: سياج الحظيرة من قطع سور v3b
      makeAnimal(r() < 0.5 ? 'horse' : 'horseW', px + 2.2, pz + 1.0, L, true);
      if (S.pop > 0.5) makeAnimal(r() < 0.5 ? 'horseW' : 'horse', px - 2.0, pz - 2.0, L, true); }
    else if (ok) { const posts2 = [], rails2 = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) { if (i % 7 === 0) continue;             // فتحتان للدخول
        const a = (i + 0.5) / 14 * 6.283, x = px + Math.cos(a) * RAD, z = pz + Math.sin(a) * RAD, y = LIB.height(x, z); posts2.push([x, y, z]); }
      const pm2 = new THREE.InstancedMesh(HG.post, POSTM, posts2.length);
      posts2.forEach((p, i) => { pm2.setMatrixAt(i, _M.compose(_P.set(p[0] - v.x, p[1] - 0.3, p[2] - v.z), _Q.identity(), _S.set(1, 1, 1)));
        const q = posts2[(i + 1) % posts2.length]; if (Math.hypot(q[0] - p[0], q[2] - p[2]) < 8)
          for (const hh of [0.8, 1.6]) { const len = Math.hypot(q[0] - p[0], q[2] - p[2]), ang = Math.atan2(q[2] - p[2], q[0] - p[0]);
            rails2.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(len, 0.09, 0.11).rotateY(-ang).translate((p[0] + q[0]) / 2 - v.x, (p[1] + q[1]) / 2 + hh - 0.3, (p[2] + q[2]) / 2 - v.z), colA(0x7a5530)]); } });
      g.add(pm2); if (rails2.length) g.add(new THREE.Mesh(mergeGeo(rails2), HM1));
      L.obst.push([px, pz, RAD * 0.85]);
      /* v14b: بلا حمار في الحظيرة (السياج باقٍ) */
      makeAnimal(r() < 0.5 ? 'horse' : 'horseW', px + 2.2, pz + 1.0, L, true);
      if (S.pop > 0.5) makeAnimal(r() < 0.5 ? 'horseW' : 'horse', px - 2.0, pz - 2.0, L, true); } }
  try { KIOSK.build(L, v); } catch (e) { console.warn('[kiosk]', e); } if (VWORK.on) try { VWORK.build(L, v); } catch (e) { console.warn('[vwork]', e); }   // v43k: أكشاك القرية
  if (VIL4.active()) try { VIL4.finish(L, v); } catch (e) { console.warn('[vil4] finish', e); }
  try { V3B.finish(L); if (window.V19 && V19.NS && V19.NS.nat && V19.NS.nat.refreshGrass) V19.NS.nat.refreshGrass(); } catch (e) { console.warn('[v3b] finish', e); }
  console.info(`[قرية] ${v.name}: ${L.stat.houses} بيتاً، سعة ${L.stat.pop}، قرويون ${L.stat.villagers}، تجار ${L.stat.traders}، حرس ${L.stat.guards}`);
}
function disposeVillage(v) {
  const L = v.live; for (const n of L.npcs) { const i = NPCS.indexOf(n); if (i >= 0) NPCS.splice(i, 1); if (DLG.n === n) closeDlg(); }
  for (const a of L.animals) { const i = ANIMALS.indexOf(a); if (i >= 0) ANIMALS.splice(i, 1); scene.remove(a.g); }
  L.g.traverse(o => { if (o.isSkinnedMesh && o.skeleton) o.skeleton.dispose(); if (o.geometry && !SHARED.has(o.geometry) && !GLB_GEO.has(o.geometry)) o.geometry.dispose(); if (o.isInstancedMesh) o.dispose(); if (o.material && o.material.map && o.material.isMeshBasicMaterial) o.material.map.dispose(); });
  scene.remove(L.g); v.live = null;
}

function cityTpl(key) {                                   // هندسة مشتركة مقيسة (أو بديل: صندوق بسقف بلون الحجر)
  if (CCACHE[key]) return CCACHE[key];
  const HR = HREP_MAP[key] ? houseRep(key) : null; if (HR) return CCACHE[key] = HR.geo;          // v13: البيوت الجديدة
  const D = cDef(key), M = MODELS[key]; let geo;
  if (M && M.geo) { const k = D.w / (M.bb.max.x - M.bb.min.x); geo = M.geo.clone().scale(k, k, k); if (D.foot) geo.userData.foot = D.foot.map(q => q * k); }
  else geo = mergeGeo([[new THREE.BoxGeometry(D.w, D.h * 0.62, D.d).translate(0, D.h * 0.31, 0), colA(0x8a857a)], [gableGeo(D.w + 0.6, D.d + 0.6, D.h * 0.38).translate(0, D.h * 0.62, 0), colA(0x7a3a2a)]]);
  geo.computeBoundingSphere(); SHARED.add(geo); return CCACHE[key] = geo;
}
function cityRaw(key) {                                   // نموذج غير مقيس (يُقيَّس بمصفوفة النسخة): سور/برج/بوابة
  const M = MODELS[key]; if (M && M.geo) return { geo: M.geo, bx: M.bb.max.x - M.bb.min.x, by: M.bb.max.y };
  const D = cDef(key), g = mergeGeo([[new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 0.3).translate(0, 0.5, 0), colA(0x8a857a)]]); SHARED.add(g); return { geo: g, bx: 1, by: 1 };
}

  [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.7, 0.9, 3.4, 10).translate(0, 1.7, 0), colA(0x8a857a)], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.0, 1.2, 0.5, 14).translate(0, 3.2, 0), colA(0x9a958a)], [new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.6, 8, 6).translate(0, 4.0, 0), colA(0x7fb4d4)]]); SHARED.add(g); return g; })();

/* ═══ v14 PMQ: القصر والجامع — مبانٍ فخمة يمكن دخولها (هندسة إجرائية + خامات ./landmarks/) ═══ */
const PMQ = (() => {
  const HQ = (() => { const q = window.__QUALITY; if (q === 'high' || q === 'mobile') return q === 'high'; return !(matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches || Math.min(innerWidth, innerHeight) < 600); })();
  const SEG = HQ ? 48 : 28, RSEG = HQ ? 20 : 12, TB = './landmarks/', TLD = new THREE.TextureLoader(), AN = Math.min(8, renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy());
  const TEX = {}, MATS = {}, ST = { built: [], inside: null, frames: {}, farHidden: false };
  function tex(name, srgb) {                     // خامة تُحمَّل كسولاً؛ نسخة -hq للجودة العالية وإلا الأساسية؛ فشل التحميل = لون المادة فقط
    if (TEX[name]) return TEX[name];
    const t = new THREE.Texture(); t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; t.anisotropy = AN; if (srgb !== false) t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; TEX[name] = t;
    const done = img => { t.image = img; t.needsUpdate = true; for (const k in MATS) { const m = MATS[k]; if (m.userData.tx && m.userData.tx.includes(name) && !m.userData.on) { m.userData.on = 1; m.color.copy(m.userData.c1); m.needsUpdate = true; } } };
    const load = (u, fb) => TLD.load(u, x => done(x.image), undefined, () => { if (fb) load(fb, null); });
    if (HQ && HQSET.has(name)) load(TB + name + '-hq.webp', TB + name + '.webp'); else load(TB + name + '.webp', null);
    return t;
  }
  const HQSET = new Set(['marble', 'zellige', 'dome_tile', 'dome_in', 'mihrab', 'ceiling', 'stone', 'cream', 'runner', 'prayer', 'callig', 'marble_n', 'zellige_n', 'dome_tile_n', 'stone_n', 'cream_n', 'ceiling_n', 'dome_in_n']);
  function canvasTex(w, h, draw) { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h; draw(c.getContext('2d'), w, h); const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; t.anisotropy = AN; return t; }
  function mat(key, o) {                          // o: { c, map, n, rough, metal, env, emis, side, alpha, basic, vc, add, op }
    if (MATS[key]) return MATS[key];
    let m;
    if (o.basic) m = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: o.c, side: o.side || THREE.FrontSide, transparent: !!o.add || !!o.op, opacity: o.op || 1, depthWrite: !o.add, blending: o.add ? THREE.AdditiveBlending : THREE.NormalBlending, fog: true });
    else m = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: o.c, roughness: o.rough === undefined ? 0.85 : o.rough, metalness: o.metal || 0, side: o.side || THREE.FrontSide, vertexColors: o.vc !== false });
    m.userData.tx = []; m.userData.c1 = new THREE.Color(o.c1 !== undefined ? o.c1 : 0xffffff);
    if (o.map) { m.map = typeof o.map === 'string' ? tex(o.map) : o.map; if (typeof o.map === 'string') m.userData.tx.push(o.map); else m.color.set(m.userData.c1); }
    if (o.amap) { m.alphaMap = o.amap; m.transparent = true; }
    if (o.n && !o.basic) { m.normalMap = tex(o.n, false); m.normalScale.set(o.ns || 1, o.ns || 1); }
    if (o.alpha) { m.alphaTest = o.alpha; m.transparent = false; }
    if (o.env && !o.basic) { m.envMap = CHARENV; m.envMapIntensity = o.env; }
    if (o.emis && !o.basic) { m.emissive = new THREE.Color(o.ec || 0xffffff); m.emissiveIntensity = o.emis; if (m.map && o.emap !== false) m.emissiveMap = m.map; }
    return MATS[key] = m;
  }
  // ── مكتبة المواد ──

    domein: mat('domein', { c: 0xd8c8a0, c1: 0xffffff, map: 'dome_in', n: 'dome_in_n', ns: 0.4, rough: 0.6, side: THREE.BackSide, emis: 0.3, env: 0.3 }),
    ceil: mat('ceil', { c: 0x5a3a24, c1: 0xffffff, map: 'ceiling', n: 'ceiling_n', ns: 0.7, rough: 0.6, emis: 0.55, env: 0.3 }),

    mihrab: mat('mihrab', { c: 0x2a6a8a, c1: 0xffffff, map: 'mihrab', rough: 0.35, env: 0.4, emis: 0.3, vc: false }),
    glass: mat('glass', { basic: true, c: 0xffffff, map: tex('glass'), alpha: 0.4, side: THREE.DoubleSide }),

    hedge: mat('hedge', { c: 0x3c6a2c, n: 'stone_n', ns: 1.6, rough: 1 }),
    cypress: mat('cypress', { c: 0x27482a, n: 'stone_n', ns: 2, rough: 1 }),
    grass: mat('grass', { c: 0x5d8a3a, rough: 1 }),
    flower: mat('flower', { c: 0xffffff, rough: 0.8 }),
    ablaq: mat('ablaq', { c: 0xffffff, map: canvasTex(256, 32, (g, w, h) => { for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { g.fillStyle = i % 2 ?     ablaqx: mat('ablaqx', { c: 0xffffff, map: MATS.ablaq ? MATS.ablaq.map : null, rough: 0.7 }),
