    shaft: mat('shaft', { basic: true, c: 0xffe2a0, add: true, op: 0.14, side: THREE.DoubleSide, amap: canvasTex(64, 256, (g, w, h) => { const gr = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, h); gr.addColorStop(0, '#fff'); gr.addColorStop(1, '#000'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); const gx = g.createLinearGradient(0, 0, w, 0); gx.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)'); gx.addColorStop(0.25, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); gx.addColorStop(0.75, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); gx.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,1)'); g.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over'; g.fillStyle = gx; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); }) }),
  });
  // ── أدوات الهندسة: كل قطعة تُحوَّل إلى مثلثات منفصلة (position/normal/uv/color) ثم تُدمج لكل مادة ──
  const _m4 = new THREE.Matrix4(), _q = new THREE.Quaternion(), _e = new THREE.Euler(), _v = new THREE.Vector3(), _s = new THREE.Vector3();
  function Batch(aoY) { this.lists = {}; this.aoY = aoY; }
  Batch.prototype.add = function (key, geo, x, y, z, ry, rx, rz, sx, sy, sz, uvs) {
    let g = geo.index ? geo.toNonIndexed() : geo; if (g === geo) g = geo.clone();
    if (uvs) { const uv = g.attributes.uv; if (uv) for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) uv.setXY(i, uv.getX(i) * uvs[0], uv.getY(i) * (uvs[1] === undefined ? uvs[0] : uvs[1])); }
    _e.set(rx || 0, ry || 0, rz || 0); _q.setFromEuler(_e); _m4.compose(_v.set(x || 0, y || 0, z || 0), _q, _s.set(sx || 1, sy || sx || 1, sz || sx || 1)); g.applyMatrix4(_m4);
    if (!g.attributes.uv) g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 2), 2));
    if (!g.attributes.normal) g.computeVertexNormals();
    (this.lists[key] = this.lists[key] || []).push(g); return g;
  };
  Batch.prototype.build = function (group, mats, shadow) {
    let tris = 0;
    for (const key in this.lists) {
      const arr = this.lists[key]; let n = 0; for (const g of arr) n += g.attributes.position.count;
      const P = new Float32Array(n * 3), N = new Float32Array(n * 3), U = new Float32Array(n * 2), C = new Float32Array(n * 3); let o = 0;
      const aoY = this.aoY;
      for (const g of arr) { const p = g.attributes.position, nn = g.attributes.normal, uu = g.attributes.uv, c = p.count;
        P.set(p.array, o * 3); N.set(nn.array, o * 3); U.set(uu.array, o * 2);
        const ca = g.attributes.color;
        for (let i = 0; i < c; i++) { const y = p.getY(i); let a = 1; if (aoY !== undefined) { const h = y - aoY.y; a = h < 0 ? 0.75 : Math.min(1, 0.7 + 0.3 * h / (aoY.h || 1.3)); }
          const j = (o + i) * 3; if (ca) { C[j] = ca.getX(i) * a; C[j + 1] = ca.getY(i) * a; C[j + 2] = ca.getZ(i) * a; } else { C[j] = C[j + 1] = C[j + 2] = a; } }
        o += c; g.dispose(); }
      const bg = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); bg.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3)); bg.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(N, 3)); bg.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(U, 2)); bg.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(C, 3));
      bg.computeBoundingSphere(); const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(bg, mats[key.split('#')[0]]); mesh.castShadow = !!shadow && !mats[key.split('#')[0]].isMeshBasicMaterial; mesh.receiveShadow = true; mesh.userData.own = true; mesh.name = 'pmq:' + key; group.add(mesh); tris += n / 3;
    }
    this.lists = {}; return tris;
  };
  // صندوق بإحداثيات UV بالأمتار (tile = طول البلاطة)
  function boxG(w, h, d, tile) { const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d); if (tile) { const uv = g.attributes.uv, D = [[d, h], [d, h], [w, d], [w, d], [w, h], [w, h]]; for (let f = 0; f < 6; f++) for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) { const i = f * 4 + k; uv.setXY(i, uv.getX(i) * D[f][0] / tile, uv.getY(i) * D[f][1] / tile); } } return g; }
  function planeG(w, h, tu, tv) { const g = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h); if (tu) { const uv = g.attributes.uv; for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) uv.setXY(i, uv.getX(i) * tu, uv.getY(i) * (tv || tu)); } return g; }
  // مسار قوس مدبّب: من (cx-a, ys) إلى القمة ثم (cx+a, ys)
  function archPts(cx, a, ys, k, n) { const R = a * (k || 1.25), pts = [], n2 = n || 10, cL = cx - a + R, cR = cx + a - R, thr = Math.acos((R - a) / R), thl = Math.PI - thr;
    for (let i = 0; i <= n2; i++) { const t = Math.PI - (Math.PI - thl) * i / n2; pts.push([cL + R * Math.cos(t), ys + R * Math.sin(t)]); }
    for (let i = n2 - 1; i >= 0; i--) { const t = thr * i / n2; pts.push([cR + R * Math.cos(t), ys + R * Math.sin(t)]); }
    return pts; }
  function archApex(a, k) { const R = a * (k || 1.25); return Math.sqrt(R * R - (R - a) * (R - a)); }
  function holePath(cx, a, y0, ys, k) { const p = new THREE.Path(); p.moveTo(cx - a, y0); p.lineTo(cx - a, ys); for (const q of archPts(cx, a, ys, k, 10).slice(1)) p.lineTo(q[0], q[1]); p.lineTo(cx + a, y0); p.lineTo(cx - a, y0); return p; }
  // جدار بفتحات مقوّسة: الطول على x من x0 إلى x1، الارتفاع y0..y1، السماكة t (حول z=0). ops: [{x, w, h(نابض), k}]
  function wallG(x0, x1, y0, y1, t, ops, tile) { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(x0, y0); s.lineTo(x1, y0); s.lineTo(x1, y1); s.lineTo(x0, y1); s.lineTo(x0, y0);
    let bottomOpen = false;
    for (const o of ops || []) { const a = o.w / 2, yb = o.y0 !== undefined ? o.y0 : y0 + 0.001; if (yb <= y0 + 0.01) bottomOpen = true; s.holes.push(holePath(o.x, a, Math.max(yb, y0 + 0.002), (o.y0 !== undefined ? o.y0 : y0) + o.h, o.k)); }
    const g = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(s, { depth: t, bevelEnabled: false, curveSegments: 4 }); g.translate(0, 0, -t / 2);
    if (tile) { const uv = g.attributes.uv; for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) uv.setXY(i, uv.getX(i) / tile, uv.getY(i) / tile); }
    return g; }
  // حلقة قوس (أحجار متناوبة) بعرض b حول فتحة نصف عرضها a، سماكة t
  function archRingG(cx, a, ys, k, b, t, n) { const inner = archPts(cx, a, ys, k, n || 12);   // الخارجي = إزاحة الداخلي على العمودي
    const out = inner.map((p, i) => { const q0 = inner[Math.max(0, i - 1)], q1 = inner[Math.min(inner.length - 1, i + 1)], dx = q1[0] - q0[0], dy = q1[1] - q0[1], l = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1; return [p[0] - dy / l * b, p[1] + dx / l * b]; });
    out[0] = [cx - a - b, ys]; out[out.length - 1] = [cx + a + b, ys];
    const P = [], U = []; let acc = 0; const L = []; for (let i = 0; i < inner.length; i++) { if (i) acc += Math.hypot(inner[i][0] - inner[i - 1][0], inner[i][1] - inner[i - 1][1]); L.push(acc); }
    const tot = acc, segs = Math.max(6, Math.round(tot / 0.55));
    for (let i = 0; i < inner.length - 1; i++) { const a0 = inner[i], a1 = inner[i + 1], b0 = out[i], b1 = out[i + 1], u0 = L[i] / tot * segs / 8, u1 = L[i + 1] / tot * segs / 8;
      for (const z of [t / 2, -t / 2]) { const f = z > 0; const quad = f ? [a0, b1, b0, a0, a1, b1] : [a0, b0, b1, a0, b1, a1], uq = f ? [[u0, 0], [u1, 1], [u0, 1], [u0, 0], [u1, 0], [u1, 1]] : [[u0, 0], [u0, 1], [u1, 1], [u0, 0], [u1, 1], [u1, 0]];
        for (let j = 0; j < 6; j++) { P.push(quad[j][0], quad[j][1], z); U.push(uq[j][0], uq[j][1]); } }
      // باطن القوس
      const q = [[a0[0], a0[1], t / 2], [a1[0], a1[1], -t / 2], [a1[0], a1[1], t / 2], [a0[0], a0[1], t / 2], [a0[0], a0[1], -t / 2], [a1[0], a1[1], -t / 2]], uq2 = [[u0, 0], [u1, 1], [u1, 0], [u0, 0], [u0, 1], [u1, 1]];
      for (let j = 0; j < 6; j++) { P.push(...q[j]); U.push(uq2[j][0], uq2[j][1] * 0.3); } }
    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(P, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(U, 2)); g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; }
  function lathe(prof, seg, tile) { const g = new THREE.LatheGeometry(prof.map(p => new THREE.Vector2(p[0], p[1])), seg || SEG); if (tile) { const uv = g.attributes.uv; for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) uv.setXY(i, uv.getX(i) * tile[0], uv.getY(i) * tile[1]); } return g; }
  // قبة بصلية/مدبّبة: r نصف القطر، h الارتفاع، shape: 'onion' | 'point' | 'half'
  const DPROF = { onion: [[0.9, 0], [1.0, 0.07], [1.13, 0.26], [1.1, 0.42], [0.9, 0.6], [0.55, 0.76], [0.24, 0.88], [0.08, 0.96], [0.001, 1]],
    point: [[1, 0], [1, 0.08], [0.97, 0.28], [0.89, 0.48], [0.72, 0.68], [0.45, 0.84], [0.17, 0.95], [0.001, 1]],
    half: [[1, 0], [0.98, 0.17], [0.92, 0.38], [0.8, 0.6], [0.6, 0.8], [0.33, 0.94], [0.001, 1]] };
  function domeProf(r, h, shape) { const C = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(DPROF[shape].map(p => new THREE.Vector3(p[0] * r, p[1] * h, 0)), false, 'centripetal'); const n = HQ ? 26 : 14;
    return C.getPoints(n).map((p, i) => [Math.max(0.001, i === n ? 0.001 : p.x), p.y]); }
  function columnProf(r, h) { return [[0.001, 0], [r * 1.5, 0], [r * 1.5, 0.22], [r * 1.25, 0.3], [r * 1.12, 0.42], [r, 0.5], [r * 0.92, h - 0.7], [r * 1.05, h - 0.6], [r * 1.0, h - 0.5], [r * 1.35, h - 0.25], [r * 1.6, h - 0.14], [r * 1.6, h], [0.001, h]]; }
  function finialProf(s) { return [[0.001, 0], [0.18 * s, 0], [0.22 * s, 0.12 * s], [0.1 * s, 0.3 * s], [0.26 * s, 0.55 * s], [0.1 * s, 0.82 * s], [0.06 * s, 0.9 * s], [0.16 * s, 1.05 * s], [0.06 * s, 1.22 * s], [0.03 * s, 1.6 * s], [0.001, 1.75 * s]]; }
  // ── تصادم بإطار المبنى ──
  function Frame(L, ox, oz, yaw) { const c = Math.cos(yaw), s = Math.sin(yaw);
    const W = (lx, lz) => [ox + lx * c + lz * s, oz - lx * s + lz * c];
    return { W, yaw,
      wall(x0, z0, x1, z1, top) { const [x, z] = W((x0 + x1) / 2, (z0 + z1) / 2), b = solidBox(x, z, Math.abs(x1 - x0), Math.abs(z1 - z0), yaw); if (top !== undefined) b.top = top; L.solid.push(b); L.obst.push(b); return b; },
      col(lx, lz, r, top) { const [x, z] = W(lx, lz), b = [x, z, r]; if (top !== undefined) b.top = top; L.solid.push(b); L.obst.push(b); return b; } }; }
  function cutSegs(len, holes, y) {                // مقاطع الجدار الأفقية خارج فتحات الأبواب التي تقطع الارتفاع y
    let segs = [[-len / 2, len / 2]];
    for (const h of holes) { if (!(h.y0 <= y + 0.01 && h.y0 + h.h > y)) continue; const a = h.x - h.w / 2, b = h.x + h.w / 2, out = [];
      for (const [p, q] of segs) { if (b <= p || a >= q) { out.push([p, q]); continue; } if (a > p) out.push([p, a]); if (b < q) out.push([b, q]); } segs = out; }
    return segs.filter(([p, q]) => q - p > 0.05);
  }
  function stripAt(B, key, segs, x, z, ry, mk) { const c = Math.cos(ry), s = Math.sin(ry); for (const [a, b] of segs) { const m = (a + b) / 2; B.add(key, mk(b - a, a, b), x + c * m, 0, z - s * m, ry); } }
  function stairs(F, B, mkey, cx, z0, dirZ, w, y0, y1, depth, tile) {   // درج على امتداد z بدءاً من z0 باتجاه dirZ (±1)، من y0 إلى y1
    const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((y1 - y0) / 0.3 - 1e-6)), hs = (y1 - y0) / n;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const top = y0 + hs * (i + 1), za = z0 + dirZ * depth * i, zb = za + dirZ * depth, h = top - (y0 - 0.6);
      B.add(mkey, boxG(w, h, Math.abs(zb - za) + (i === n - 1 ? 0 : 0.02), tile || 2), cx, (y0 - 0.6) + h / 2, (za + zb) / 2); F.wall(cx - w / 2, Math.min(za, zb), cx + w / 2, Math.max(za, zb), top); }
    return n * depth; }
  // ── قطع زخرفية مركّبة ──
  function merlons(B, key, x0, x1, z, y, s, rot) { const n = Math.max(2, Math.round((x1 - x0) / (1.1 * s))); const g = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.0, 0.42 * s, 0.5 * s, 4, 1).rotateY(Math.PI / 4);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const x = x0 + (i + 0.5) * (x1 - x0) / n; const p = rot ? [z, x] : [x, z];
      B.add(key, boxG(0.6 * s, 0.75 * s, 0.5 * s, 1), p[0], y + 0.375 * s, p[1]); B.add(key, g, p[0], y + 0.75 * s + 0.25 * s, p[1], 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 0.6); } }
  function tower(B, key, x, z, y0, r, h, domeKey, domeShape, o) {   // برج مثمّن/دائري بشرفة وقبة وذروة ذهبية
    o = o || {}; const sides = o.sides || (HQ ? 16 : 10);
    B.add(key, new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r * 1.06, r * 1.12, h * 0.55, sides, 1), x, y0 + h * 0.275, z, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, [3 * r / 2, h * 0.55 / 3]);
    B.add(key, new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r, r * 1.06, h * 0.45, sides, 1), x, y0 + h * 0.775, z, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, [3 * r / 2, h * 0.45 / 3]);
    B.add(o.band || 'zelx', new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r * 1.075, r * 1.075, 0.9, sides, 1, true), x, y0 + h * 0.55 - 0.6, z, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, [3, 0.6]);
    B.add('gold', new THREE.TorusGeometry(r * 1.08, 0.07, 4, sides), x, y0 + h * 0.55 - 0.12, z, 0, Math.PI / 2);
    // شرفة
    const by = y0 + h * 0.8; B.add(key, new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r * 1.45, r * 1.05, 0.9, sides, 1), x, by - 0.45, z, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, [2, 0.4]);
    for (let i = 0; i < sides; i++) { const a = i / sides * Math.PI * 2; B.add(key, boxG(0.18, 0.9, 0.18, 1), x + Math.cos(a) * r * 1.38, by + 0.45, z + Math.sin(a) * r * 1.38); }
    B.add(key, new THREE.TorusGeometry(r * 1.38, 0.08, 4, sides), x, by + 0.92, z, 0, Math.PI / 2);
    // مشربيات على الجذع
    if (o.mash !== false) for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const a = i * Math.PI / 2 + (o.mrot || 0); B.add('mash', planeG(r * 0.75, r * 1.2, 1, 1.6), x + Math.cos(a) * r * 1.02, y0 + h * 0.66, z + Math.sin(a) * r * 1.02, -a + Math.PI / 2);
      B.add('dkwood', boxG(r * 0.85, r * 1.3, 0.12, 1), x + Math.cos(a) * r * 0.97, y0 + h * 0.66, z + Math.sin(a) * r * 0.97, -a + Math.PI / 2); }
    // القبة والذروة
    const dy = y0 + h; B.add(key, new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r * 1.02, r * 1.02, 0.4, sides, 1), x, dy + 0.2, z);
    const dh = (o.dh || 1.6) * r; B.add(domeKey, lathe(domeProf(r * (o.dr || 1.05), dh, domeShape), SEG, [6, 3]), x, dy + 0.4, z);
    B.add('gold', lathe(finialProf(o.fs || r * 0.9), RSEG), x, dy + 0.4 + dh - 0.05, z);
    return dy + 0.4 + dh; }
  function chandelier(B, x, y, z, r, n, drop) {   // ثريا نحاسية: حلقة + مصابيح زجاجية متوهجة + سلاسل
    B.add('brass', new THREE.TorusGeometry(r, 0.06 + r * 0.02, 6, HQ ? 40 : 24), x, y, z, 0, Math.PI / 2);
    if (r > 1.2) B.add('brass', new THREE.TorusGeometry(r * 0.55, 0.05, 6, 24), x, y + 0.45, z, 0, Math.PI / 2);
    B.add('brass', lathe([[0.001, -0.9], [0.14, -0.75], [0.3, -0.35], [0.32, 0], [0.18, 0.3], [0.08, 0.7], [0.001, 0.8]], RSEG), x, y, z, 0, 0, 0, 0.55 + r * 0.12, 0.6 + r * 0.1, 0.55 + r * 0.12);
    B.add('brass', new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.03, 0.03, drop, 4), x, y + drop / 2 + 0.6, z);
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { const a = i * 2.094; const ex = x + Math.cos(a) * r, ez = z + Math.sin(a) * r; const len = Math.hypot(r, 1.6);
      const g = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.015, 0.015, len, 3); g.rotateZ(Math.atan2(r, 1.6)); g.rotateY(-a); B.add('brass', g, (x + ex) / 2, y + 0.8, (z + ez) / 2); }
    const lamp = lathe([[0.001, 0], [0.09, 0.03], [0.12, 0.14], [0.09, 0.26], [0.05, 0.3], [0.001, 0.31]], 8);
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const a = i / n * Math.PI * 2; B.add('glow', lamp, x + Math.cos(a) * r, y + 0.04, z + Math.sin(a) * r, 0, 0, 0, 1.2, 1.2, 1.2); }
    if (r > 1.2) for (let i = 0; i < n / 2; i++) { const a = (i + 0.5) / (n / 2) * Math.PI * 2; B.add('glow', lamp, x + Math.cos(a) * r * 0.55, y + 0.5, z + Math.sin(a) * r * 0.55); }
    B.add('glow', lathe([[0.001, -1.1], [0.16, -1.0], [0.2, -0.9], [0.001, -0.8]], 8), x, y, z); }
  function lantern(B, x, y, z, s, drop) {         // فانوس معلّق مشغول (نحاس + زجاج متوهج)
    B.add('brass', new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.02, 0.02, drop, 4), x, y + drop / 2 + 0.45 * s, z);
    B.add('brass', lathe([[0.001, 0.75], [0.12, 0.62], [0.3, 0.45], [0.34, 0.4], [0.26, 0.36], [0.3, -0.2], [0.34, -0.25], [0.16, -0.4], [0.06, -0.55], [0.001, -0.6]].map(p => [p[0] * s, p[1] * s]), 8), x, y, z);
    B.add('glow', new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.27 * s, 0.25 * s, 0.56 * s, 8, 1, true), x, y + 0.08 * s, z); }
  function fountain(B, x, y, z, s, tiers) {        // نافورة رخامية متدرجة
    B.add('marble', lathe([[0.001, 0], [4.2 * s, 0], [4.3 * s, 0.2], [4.3 * s, 0.75], [4.0 * s, 0.85], [3.9 * s, 0.4], [0.001, 0.4]], SEG, [8, 1]), x, y, z);
    B.add('zelx', new THREE.CylinderGeometry(4.31 * s, 4.31 * s, 0.4, SEG, 1, true), x, y + 0.42, z, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, [10, 0.3]);
    B.add('gold', new THREE.TorusGeometry(4.15 * s, 0.07, 4, SEG), x, y + 0.86, z, 0, Math.PI / 2);
    B.add('water', new THREE.CircleGeometry(3.9 * s, SEG).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), x, y + 0.66, z);
    B.add('colm', lathe(columnProf(0.35 * s, 2.2 * s), RSEG), x, y + 0.4, z);
    B.add('marble', lathe([[0.001, 0], [0.4 * s, 0], [2.0 * s, 0.35 * s], [2.1 * s, 0.55 * s], [1.9 * s, 0.6 * s], [0.001, 0.4 * s]], SEG), x, y + 2.4 * s, z);
    B.add('water', new THREE.CircleGeometry(1.85 * s, SEG).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), x, y + 2.4 * s + 0.5 * s, z);
    if (tiers > 1) { B.add('colm', lathe(columnProf(0.2 * s, 1.2 * s), RSEG), x, y + 2.95 * s, z); B.add('marble', lathe([[0.001, 0], [0.25 * s, 0], [1.0 * s, 0.22 * s], [1.05 * s, 0.35 * s], [0.001, 0.25 * s]], SEG), x, y + 4.1 * s, z);
      B.add('water', new THREE.CircleGeometry(0.95 * s, 24).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), x, y + 4.1 * s + 0.31 * s, z); B.add('gold', lathe(finialProf(0.5 * s), RSEG), x, y + 4.4 * s, z); }
    // نوافير الماء (رذاذ شفاف)
    const jet = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.03, 0.12, 1.0, 6, 1, true); for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { const a = i / 8 * Math.PI * 2; B.add('jet', jet, x + Math.cos(a) * 1.5 * s, y + 2.4 * s + 0.9, z + Math.sin(a) * 1.5 * s); } }
  function cypress(B, x, y, z, h) { B.add('cypress', new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.78, 12, 8).scale(1, 0.82, 1), x, y + 0.6, z); }   // v16: بدل السرو (لا أشجار داخل السور)
  function flowerBed(B, x0, z0, x1, z1, y, rnd) {   // حوض أزهار بحافة سياج
    const w = x1 - x0, d = z1 - z0; B.add('hedge', boxG(w, 0.7, 0.5, 1.5), (x0 + x1) / 2, y + 0.35, z0 + 0.25); B.add('hedge', boxG(w, 0.7, 0.5, 1.5), (x0 + x1) / 2, y + 0.35, z1 - 0.25);
    B.add('hedge', boxG(0.5, 0.7, d - 1, 1.5), x0 + 0.25, y + 0.35, (z0 + z1) / 2); B.add('hedge', boxG(0.5, 0.7, d - 1, 1.5), x1 - 0.25, y + 0.35, (z0 + z1) / 2);
    B.add('grass', planeG(w - 1, d - 1).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), (x0 + x1) / 2, y + 0.12, (z0 + z1) / 2);
    const bush = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.34, 1).scale(1, 0.75, 1), bud = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.1, 0), cols = [[0.78, 0.05, 0.1], [0.95, 0.72, 0.1], [0.95, 0.93, 0.88], [0.52, 0.3, 0.75], [0.92, 0.35, 0.45]];
    const paint = (g, c) => { const C = new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 3); for (let j = 0; j < C.length; j += 3) { C[j] = c[0]; C[j + 1] = c[1]; C[j + 2] = c[2]; } g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(C, 3)); return g; };
    const n = Math.round(w * d * (HQ ? 0.9 : 0.45)), per = HQ ? 7 : 4;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const bx = x0 + 0.9 + rnd() * (w - 1.8), bz = z0 + 0.9 + rnd() * (d - 1.8), sc = 0.8 + rnd() * 0.5, by = y + 0.18 + 0.2 * sc;
      const g = B.add('hedge', bush.clone().scale(sc, sc, sc), bx, by, bz, rnd() * 6); const c = cols[(rnd() * cols.length) | 0];
      for (let k = 0; k < per; k++) { const a = rnd() * 6.283, rr = rnd() * 0.26 * sc; paint(B.add('flower', bud, bx + Math.cos(a) * rr, by + 0.2 * sc + 0.02 - rr * 0.35, bz + Math.sin(a) * rr), c); } } }
  function bannerAt(B, x, y, z, ry, w, h) { B.add('banner', planeG(w, h), x, y, z, ry); B.add('gold', new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.05, 0.05, w + 0.4, 6).rotateZ(Math.PI / 2), x, y + h / 2 + 0.05, z, ry); for (const s of [-1, 1]) B.add('gold', new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.09, 8, 6), x + Math.cos(ry) * s * (w / 2 + 0.22), y + h / 2 + 0.05, z - Math.sin(ry) * s * (w / 2 + 0.22)); }

  // ═══ القصر ═══ (الإطار المحلي: الأصل مركز التل، +z جنوباً نحو البوابة)
  function buildPalace(L, ctx) {
    const { pcx, pcz, cx, cz } = ctx, G = WORLD.hill.level, FY = G + 0.9, F = Frame(L, pcx, pcz, 0), mats = M(), rnd = rng(9917);
    const ext = new THREE.Group(), int = new THREE.Group(); ext.position.set(pcx - cx, 0, pcz - cz); int.position.copy(ext.position); L.g.add(ext, int);
    const BX = new Batch({ y: G, h: 2.5 }), BI = new Batch({ y: FY, h: 1.6 }), RY = -Math.PI / 2;
    // ── أرضية الساحة والسور الخارجي يبقى كما هو (wlS) ──
    BX.add('pave', new THREE.CircleGeometry(35.6, HQ ? 64 : 40).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), 0, G + 0.06, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, [35.6 / 3.2]);
    // ── الكتلة الرئيسية (جدران حجرية سميكة) ──
    const RT = G + 11;
    BX.add('pstone', boxG(41, RT - G + 0.3, 1.2, 3), 0, (G + RT) / 2 - 0.15, -14);                  // الخلفي
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) BX.add('pstone', boxG(1.2, RT - G + 0.3, 27.2, 3), s * 20, (G + RT) / 2 - 0.15, -1);   // الجانبان
    BX.add('pstone', wallG(-20, 20, G, RT, 1.2, [{ x: 0, w: 4, h: FY + 3.7 - G, y0: FY }], 3), 0, 0, 12);   // الواجهة (باب المدخل)
    // سطح مثقوب للقبة + شرفات
    { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(-20.6, -14.6); s.lineTo(20.6, -14.6); s.lineTo(20.6, 12.6); s.lineTo(-20.6, 12.6); s.lineTo(-20.6, -14.6); const h = new THREE.Path(); h.absarc(0, -3, 6.9, 0, Math.PI * 2, true); s.holes.push(h);
      const g = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(s, { depth: 0.6, bevelEnabled: false, curveSegments: HQ ? 24 : 14 }); g.rotateX(Math.PI / 2); BX.add('pstone', g, 0, RT + 0.6, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1, 1, [1 / 3]); }
    merlons(BX, 'pstone', -20.5, 20.5, -14.4, RT + 0.6, 1.1); merlons(BX, 'pstone', -20.5, 20.5, 12.4, RT + 0.6, 1.1);
    merlons(BX, 'pstone', -14.4, 12.4, -20.4, RT + 0.6, 1.1, true); merlons(BX, 'pstone', -14.4, 12.4, 20.4, RT + 0.6, 1.1, true);
    // أشرطة البلاط والذهب حول الكتلة
    for (const [w, d, x, z] of [[41.3, 0.1, 0, -14.66], [41.3, 0.1, 0, 12.66], [0.1, 27.3, -20.66, -1], [0.1, 27.3, 20.66, -1]]) { BX.add('zelx', boxG(w, 1.0, d, 1.4), x, RT - 1.3, z); BX.add('gold', boxG(w + 0.02, 0.1, d + 0.04), x, RT - 0.75, z); BX.add('gold', boxG(w + 0.02, 0.1, d + 0.04), x, RT - 1.85, z); }
    // شرفات خشبية بمشربيات على الجانبين والخلف
    const balcony = (x, z, ry) => { const c = Math.cos(ry), s = Math.sin(ry), P = (lx, lz) => [x + lx * c + lz * s, z - lx * s + lz * c];
      let p = P(0, 0.6); BX.add('dkwood', boxG(2.6, 0.35, 1.3, 1), p[0], G + 4.1, p[1], ry); BX.add('dkwood', boxG(2.7, 0.3, 1.35, 1), p[0], G + 7.0, p[1], ry);
      p = P(0, 1.22); BX.add('mash', planeG(2.4, 2.5, 1.5, 1.5), p[0], G + 5.5, p[1], ry); for (const sx of [-1.25, 1.25]) { p = P(sx, 0.6); BX.add('mash', planeG(1.2, 2.5, 0.75, 1.5), p[0], G + 5.5, p[1], ry + Math.PI / 2); }
      p = P(0, 0.62); BX.add('copper', lathe(domeProf(1.0, 0.9, 'onion'), 12), p[0], G + 7.15, p[1], 0, 0, 0, 1.25, 1, 0.65); };
    for (const z of [-9, -3, 3]) { balcony(20.6, z, Math.PI / 2); balcony(-20.6, z, -Math.PI / 2); }
    for (const x of [-12, -6, 6, 12]) balcony(x, -14.6, Math.PI);
    // ── البوابة الكبرى (بيشتاق) + الإيوان ──
    BX.add('cream', wallG(-5.6, 5.6, G, G + 16, 1.0, [{ x: 0, w: 6, h: 7.6, y0: G }], 2.5), 0, 0, 14.1);
    merlons(BX, 'cream', -5.6, 5.6, 14.1, G + 16, 0.9);
    BX.add('zelx', wallG(-5.0, 5.0, G + 1.0, G + 15.2, 0.06, [{ x: 0, w: 6.9, h: 6.6, y0: G + 1.0 }], 1.6), 0, 0, 14.63);  // إطار البلاط
    BX.add('gold', archRingG(0, 3, G + 7.6, 1.25, 0.32, 1.06, 16), 0, 0, 14.1);
    BX.add('callig', planeG(9.4, 0.9, 6, 1), 0, G + 14.4, 14.66);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) { BX.add('cream', boxG(1.1, 11.6, 2.0, 2.5), s * 3.55, G + 5.8, 13.1); BX.add('colm', lathe(columnProf(0.32, 7.4), RSEG), s * 3.15, G, 14.75); BX.add('gold', lathe(finialProf(1.4), RSEG), s * 5.3, G + 16.7, 14.1); }
    { const hd = new THREE.SphereGeometry(3, HQ ? 24 : 14, 8, 0, Math.PI, 0, Math.PI / 2); hd.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2); hd.rotateY(Math.PI); BX.add('goldi', hd, 0, G + 7.6 + archApex(3, 1.25) - 3.0, 12.65, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1.05, 0.66); }
    BX.add('zelx', boxG(5.95, 0.1, 2.0, 1.5), 0, G + 7.6 + archApex(3, 1.25) - 0.02 + 0.8, 13.6);
    bannerAt(BX, -4.6 - 0.9, G + 9.6, 15.2, 0, 1.6, 4.2); bannerAt(BX, 4.6 + 0.9, G + 9.6, 15.2, 0, 1.6, 4.2);
    // ── المصطبة الأمامية والدرج ──
    BX.add('cream', boxG(33, FY - G + 0.6, 3.0, 2), 0, (FY + G - 0.6) / 2, 14.0); BX.add('marble', planeG(33, 3.0, 8, 0.75).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), 0, FY + 0.01, 14.0);
    F.wall(-16.5, 12.5, 16.5, 15.5, FY);
    stairs(F, BX, 'cream', 0, 17.3, -1, 7.2, G, FY, 0.6, 2);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) { BX.add('marble', boxG(0.5, 1.4, 1.8, 1), s * 3.85, FY + 0.1, 16.4); BX.add('gold', lathe(finialProf(0.7), RSEG), s * 3.85, FY + 0.8, 16.4); }
    // ── الأروقة الجانبية (لوجيا) ──
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const x0 = s > 0 ? 5.6 : -16.2, x1 = s > 0 ? 16.2 : -5.6, ops = [], pierX = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { const xc = x0 + (i + 0.5) * (x1 - x0) / 3; ops.push({ x: xc, w: 2.7, h: 3.0, y0: FY }); }
      BX.add('cream', wallG(x0, x1, G, G + 8.2, 0.7, ops, 2.5), 0, 0, 15.15);
      for (const o of ops) BX.add('goldi', archRingG(o.x, 1.35, FY + 3.0, 1.25, 0.16, 0.74, 12), 0, 0, 15.15);

    { const xs = [-15.5]; for (const d of doors) xs.push(d.x - d.w / 2, d.x + d.w / 2); xs.push(15.5); for (let i = 0; i < xs.length; i += 2) F.wall(xs[i], -0.5, xs[i + 1], 0.5); }
    F.wall(-4.6, 0.65, -2.5, 1.55); F.wall(2.5, 0.65, 4.6, 1.55);
    BI.add('marble', planeG(2 * HX, HZ1 - HZ0, 7, 3.2).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), 0, FY + 0.008, (HZ0 + HZ1) / 2);
    BI.add('prayer', planeG(2 * HX - 0.6, 12.4, (2 * HX - 0.6) / 1.05, 12.4 / 1.45).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), 0, FY + 0.018, -6.9);
    const skin = (x, z, len, ry, holes) => { const t = 0.04;
      stripAt(BI, 'zellige', cutSegs(len, holes || [], FY + 0.8), x, z, ry, (l, a) => wallG(a, a + l, FY, FY + 1.6, t, [], 1.2).translate(-(a + l / 2), 0, 0));
      BI.add('plaster', wallG(-len / 2, len / 2, FY + 1.6, HC, t, (holes || []).map(h => h.y0 < FY + 1.6 ? { ...h, y0: FY + 1.6, h: h.h - (FY + 1.6 - h.y0) } : h), 3), x, 0, z, ry);
      stripAt(BI, 'goldi', cutSegs(len, holes || [], FY + 1.6), x, z, ry, l => boxG(l, 0.09, 0.06).translate(0, FY + 1.6, 0));
      const c = Math.cos(ry), s = Math.sin(ry), segs = [[-len / 2, len / 2]]; for (const h of holes || []) if (h.y0 + h.h + archApex(h.w / 2) > FY + 6.0) { const sg = segs.pop(); segs.push([sg[0], h.x - h.w / 2 - 0.5], [h.x + h.w / 2 + 0.5, sg[1]]); }
      for (const [a, b] of segs) if (b - a > 0.6) { const m = (a + b) / 2; BI.add('callig', planeG(b - a, 0.75, (b - a) / 3.2, 1), x + c * m - s * 0.035, FY + 6.4, z - s * m - c * 0.035, ry); }
      BI.add('goldi', boxG(len, 0.07, 0.07), x, FY + 6.82, z, ry); BI.add('goldi', boxG(len, 0.07, 0.07), x, FY + 5.98, z, ry); };
    skin(0, HZ0 + 0.02, 2 * HX, 0, [{ x: 0, w: 2.8, h: 3.0, y0: FY }, { x: -6.5, w: 1.3, h: 2.0, y0: FY + 4.6 }, { x: 6.5, w: 1.3, h: 2.0, y0: FY + 4.6 }]);
    skin(0, HZ1 - 0.02, 2 * HX, Math.PI, doors.map(d => ({ ...d, x: -d.x })));
    skin(-HX + 0.02, (HZ0 + HZ1) / 2, HZ1 - HZ0, Math.PI / 2, [-11, -7, -3].map(z => ({ x: z + 7, w: 1.3, h: 2.0, y0: FY + 4.6 })));
    skin(HX - 0.02, (HZ0 + HZ1) / 2, HZ1 - HZ0, -Math.PI / 2, [-11, -7, -3].map(z => ({ x: -(z + 7), w: 1.3, h: 2.0, y0: FY + 4.6 })));
    for (const d of doors) BI.add('ablaq', archRingG(d.x, d.w / 2, d.y0 + d.h, 1.25, 0.3, 0.1, 10), 0, 0, HZ1 - 0.06, 0);
    // السقف المرسوم مع فتحة القبة + عوارض
    { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(-HX, HZ0); s.lineTo(HX, HZ0); s.lineTo(HX, HZ1); s.lineTo(-HX, HZ1); s.lineTo(-HX, HZ0); const h = new THREE.Path(); h.absarc(0, DZ, 6.3, 0, Math.PI * 2, true); s.holes.push(h);
      const g = new THREE.ShapeGeometry(s, HQ ? 32 : 18); g.rotateX(Math.PI / 2); const uv = g.attributes.uv; for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) uv.setXY(i, uv.getX(i) / 3.4, uv.getY(i) / 3.4); BI.add('ceil', g, 0, HC, 0);
      BI.add('goldi', new THREE.TorusGeometry(6.35, 0.16, 6, SEG), 0, HC - 0.08, DZ, 0, Math.PI / 2); }
    // الأعمدة والأقواس الأبلق (صفّان على كل جانب)
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) for (const xr of [7.0, 10.8]) { const x = s * xr, E = [HZ0 + 0.4, -10.25, -6.75, -3.25, HZ1 - 0.4], ops = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const a = E[i] + (i ? 0.42 : 0), b = E[i + 1] - (i < 3 ? 0.42 : 0); ops.push({ x: (a + b) / 2, w: b - a, h: 4.0, y0: FY }); }
      const top = xr < 8 ? HC : HC; BI.add('plaster', wallG(HZ0, HZ1, FY, top, 0.62, ops, 3), x, 0, 0, RY);
      for (const o of ops) { BI.add('ablaq', archRingG(o.x, o.w / 2, FY + 4.0, 1.25, 0.34, 0.66, 10), x, 0, 0, RY); }
      for (const z of [-10.25, -6.75, -3.25]) { BI.add('colm', lathe(columnProf(0.36, 4.0), RSEG), x, FY, z); BI.add('goldi', new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.4, 0.05, 5, RSEG), x, FY + 3.6, z, 0, Math.PI / 2); F.col(x, z, 0.5); }
      F.wall(x - 0.31, HZ0, x + 0.31, HZ0 + 0.4); F.wall(x - 0.31, HZ1 - 0.4, x + 0.31, HZ1);
      for (const side of [-1, 1]) BI.add('callig', planeG(HZ1 - HZ0, 0.6, (HZ1 - HZ0) / 3.2, 1), x + side * 0.33, FY + 7.2, (HZ0 + HZ1) / 2, side > 0 ? Math.PI / 2 : -Math.PI / 2); }
    // المحراب
    { BI.add('zellige', wallG(-3.3, 3.3, FY, FY + 7.4, 0.06, [{ x: 0, w: 2.8, h: 3.0, y0: FY }], 1.0), 0, 0, HZ0 + 0.08);
      BI.add('goldi', archRingG(0, 1.4, FY + 3.0, 1.25, 0.22, 0.14, 14), 0, 0, HZ0 + 0.1); BI.add('goldi', archRingG(0, 3.0, FY + 4.9, 1.25, 0.18, 0.1, 14), 0, 0, HZ0 + 0.1);
      for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { BI.add('colm', lathe(columnProf(0.12, 3.0), 10), sx * 1.55, FY, HZ0 + 0.22); BI.add('goldi', boxG(0.25, 4.9, 0.1), sx * 3.05, FY + 2.45, HZ0 + 0.12); }
      BI.add('mihrab', planeG(2.75, 4.8), 0, FY + 2.4, -14.42); BI.add('callig', planeG(6.4, 0.7, 2, 1), 0, FY + 6.95, HZ0 + 0.12);
      const hd = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.4, 16, 6, 0, Math.PI, 0, Math.PI / 2); hd.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2); hd.rotateY(Math.PI); BI.add('goldi', hd, 0, FY + 3.0 + archApex(1.4) - 1.45, -14.42, 0, 0, 0, 1, 1.1, 0.62);
      F.wall(-1.6, -13.6, 1.6, -13.3); }
    // المنبر (خشب محفور: درج + جوسق بقبة صغيرة)
    { const mx = 3.7, z0 = -7.9, zt = -12.0, PY = FY + 2.7, n = 9; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const t = (i + 1) / n, zz = z0 + (zt - z0) * (i + 0.5) / n; BI.add('dkwood', boxG(1.0, 0.12, (z0 - zt) / n + 0.02, 1), mx, FY + (PY - FY) * t, zz); BI.add('prayer', planeG(0.8, (z0 - zt) / n).rotateX(-Math.PI / 2), mx, FY + (PY - FY) * t + 0.065, zz); }
      for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(0, 0); s.lineTo(z0 - zt, 0); s.lineTo(z0 - zt, 0.2); s.lineTo(0, PY - FY + 0.9); s.lineTo(0, 0);
        const g = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(s, { depth: 0.08, bevelEnabled: false }); const uv = g.attributes.uv; for (let i = 0; i < uv.count; i++) uv.setXY(i, uv.getX(i) / 1.6, uv.getY(i) / 1.6); BI.add('wood', g, mx + sx * 0.56 + 0.04, FY, zt, -Math.PI / 2); }
      BI.add('dkwood', boxG(1.2, PY - FY, 1.3, 1), mx, (PY + FY) / 2, zt - 0.6); BI.add('wood', boxG(1.22, PY - FY - 0.2, 1.32, 1.6), mx, (PY + FY) / 2, zt - 0.6);
      for (const [px, pz] of [[-0.55, -0.0], [0.55, -0.0], [-0.55, -1.2], [0.55, -1.2]]) BI.add('dkwood', boxG(0.12, 2.3, 0.12, 1), mx + px, PY + 1.15, zt + pz);
      BI.add('dkwood', boxG(1.3, 0.25, 1.4, 1), mx, PY + 2.4, zt - 0.6); BI.add('goldi', lathe(domeProf(0.62, 1.3, 'point'), 16), mx, PY + 2.52, zt - 0.6); BI.add('goldi', lathe(finialProf(0.45), 8), mx, PY + 3.8, zt - 0.6);
      BI.add('wood', wallG(-0.65, 0.65, FY, FY + 3.2, 0.14, [{ x: 0, w: 0.86, h: 2.0, y0: FY }], 1.4), mx, 0, z0 + 0.05); BI.add('goldi', archRingG(0, 0.43, FY + 2.0, 1.25, 0.06, 0.16, 8), mx, 0, z0 + 0.05); merlons(BI, 'goldi', mx - 0.6, mx + 0.6, z0 + 0.05, FY + 3.2, 0.3);
      F.wall(mx - 0.65, -13.4, mx + 0.65, z0 + 0.15); }
    // الثريات: حلقة كبيرة تحت القبة + ثريات صغيرة + فوانيس
    chandelier(BI, 0, FY + 5.0, DZ, 3.8, HQ ? 36 : 24, HT + 0.5 + 3.6 + 7 - FY - 5.6);
    chandelier(BI, 0, FY + 5.7, DZ, 2.0, HQ ? 18 : 12, 0.1);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) { for (const z of [-11.8, -1.9]) chandelier(BI, s * 8.9, FY + 4.7, z, 0.8, 8, HC - FY - 5.3); for (const z of [-8.5, -5.0]) lantern(BI, s * 12.6, FY + 4.4, z, 0.8, HC - FY - 4.9); for (const z of [-8.5, -5.0]) lantern(BI, s * 8.9, FY + 4.8, z, 0.7, HC - FY - 5.3); }
    // أشعة الضوء من النوافذ الملوّنة
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) for (const z of [-11, -7, -3]) { const sh = planeG(1.3, 8.0); sh.rotateX(-0.7); BI.add('shaft', sh, s * (HX - 2.6), FY + 3.2, z, s > 0 ? -Math.PI / 2 : Math.PI / 2); }
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const sh = planeG(1.3, 7.0); sh.rotateX(-0.7); BI.add('shaft', sh, s * 6.5, FY + 3.4, HZ0 + 2.4, 0); }
    const tris = BX.build(ext, mats, true) + BI.build(int, mats, false);
    const B = { name: 'mosque', ext, int, ox, oz, yaw, F, tris, R: 60, rooms: [{ x0: -HX, x1: HX, z0: HZ0, z1: HZ1, ceil: HC - 0.3, fy: FY }, { x0: RX, x1: 15, z0: 1.0, z1: 14, ceil: RH - 0.3 }, { x0: -15, x1: -RX, z0: 1.0, z1: 14, ceil: RH - 0.3 }, { x0: -15, x1: 15, z0: RZ, z1: 14.5, ceil: RH - 0.3 }] };
    ST.built.push(B); ST.frames.mosque = (x, z) => F.W(x, z); return B;
  }

  // ── التشغيل كل إطار: إظهار الداخل قرباً، الكاميرا تحت السقف داخل القاعات، إخفاء صندوق القصر البعيد القديم ──

  function pmqAvg24(tex) { const im = tex && tex.image; if (!im || !(im.width > 0)) return null; if (tex.userData.avg24) return tex.userData.avg24;
    const cv = document.createElement('canvas'); cv.width = cv.height = 8; const x = cv.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true }); x.drawImage(im, 0, 0, 8, 8); const d = x.getImageData(0, 0, 8, 8).data; let r = 0, g = 0, b = 0; for (let i = 0; i < d.length; i += 4) { r += d[i]; g += d[i + 1]; b += d[i + 2]; }
    const f = v => Math.pow(v / 64 / 255, 2.2); return (tex.userData.avg24 = [f(r), f(g), f(b)]); }
  function pmqFar24(B) {   /* v24: نسخة بعيدة من القصر/الجامع = شبكة واحدة بألوان الرؤوس (نداء رسم واحد بدل ~٢٠) — تُستعمل بعد ١٣٥م فقط */
    if (B.far24) return B.far24; if (B.f24x) return null; const now = performance.now(); if (B.f24t && now - B.f24t < 1500) return null; B.f24t = now;
    try { const ext = B.ext, list = []; let ok = true; ext.updateMatrixWorld(true); const inv = ext.matrixWorld.clone().invert();
      ext.traverse(o => { if (!o.isMesh || o.isInstancedMesh || o.isSkinnedMesh || !o.geometry || !o.geometry.attributes.position) return; let p = o, vis = true; while (p && p !== ext) { if (!p.visible) vis = false; p = p.parent; } if (!vis) return;
        const m = Array.isArray(o.material) ? o.material[0] : o.material; if (!m || m.transparent && m.opacity < 0.3) return; let c = [m.color ? m.color.r : 1, m.color ? m.color.g : 1, m.color ? m.color.b : 1];
        if (m.map) { const a = pmqAvg24(m.map); if (!a) { ok = false; return; } c = [c[0] * a[0], c[1] * a[1], c[2] * a[2]]; }
        if ((m.metalness || 0) < 0.9 && m.emissive && m.emissiveIntensity) c = c.map((v, i) => v + m.emissive.toArray()[i] * m.emissiveIntensity * 0.5);
        list.push([o, c, new THREE.Matrix4().multiplyMatrices(inv, o.matrixWorld), (m.metalness || 0) >= 0.9 ? m : null]); });
      list.sort((a, b) => (a[3] ? 1 : 0) - (b[3] ? 1 : 0)); let k0 = -1, envM = null;
      if (!ok) return null; if (!list.length) { B.f24x = 1; return null; }
      let nv = 0; for (const [o] of list) nv += o.geometry.index ? o.geometry.index.count : o.geometry.attributes.position.count;
      const P = new Float32Array(nv * 3), N = new Float32Array(nv * 3), Cc = new Float32Array(nv * 3), v = new THREE.Vector3(), nm = new THREE.Matrix3(); let k = 0;
      for (const [o, c, M, mt] of list) { if (mt && k0 < 0) { k0 = k; envM = mt; } const g = o.geometry, pa = g.attributes.position, na = g.attributes.normal, ca = g.attributes.color, ix = g.index; nm.getNormalMatrix(M); const n = ix ? ix.count : pa.count;
        for (let t = 0; t < n; t++) { const i = ix ? ix.getX(t) : t; v.fromBufferAttribute(pa, i).applyMatrix4(M); P[k * 3] = v.x; P[k * 3 + 1] = v.y; P[k * 3 + 2] = v.z;
          if (na) { v.fromBufferAttribute(na, i).applyMatrix3(nm).normalize(); N[k * 3] = v.x; N[k * 3 + 1] = v.y; N[k * 3 + 2] = v.z; } else N[k * 3 + 1] = 1;
          const vc = ca && o.material.vertexColors ? [ca.getX(i), ca.getY(i), ca.getZ(i)] : [1, 1, 1]; Cc[k * 3] = c[0] * vc[0]; Cc[k * 3 + 1] = c[1] * vc[1]; Cc[k * 3 + 2] = c[2] * vc[2]; k++; } }
      const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3)); g.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(N, 3)); g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(Cc, 3)); g.computeBoundingSphere();
      if (k0 < 0) k0 = k; g.addGroup(0, k0, 0); g.addGroup(k0, k - k0, 1);   // المعادن (ذهب/نحاس) بمادة معدنية لامعة كالأصل
      const mats = [pmqFar24.mat || (pmqFar24.mat = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.82, metalness: 0 })), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, metalness: 1, roughness: envM ? envM.roughness : 0.3, envMap: envM ? envM.envMap : null, envMapIntensity: envM ? envM.envMapIntensity : 1.3 })];
      const fm = new THREE.Mesh(g, mats); fm.name = 'pmq24far'; fm.receiveShadow = true; fm.castShadow = false;
      fm.position.copy(ext.position); fm.quaternion.copy(ext.quaternion); fm.scale.copy(ext.scale); fm.visible = false; ext.parent.add(fm); B.far24 = fm; console.info('[v24] PMQ far mesh', list.length, 'parts →1,', k, 'verts'); return fm;
    } catch (e) { B.f24x = 1; console.warn('[v24] PMQ far', e); return null; } }
  function tick(dt) {
    if (!ST.built.length) return;
    const L = ST.L; ST.inside = null;
    if (L && !ST.farHidden && L.farG && L.farG.children.length) { for (const m of L.farG.children) { const p = m.geometry && m.geometry.parameters; if (p && p.width === 40 && p.height === 17) m.visible = false; } ST.farHidden = true; }
    for (const B of ST.built) {
      const dx = PL.x - B.ox, dz = PL.z - B.oz, d = Math.hypot(dx, dz); B.int.visible = d < B.R - 15;   /* الداخل يُرسم فقط على بعد ≤45م */ { const fd = d > ((B.far24 && B.far24.visible) ? 125 : 135), fm = fd && !/nofar/.test(location.search) ? pmqFar24(B) : null; B.ext.visible = d < 1100 && !fm; if (B.far24) B.far24.visible = d < 1100 && !!fm; }
      if (d > B.R) continue; const c = Math.cos(B.yaw), s = Math.sin(B.yaw), lx = dx * c - dz * s, lz = dx * s + dz * c;
      for (const r of B.rooms) if (lx > r.x0 - 0.3 && lx < r.x1 + 0.3 && lz > r.z0 - 0.3 && lz < r.z1 + 0.3 && PL.y < r.ceil - 1.2) { ST.inside = { B, r, lx, lz }; break; }
    }
    const I = ST.inside; if (!I) return;
    const B = I.B, r = I.r, c = Math.cos(B.yaw), s = Math.sin(B.yaw);
    let wx = camera.position.x + ORG.x - B.ox, wz = camera.position.z + ORG.z - B.oz, cl = wx * c - wz * s, cz2 = wx * s + wz * c, moved = false;
    const m = 0.35; if (cl < r.x0 + m) { cl = r.x0 + m; moved = true; } if (cl > r.x1 - m) { cl = r.x1 - m; moved = true; } if (cz2 < r.z0 + m) { cz2 = r.z0 + m; moved = true; } if (cz2 > r.z1 - m) { cz2 = r.z1 - m; moved = true; }
    if (camera.position.y > r.ceil) { camera.position.y = r.ceil; moved = true; }
    if (moved) { camera.position.x = B.ox + cl * c + cz2 * s - ORG.x; camera.position.z = B.oz - cl * s + cz2 * c - ORG.z; camera.lookAt(CAM.tx - ORG.x, Math.min(CAM.ty, r.ceil - 0.2), CAM.tz - ORG.z); }
  }
  function palace(L, ctx) { ST.L = L; try { const t0 = performance.now(), B = buildPalace(L, ctx); console.info('[PMQ] palace', Math.round(B.tris), 'tris', Math.round(performance.now() - t0), 'ms', HQ ? 'high' : 'mobile'); }
    catch (e) { console.warn('[PMQ] palace build failed', e); } }
  function mosque(L, RES, ctx, p) { ST.L = L; const [x, z] = p, yaw = Math.atan2(ctx.cx - x, ctx.cz - z); RES.push({ x, z, r: 19, lamp: true });
    try { const t0 = performance.now(), B = buildMosque(L, ctx, x, z, yaw); console.info('[PMQ] mosque', Math.round(B.tris), 'tris', Math.round(performance.now() - t0), 'ms'); }
    catch (e) { console.warn('[PMQ] mosque build failed', e); } }
  window.__pmq = { ST, HQ, frame: k => ST.frames[k], get inside() { return ST.inside ? ST.inside.B.name : null; }, get tris() { return ST.built.map(b => [b.name, Math.round(b.tris)]); } };
  return { palace, mosque, tick, HQ, ST };
})();


/* ═══ v15: سور المدينة الجديد (حجر كلسي كريمي/عسلي، شرفات، أبراج مربعة مشرّفة ودائرية بقباب صغيرة، رايات زرقاء بأسد ذهبي، بوابة بقوس مدبّب وأبواب خشب بمسامير حديد) — نفس المواضع والمصادمات ═══ */
const C15 = (() => {
  const nrm = new THREE.Vector2(0.6, 0.6), M = [
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: T15.wallstone, normalMap: T15.wallstone_n, normalScale: nrm, color: 0xffffff, roughness: 0.93 }),          // 0 حجر
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: T15.wallstone, normalMap: T15.wallstone_n, normalScale: nrm, color: 0xd6c6a8, roughness: 0.9 }),           // 1 حجر أغمق (أطر وأحزمة)
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: T15.roof, normalMap: T15.roof_n, color: 0xf2e6e0, roughness: 0.82 }),                                     // 2 قرميد
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: T15.door, normalMap: T15.door_n, color: 0xffffff, roughness: 0.78, metalness: 0.08, side: THREE.DoubleSide }),   // 3 خشب الأبواب
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x15120f, roughness: 1 }),                                                                          // 4 فتحات الرمي
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: T15.banner, color: 0xffffff, roughness: 0.88, side: THREE.DoubleSide }),                                  // 5 الرايات
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc9a043, roughness: 0.45, metalness: 0.6 }) ];                                                        // 6 ذهب (قضبان الرايات وأهلّة القباب)                                                                          // 4 فتحات الرمي
  const TILE = 1.7, V3 = THREE.Vector3;
  const proj = (geo, tile) => { const g = geo.index ? geo.toNonIndexed() : geo, p = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(p.count * 2), a = new V3(), b = new V3(), c = new V3(), n = new V3();
    for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i += 3) { a.fromBufferAttribute(p, i); b.fromBufferAttribute(p, i + 1); c.fromBufferAttribute(p, i + 2); n.subVectors(c, b).cross(new V3().subVectors(a, b)).normalize();
      const ax = Math.abs(n.x), ay = Math.abs(n.y), az = Math.abs(n.z);
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const q = [a, b, c][k]; let u, v; if (ay >= ax && ay >= az) { u = q.x; v = q.z; } else if (ax >= az) { u = q.z; v = q.y; } else { u = q.x; v = q.y; } uv[(i + k) * 2] = u / tile; uv[(i + k) * 2 + 1] = v / tile; } }
    g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); g.deleteAttribute('normal'); g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; };
  const merge = parts => { const by = [[], [], [], [], [], [], []]; for (const [g, mi, keepUV] of parts) by[mi].push(keepUV ? (g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g) : proj(g, mi === 2 ? 2.2 : TILE));
    let n = 0; for (const L of by) for (const g of L) n += g.attributes.position.count;
    const P = new Float32Array(n * 3), N = new Float32Array(n * 3), U = new Float32Array(n * 2), out = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); let o = 0;
    by.forEach((L, mi) => { const s = o; for (const g of L) { P.set(g.attributes.position.array, o * 3); N.set(g.attributes.normal.array, o * 3); U.set(g.attributes.uv.array, o * 2); o += g.attributes.position.count; } if (o > s) out.addGroup(s, o - s, mi); });
    out.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3)); out.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(N, 3)); out.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(U, 2));
    out.computeBoundingBox(); out.computeBoundingSphere(); out.userData.mat = M; SHARED.add(out); return out; };
  const box = (w, h, d, x, y, z) => new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d).translate(x || 0, (y || 0) + h / 2, z || 0);
  const merlons = (parts, x0, x1, y, zs, step, mw, mh, md) => { for (let x = x0 + step / 2; x < x1; x += step) for (const z of zs) parts.push([box(mw, mh, md, x, y, z), 0]); };
  const ring = (parts, r, y, n, mw, mh, md) => { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const a = (i + 0.5) / n * Math.PI * 2; parts.push([box(mw, mh, md).rotateY(-a).translate(Math.cos(a) * r, y, Math.sin(a) * r), 0]); } };
  const banner = (parts, x, y, z, ry, w, h) => { const s = new THREE.Shape(), t = h * 0.16; s.moveTo(-w / 2, 0); s.lineTo(w / 2, 0); s.lineTo(w / 2, -h); s.lineTo(0, -h + t); s.lineTo(-w / 2, -h); s.closePath();
    const g = new THREE.ShapeGeometry(s).toNonIndexed(), p = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(p.count * 2); for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) { uv[i * 2] = p.getX(i) / w + 0.5; uv[i * 2 + 1] = 1 + p.getY(i) / h; }
    g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); g.rotateY(ry).translate(x, y, z); g.computeVertexNormals(); parts.push([g, 5, true]);
    parts.push([new THREE.BoxGeometry(w + 0.3, 0.09, 0.09).rotateY(ry).translate(x, y + 0.04, z), 6]); };
  const slit = (parts, x, y, z, ry) => parts.push([box(0.22, 1.1, 0.08).rotateY(ry || 0).translate(x, y, z), 4]);
  const arch = (a, s, h, n) => { const R = (a * a + h * h) / (2 * a), c = a - R, th = Math.atan2(h, -c), pts = [];   // قوس مدبّب: نصف عرض a، بداية القوس s، ارتفاع القوس h
    for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const t = th * i / n; pts.push([c + R * Math.cos(t), s + R * Math.sin(t)]); } return pts; };          // من (a,s) إلى القمة (0,s+h)
  // ── قطعة السور (محلي: x ±4.22، يُقاس بمصفوفة النسخة) ──
  const wall = () => { const p = [];
    p.push([box(8.6, 7.0, 2.3, 0, 0, 0), 0], [box(8.6, 1.0, 2.86, 0, 0, 0), 1], [box(8.6, 0.25, 2.62, 0, 1.0, 0), 1], [box(8.6, 0.32, 2.62, 0, 6.9, 0), 1]);
    merlons(p, -4.3, 4.3, 7.22, [1.13, -1.13], 1.43, 0.78, 1.0, 0.34);
    banner(p, 0, 6.55, 1.18, 0, 1.25, 3.3); banner(p, 0, 6.55, -1.18, Math.PI, 1.25, 3.3); return merge(p); };
  // ── برج مربّع (محلي ±3.68) بسطح مشرّف ورايات ──
  const towerQ = () => { const p = [];
    p.push([box(6.6, 10.2, 6.6), 0], [box(7.36, 1.2, 7.36), 1], [box(7.4, 0.9, 7.4, 0, 10.1, 0), 1]);
    merlons(p, -3.7, 3.7, 11.0, [3.52, -3.52], 1.48, 0.8, 0.95, 0.36);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) p.push([box(0.36, 0.95, 0.8, s * 3.52, 11.0, -2.96 + k * 1.48 + 0.74), 0]);
    for (const [x, z, ry] of [[0, 3.72, 0], [0, -3.72, Math.PI], [3.72, 0, Math.PI / 2], [-3.72, 0, -Math.PI / 2]]) banner(p, x, 9.5, z, ry, 1.7, 4.4);
    for (const [x, z, ry] of [[0, 3.31, 0], [0, -3.31, 0], [3.31, 0, Math.PI / 2], [-3.31, 0, Math.PI / 2]]) { slit(p, x, 4.2, z, ry); slit(p, x, 7.4, z, ry); }
    return merge(p); };
  // ── برج دائري (محلي r≈2.66) بقبة صغيرة ──
  const towerR = () => { const p = [];
    p.push([new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.45, 2.6, 15.6, 18).translate(0, 7.8, 0), 0], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.7, 2.78, 1.5, 18).translate(0, 0.75, 0), 1], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.88, 2.6, 1.0, 18).translate(0, 16.0, 0), 1]);
    ring(p, 2.7, 16.5, 12, 0.62, 0.9, 0.34);
    p.push([new THREE.CylinderGeometry(2.2, 2.2, 0.9, 18).translate(0, 16.95, 0), 1], [new THREE.SphereGeometry(2.2, 18, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2).scale(1, 1.08, 1).translate(0, 17.4, 0), 0], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.07, 0.12, 1.1, 6).translate(0, 20.2, 0), 6], [new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.2, 8, 6).translate(0, 20.8, 0), 6]);
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const a = i * Math.PI / 2 + 0.4; slit(p, Math.cos(a) * 2.47, 6 + (i % 2) * 3.5, Math.sin(a) * 2.47, -a + Math.PI / 2); }
    return merge(p); };
  // ── البوابة: كتلة بفتحة قوس مدبّب (نصف عرض 1.5 محلي = فتحة الاصطدام) + إطار القوس + الأبواب ──
  const A = 1.5, SP = 3.3, AH = 1.75, ARC = arch(A, SP, AH, 10);
  const gateShape = () => { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(-4.3, 0); s.lineTo(-A, 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < ARC.length; i++) s.lineTo(-ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]); for (let i = ARC.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) s.lineTo(ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]);
    s.lineTo(A, 0); s.lineTo(4.3, 0); s.lineTo(4.3, 7.4); s.lineTo(-4.3, 7.4); s.closePath(); return s; };
  const archRing = (w) => { const s = new THREE.Shape(), OUT = arch(A + w, SP, AH + w * 0.9, 10); s.moveTo(-A - w, 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < OUT.length; i++) s.lineTo(-OUT[i][0], OUT[i][1]); for (let i = OUT.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) s.lineTo(OUT[i][0], OUT[i][1]);
    s.lineTo(A + w, 0); s.lineTo(A, 0);
    for (let i = 0; i < ARC.length; i++) s.lineTo(ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]); for (let i = ARC.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) s.lineTo(-ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]);
    s.lineTo(-A, 0); s.closePath(); return s; };
  const leaf = (side) => { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(0, 0); s.lineTo(side * A, 0); s.lineTo(side * A, SP); for (let i = 1; i < ARC.length; i++) s.lineTo(side * ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]); s.lineTo(0, 0); return new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(s, { depth: 0.16, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, -0.08); };
  const doorUV = (g) => { g = g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g; const p = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(p.count * 2); for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) { uv[i * 2] = (p.getX(i) + p.getZ(i)) / 1.5; uv[i * 2 + 1] = p.getY(i) / 2.4; } g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; };
  const gate = (closed) => { const p = [];
    p.push([new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(gateShape(), { depth: 2.9, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, -1.45), 0]);
    for (const z of [1.45, -1.6]) p.push([new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(archRing(0.42), { depth: 0.15, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, z), 1]);
    p.push([box(8.6, 0.3, 3.1, 0, 7.4, 0), 1]); merlons(p, -4.3, 4.3, 7.7, [1.38, -1.38], 1.43, 0.78, 1.0, 0.34);
    p.push([box(8.9, 0.9, 3.25, 0, 0, 0), 1]);
    for (const zz of [1.62, -1.77]) for (const xx of [-3.05, 3.05]) banner(p, xx, 6.9, zz, zz > 0 ? 0 : Math.PI, 1.15, 3.0);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const g = doorUV(leaf(s)); if (closed) p.push([g, 3, true]); else { g.translate(-s * A, 0, 0); g.rotateY(s * 1.62); g.translate(s * A, 0, -0.9); p.push([g, 3, true]); } }
    return merge(p); };
  let G = null;
  const api = { mats: M, get geo() { if (!G) { const t0 = performance.now(); G = { wlS: wall(), wlQ: towerQ(), wlR: towerR(), wlG: gate(false), wlC: gate(true) }; console.info('[v15] السور الجديد: ' + (performance.now() - t0).toFixed(0) + ' م.ث'); } return G; } };
  return api; })();
window.__C15 = C15;

/* ═══ v16 (apply_v16_walls.py): سور المدينة بطراز أسوار القدس ودمشق — حجر كلسي كريمي بكتل كبيرة ومداميك ظاهرة، قاعدة مائلة، شرفات مدرّجة (شُرّافات)، أبراج مربعة بسقّاطات (مشربيات دفاعية) وفتحات رمي، وبوابة بقوس مدبّب بصنج متناوبة (أبلق) وإطار ولوحة كتابة — نفس المواضع والمصادمات ═══ */
(() => { const TL = new THREE.TextureLoader(), V2 = THREE.Vector2, V3 = THREE.Vector3;
  const tx = (p, srgb) => { const t = TL.load(p); t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; t.anisotropy = 4; if (srgb) t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; return t; };
  const cr = tx('landmarks/cream.webp', 1), crn = tx('landmarks/cream_n.webp'), M0 = C15.mats;
  const cvs = (w, h, f) => { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h; f(c.getContext('2d'), w, h); const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; t.anisotropy = 4; return t; };
  const NAVY = '#13285a', GOLD = '#d9b04a', GOLD2 = '#f2d27a';
  const star8 = (g, x, y, r, fill) => { g.beginPath(); for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) { const a = i * Math.PI / 8 - Math.PI / 2, rr = i % 2 ? r * 0.62 : r; g.lineTo(x + Math.cos(a) * rr, y + Math.sin(a) * rr); } g.closePath(); g.fillStyle = fill; g.fill(); };
  const banTex = cvs(256, 512, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = NAVY; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.strokeStyle = GOLD; g.lineWidth = 14; g.strokeRect(10, -10, w - 20, h); g.lineWidth = 4; g.strokeRect(30, 10, w - 60, h - 20);
    for (let y = 40; y < h; y += 46) { g.fillStyle = GOLD; g.save(); g.translate(w / 2, y + 380); g.restore(); }
    star8(g, w / 2, h * 0.42, 74, GOLD); star8(g, w / 2, h * 0.42, 50, NAVY); star8(g, w / 2, h * 0.42, 30, GOLD2);
    g.fillStyle = GOLD; g.beginPath(); g.arc(w / 2, h * 0.17, 34, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); g.fillStyle = NAVY; g.beginPath(); g.arc(w / 2 + 13, h * 0.17 - 6, 29, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill();
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { g.save(); g.translate(w / 2, h * 0.62 + i * 26); g.rotate(Math.PI / 4); g.fillStyle = i % 2 ? GOLD : GOLD2; g.fillRect(-7, -7, 14, 14); g.restore(); } });
  const insTex = cvs(1024, 128, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = NAVY; g.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); g.strokeStyle = GOLD; g.lineWidth = 6; g.strokeRect(6, 6, w - 12, h - 12); g.lineWidth = 2; g.strokeRect(16, 16, w - 32, h - 32);
    let s = 7; const r = () => (s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647; g.strokeStyle = GOLD2; g.lineCap = 'round'; g.lineWidth = 5;
    for (let x = 70; x < w - 70; x += 13 + r() * 10) { const hh = 22 + r() * 58; g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, 98); g.lineTo(x, 98 - hh); g.stroke(); if (r() < 0.45) { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(x, 98); g.quadraticCurveTo(x + 14, 108, x + 26, 92); g.stroke(); } if (r() < 0.25) { g.beginPath(); g.arc(x + 8, 74, 6, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.stroke(); } }
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(60, 100); g.lineTo(w - 60, 100); g.stroke(); star8(g, 38, h / 2, 20, GOLD); star8(g, w - 38, h / 2, 20, GOLD); });
  const rosTex = cvs(256, 256, (g, w, h) => { g.fillStyle = '#e9dfca'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(128, 128, 126, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); g.strokeStyle = '#8a7350'; g.lineWidth = 6; g.beginPath(); g.arc(128, 128, 118, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.stroke();
    for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) { const a = i * Math.PI / 8; g.beginPath(); g.ellipse(128 + Math.cos(a) * 62, 128 + Math.sin(a) * 62, 38, 13, a, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.lineWidth = 4; g.stroke(); }
    star8(g, 128, 128, 52, '#b8913f'); star8(g, 128, 128, 30, '#e9dfca'); g.fillStyle = '#8a7350'; g.beginPath(); g.arc(128, 128, 11, 0, Math.PI * 2); g.fill(); });
  const M = [new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.42, 0.42), color: 0xf1eee8, roughness: 0.88 }),     // 0 حجر كبير
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.5, 0.5), color: 0xd9cfbd, roughness: 0.86 }),               // 1 أحزمة وأطر
    M0[2], M0[3], new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x17140f, roughness: 1 }), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: banTex, side: THREE.DoubleSide, roughness: 0.75 }), M0[6],                                             // 2 قرميد، 3 خشب، 4 فتحات، 5 رايات، 6 ذهب
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.35, 0.35), color: 0x4e4640, roughness: 0.8 }),               // 7 حجر أبلق داكن
    new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: insTex, roughness: 0.6 }), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: rosTex, roughness: 0.8 })];   // 8 لوح الكتابة، 9 وردة                                                                     // 8 لوح كحلي
  const TILE = 5.2, NM = M.length;
  const proj = (geo, tile) => { const g = geo.index ? geo.toNonIndexed() : geo, p = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(p.count * 2), a = new V3(), b = new V3(), c = new V3(), n = new V3();
    for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i += 3) { a.fromBufferAttribute(p, i); b.fromBufferAttribute(p, i + 1); c.fromBufferAttribute(p, i + 2); n.subVectors(c, b).cross(new V3().subVectors(a, b)).normalize();
      const ax = Math.abs(n.x), ay = Math.abs(n.y), az = Math.abs(n.z);
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const q = [a, b, c][k]; let u, v; if (ay >= ax && ay >= az) { u = q.x; v = q.z; } else if (ax >= az) { u = q.z; v = q.y; } else { u = q.x; v = q.y; } uv[(i + k) * 2] = u / tile; uv[(i + k) * 2 + 1] = v / tile; } }
    g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); g.deleteAttribute('normal'); g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; };
  const merge = parts => { const by = Array.from({ length: NM }, () => []); for (const [g, mi, keepUV] of parts) by[mi].push(keepUV ? (g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g) : proj(g, mi === 1 ? 2.2 : TILE));
    let n = 0; for (const L of by) for (const g of L) n += g.attributes.position.count;
    const P = new Float32Array(n * 3), N = new Float32Array(n * 3), U = new Float32Array(n * 2), out = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); let o = 0;
    by.forEach((L, mi) => { const s = o; for (const g of L) { P.set(g.attributes.position.array, o * 3); N.set(g.attributes.normal.array, o * 3); U.set(g.attributes.uv.array, o * 2); o += g.attributes.position.count; } if (o > s) out.addGroup(s, o - s, mi); });
    out.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3)); out.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(N, 3)); out.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(U, 2));
    out.computeBoundingBox(); out.computeBoundingSphere(); out.userData.mat = M; SHARED.add(out); return out; };
  const box = (w, h, d, x, y, z) => new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d).translate(x || 0, (y || 0) + h / 2, z || 0);
  const talus = (w, h, d0, d1, y, w1) => { const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d0), p = g.attributes.position; for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i++) if (p.getY(i) > 0) { p.setZ(i, p.getZ(i) * d1 / d0); if (w1) p.setX(i, p.getX(i) * w1 / w); } g.computeVertexNormals(); return g.translate(0, (y || 0) + h / 2, 0); };
  // شُرّافة مدرّجة (ثلاث درجات) بعرض w

    const cap = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(w * 0.35, w * 0.35, d, 6, 1, false, 0, Math.PI).rotateZ(Math.PI / 2).rotateY(Math.PI / 2).translate(0, 0.76, 0); const tip = new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.07, 0.22, 4).translate(0, 0.76 + w * 0.35 + 0.08, 0); if (ry) { cap.rotateY(ry); tip.rotateY(ry); } p.push([cap.translate(x, y, z), 0], [tip.translate(x, y, z), 0]); };
  const merlRow = (p, x0, x1, y, z, step, w, d) => { const n = Math.max(1, Math.round((x1 - x0) / step)), s = (x1 - x0) / n; for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) merlon(p, x0 + s * (i + 0.5), y, z, w, d); };
  const banner = (p, x, y, z, ry, w, h) => { const s = new THREE.Shape(), t = h * 0.16; s.moveTo(-w / 2, 0); s.lineTo(w / 2, 0); s.lineTo(w / 2, -h); s.lineTo(0, -h + t); s.lineTo(-w / 2, -h); s.closePath();
    const g = new THREE.ShapeGeometry(s).toNonIndexed(), q = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(q.count * 2); for (let i = 0; i < q.count; i++) { uv[i * 2] = q.getX(i) / w + 0.5; uv[i * 2 + 1] = 1 + q.getY(i) / h; }

  const slit = (p, x, y, z, ry, hh) => { const g = box(0.2, hh || 1.0, 0.1); if (ry) g.rotateY(ry); p.push([g.translate(x, y, z), 4]); const c = box(0.5, 0.12, 0.14); if (ry) c.rotateY(ry); p.push([c.translate(x, y + (hh || 1.0), z), 1]); };
  // صف سقّاطات: كوابيل مدرّجة تحت شرفة بارزة على وجه طوله len (محلي x، الوجه نحو +z عند zf)
  const machi = (p, len, y, zf, ry) => { const n = Math.max(3, Math.round(len / 1.1)), s = len / n, add = (g, mi) => { if (ry) g.rotateY(ry); p.push([g, mi]); };

  const A = 1.5, SP = 3.3, AH = 1.75;
  const arch = (a, s, h, n) => { const R = (a * a + h * h) / (2 * a), c = a - R, th = Math.atan2(h, -c), pts = []; for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const t = th * i / n; pts.push([c + R * Math.cos(t), s + R * Math.sin(t)]); } return pts; };
  const ARC = arch(A, SP, AH, 10);
  // ── قطعة السور: نواة ٧م (سطح المشي كالسابق)، قاعدة مائلة، حزامان، ممشى بشرفات مدرّجة على الوجهين ──
  const wall = () => { const p = [];
    p.push([box(8.6, 7.0, 2.3, 0, 0, 0), 0], [talus(8.6, 1.5, 3.2, 2.3, 0), 0], [box(8.6, 0.2, 2.5, 0, 1.5, 0), 1], [box(8.6, 0.18, 2.44, 0, 5.3, 0), 1], [box(8.6, 0.16, 2.56, 0, 6.86, 0), 1]);
    for (const z of [1.0, -1.0]) { p.push([box(8.6, 0.55, 0.36, 0, 7.0, z), 0]); merlRow(p, -4.3, 4.3, 7.55, z, 1.43, 0.9, 0.36); }
    for (const x of [-2.15, 2.15]) slit(p, x, 2.9, 1.16, 0, 1.1);
    return merge(p); };
  // ── برج مربّع بسقّاطات وشرفات مدرّجة ورايات (أبراج البوابات والأبراج الوسطى) ──
  const towerQ = (banners) => { const p = [], hw = 3.3, H = 10.6;
    p.push([box(6.6, H, 6.6), 0], [talus(7.8, 1.8, 7.8, 6.6, 0, 6.6), 0], [box(6.9, 0.22, 6.9, 0, 1.8, 0), 1], [box(6.8, 0.18, 6.8, 0, 6.4, 0), 1]);
    for (let f = 0; f < 4; f++) machi(p, 6.6, H + 0.02, hw, f * Math.PI / 2);
    p.push([box(7.8, 1.15, 7.8, 0, H, 0), 0], [box(8.0, 0.16, 8.0, 0, H + 1.15, 0), 1]);
    for (let f = 0; f < 4; f++) { const sub = []; merlRow(sub, -3.9, 3.9, H + 1.31, 3.72, 1.3, 0.85, 0.34); for (const [g, mi] of sub) { g.rotateY(f * Math.PI / 2); p.push([g, mi]); } }
    for (const [x, z, ry] of [[0, 3.31, 0], [0, -3.31, 0], [3.31, 0, Math.PI / 2], [-3.31, 0, Math.PI / 2]]) { slit(p, x, 3.4, z, ry, 1.2); slit(p, x - (ry ? 0 : 1.6), 7.2, z - (ry ? 1.6 : 0), ry); slit(p, x + (ry ? 0 : 1.6), 7.2, z + (ry ? 1.6 : 0), ry); }
    if (banners) for (const [x, z, ry] of [[0, 3.95, 0], [0, -3.95, Math.PI], [3.95, 0, Math.PI / 2], [-3.95, 0, -Math.PI / 2]]) banner(p, x, H + 0.9, z, ry, 1.6, 4.6);
    return merge(p); };
  // ── بدل البرج الدائري: برج مربّع أعلى (حصن الزاوية) بلا رايات ──
  const towerR = () => { const p = [], hw = 2.75, H = 14.8;
    p.push([box(5.5, H, 5.5), 0], [talus(6.6, 2.0, 6.6, 5.5, 0, 5.5), 0], [box(5.8, 0.22, 5.8, 0, 2.0, 0), 1], [box(5.7, 0.18, 5.7, 0, 8.2, 0), 1]);
    for (let f = 0; f < 4; f++) machi(p, 5.5, H + 0.02, hw, f * Math.PI / 2);
    p.push([box(6.7, 1.15, 6.7, 0, H, 0), 0], [box(6.9, 0.16, 6.9, 0, H + 1.15, 0), 1]);
    for (let f = 0; f < 4; f++) { const sub = []; merlRow(sub, -3.35, 3.35, H + 1.31, 3.17, 1.3, 0.85, 0.34); for (const [g, mi] of sub) { g.rotateY(f * Math.PI / 2); p.push([g, mi]); } }
    for (const [x, z, ry] of [[0, 2.76, 0], [0, -2.76, 0], [2.76, 0, Math.PI / 2], [-2.76, 0, Math.PI / 2]]) { slit(p, x, 4.0, z, ry, 1.2); slit(p, x, 7.8, z, ry, 1.2); slit(p, x, 11.4, z, ry, 1.1); }
    return merge(p); };
  // ── البوابة: قوس مدبّب، صنج متناوبة (أبلق)، إطار مستطيل، لوح كتابة كحلي بحافة ذهبية، سقّاطة فوق القوس، شرفات مدرّجة ──
  const gateShape = () => { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(-4.3, 0); s.lineTo(-A, 0); for (let i = 0; i < ARC.length; i++) s.lineTo(-ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]); for (let i = ARC.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) s.lineTo(ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]);
    s.lineTo(A, 0); s.lineTo(4.3, 0); s.lineTo(4.3, 7.4); s.lineTo(-4.3, 7.4); s.closePath(); return s; };
  const vouss = (p, z, dep) => { const W = 0.55, IN = arch(A, SP, AH, 40), OUT = arch(A + W, SP, AH + W * 0.95, 40), pts = []; // نصف القوس من اليمين للقمة، ثم مرآة
    const full = [...IN.map((q, i) => [q, OUT[i]]), ...IN.slice(0, -1).reverse().map((q, i) => { const o = OUT[OUT.length - 2 - i]; return [[-q[0], q[1]], [-o[0], o[1]]]; })];
    const NV = 15, per = (full.length - 1) / NV; for (let v = 0; v < NV; v++) { const i0 = Math.round(v * per), i1 = Math.round((v + 1) * per), s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(full[i0][0][0], full[i0][0][1]);
      for (let i = i0 + 1; i <= i1; i++) s.lineTo(full[i][0][0], full[i][0][1]); for (let i = i1; i >= i0; i--) s.lineTo(full[i][1][0], full[i][1][1]); s.closePath();
      const g = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(s, { depth: dep, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, z); p.push([g, v % 2 ? 7 : 0]); }
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { p.push([box(W + 0.1, 0.5, dep + 0.04, sx * (A + W / 2), SP - 0.5, z + dep / 2), 1]); p.push([box(W + 0.02, SP - 0.5, dep, sx * (A + W / 2), 0, z + dep / 2), 0]); } };
  const leaf = side => { const s = new THREE.Shape(); s.moveTo(0, 0); s.lineTo(side * A, 0); s.lineTo(side * A, SP); for (let i = 1; i < ARC.length; i++) s.lineTo(side * ARC[i][0], ARC[i][1]); s.lineTo(0, 0); return new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(s, { depth: 0.16, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, -0.08); };
  const doorUV = g => { g = g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g; const q = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(q.count * 2); for (let i = 0; i < q.count; i++) { uv[i * 2] = (q.getX(i) + q.getZ(i)) / 1.5; uv[i * 2 + 1] = q.getY(i) / 2.4; } g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; };

  // ── v16b: البوابة الكبرى (طراز مملوكي/عثماني): بيشتاق بارز بقوس مدبّب كبير مجوّف، صنج أبلق واضحة، مقرنصات تحت القوس، إطار ألفيز، شريط كتابة، وردتان، مداميك أبلق ──
  const voussG = (p, z, dep, a, s, h, W, NV, mA, mB) => { const IN = arch(a, s, h, 48), OUT = arch(a + W, s, h + W * 0.95, 48);
    const full = [...IN.map((q, i) => [q, OUT[i]]), ...IN.slice(0, -1).reverse().map((q, i) => { const o = OUT[OUT.length - 2 - i]; return [[-q[0], q[1]], [-o[0], o[1]]]; })];
    const per = (full.length - 1) / NV; for (let v = 0; v < NV; v++) { const i0 = Math.round(v * per), i1 = Math.round((v + 1) * per), sh = new THREE.Shape(); sh.moveTo(full[i0][0][0], full[i0][0][1]);
      for (let i = i0 + 1; i <= i1; i++) sh.lineTo(full[i][0][0], full[i][0][1]); for (let i = i1; i >= i0; i--) sh.lineTo(full[i][1][0], full[i][1][1]); sh.closePath();
      p.push([new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sh, { depth: dep, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, z), v % 2 ? mB : mA]); } };
  const A2 = 2.25, S2 = 4.4, H2 = 2.75, PW = 3.25, PH = 9.7, PD = 0.8, ARC2 = arch(A2, S2, H2, 24);
  const portal = (p, dir) => { const z0 = dir > 0 ? 1.45 : -1.45 - PD, zf = dir > 0 ? 1.45 + PD : -1.45 - PD, fo = dir > 0 ? zf + 0.005 : zf - 0.005, sgn = dir;
    const sh = new THREE.Shape(); sh.moveTo(-PW, 0); sh.lineTo(-A2, 0); for (let i = 0; i < ARC2.length; i++) sh.lineTo(-ARC2[i][0], ARC2[i][1]); for (let i = ARC2.length - 2; i >= 0; i--) sh.lineTo(ARC2[i][0], ARC2[i][1]); sh.lineTo(A2, 0); sh.lineTo(PW, 0); sh.lineTo(PW, PH); sh.lineTo(-PW, PH); sh.closePath();
    p.push([new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(sh, { depth: PD, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, z0), 0]);
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) p.push([talus(1.0, 1.1, PD + 0.5, PD, 0).translate(sx * (PW - 0.5), 0, z0 + PD / 2 + (dir > 0 ? 0.25 : -0.25)), 0]);
    voussG(p, dir > 0 ? zf : zf - 0.12, 0.12, A2 - 0.02, S2, H2, 0.62, 19, 0, 7);                                                   // صنج أبلق
    for (const y of [1.15, 2.3, 3.45]) for (const sx of [-1, 1]) p.push([box(PW - A2 - 0.02, 0.42, 0.04, sx * (A2 + (PW - A2) / 2), y, fo), 7]);   // مداميك داكنة
    const ft = S2 + H2 + 0.95, fx = A2 + 0.82;                                                                                     // ألفيز
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) p.push([box(0.18, ft, 0.1, sx * fx, 0, fo + sgn * 0.03), 1]); p.push([box(2 * fx + 0.18, 0.18, 0.1, 0, ft, fo + sgn * 0.03), 1]);
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) { const r = new THREE.CircleGeometry(0.36, 24).translate(sx * (A2 + 0.42), S2 + H2 * 0.62, 0); if (dir < 0) r.rotateY(Math.PI); r.translate(0, 0, dir > 0 ? fo + 0.04 : -(-fo + 0.04) ); p.push([r, 9, true]);
      p.push([new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.37, 0.04, 6, 24).translate(sx * (A2 + 0.42), S2 + H2 * 0.62, fo + sgn * 0.04), 6]); }
    const ins = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(2 * PW - 0.7, 0.72).translate(0, ft + 0.62, 0); if (dir < 0) ins.rotateY(Math.PI); ins.translate(0, 0, fo + sgn * 0.02); p.push([ins, 8, true]);     // شريط الكتابة
    p.push([box(2 * PW - 0.5, 0.07, 0.07, 0, ft + 0.22, fo + sgn * 0.04), 6], [box(2 * PW - 0.5, 0.07, 0.07, 0, ft + 0.99, fo + sgn * 0.04), 6]);
    p.push([box(2 * PW + 0.3, 0.22, PD + 0.3, 0, PH, z0 + PD / 2), 1], [box(2 * PW + 0.1, 0.16, PD + 0.1, 0, PH - 0.3, z0 + PD / 2), 7]);
    merlRow(p, -PW, PW, PH + 0.22, z0 + PD / 2, 1.08, 0.78, 0.4);
    // مقرنصات: ثلاث طبقات من الخلايا المعلّقة بين قمة باب القوس وقمة التجويف، كل طبقة أبرز من التي تحتها
    const back = dir > 0 ? 1.45 : -1.45, cellW = 0.34;
    [[5.15, 1.95], [5.6, 1.6], [6.05, 1.2], [6.5, 0.75]].forEach(([y, hw], k) => { const n = Math.max(2, Math.round(2 * hw / cellW)), s = 2 * hw / n, dep = 0.16 + 0.15 * k;
      p.push([box(2 * hw, 0.08, dep + 0.04, 0, y + 0.36, back + sgn * (dep + 0.04) / 2), 1]);
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const x = -hw + s * (i + 0.5) + (k % 2 ? s * 0.25 : 0); if (Math.abs(x) > hw - s * 0.3) continue;
        p.push([box(s * 0.86, 0.34, dep, x, y, back + sgn * dep / 2), (i + k) % 3 === 0 ? 7 : 1]);
        p.push([new THREE.ConeGeometry(s * 0.5, 0.24, 4).rotateX(Math.PI).rotateY(Math.PI / 4).translate(x, y - 0.12, back + sgn * dep * 0.6), 1]); } });
  };
  const gate = closed => { const p = [];
    p.push([new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(gateShape(), { depth: 2.9, bevelEnabled: false }).translate(0, 0, -1.45), 0]);
    for (const sx of [-1, 1]) p.push([talus(1.7, 1.2, 3.5, 2.9, 0).translate(sx * 3.45, 0, 0), 0]);
    for (const [z, dir] of [[1.45, 1], [-1.45, -1]]) { vouss(p, dir > 0 ? z : z - 0.16, 0.16);
      portal(p, dir); }
    p.push([box(8.6, 0.3, 3.1, 0, 7.4, 0), 1]); for (const z of [1.38, -1.38]) merlRow(p, -4.3, 4.3, 7.7, z, 1.43, 0.9, 0.34);
    for (const zz of [1.62, -1.77]) for (const xx of [-3.8, 3.8]) banner(p, xx, 6.9, zz, zz > 0 ? 0 : Math.PI, 0.9, 3.0);
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const g = doorUV(leaf(s)); if (closed) p.push([g, 3, true]); else { g.translate(-s * A, 0, 0); g.rotateY(s * 1.62); g.translate(s * A, 0, -0.9); p.push([g, 3, true]); } }
    return merge(p); };
  let G = null; C15.mats = M;
  Object.defineProperty(C15, 'geo', { configurable: true, get() { if (!G) { const t0 = performance.now(); G = { wlS: wall(), wlQ: towerQ(true), wlR: towerR(), wlG: gate(false), wlC: gate(true) }; console.info('[v16] السور الجديد (طراز القدس/دمشق): ' + (performance.now() - t0).toFixed(0) + ' م.ث', JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(G).map(([k, g]) => [k, (g.index ? g.index.count : g.attributes.position.count) / 3])))); } return G; } });
})();

/* ═══ v15: محلات السوق: كل الأكشاك الخشبية القديمة صارت مباني محلات/بيوت من الستايل الجديد (مصغّرة لتناسب صف السوق) ═══ */
const STALL15 = { tools: ['mixSmith', 0.74], weapon: ['mixSmith', 0.74], general: ['mixH4', 0.88], pharm: ['mixH5', 0.8], bakery: ['mixFood', 0.68], cloth: ['mixH4', 0.88], spice: ['mixFood', 0.68] };
function stallRep15(type) { const k0 = SHOP_REP[type]; if (k0 && MODELS[k0] && MODELS[k0].house) return MODELS[k0];
  const q = STALL15[type], M = q && MODELS[q[0]]; if (!M || !M.house) return null; return Object.assign({}, M, { w: M.w * q[1], d: M.d * q[1], h: M.h * q[1], k15: q[1] }); }

/* ═══ v16 (apply_v16_hill.py): تل القصر — ثلاث مصاطب بجدران حجر كلسي كريمي (كتل كبيرة) وأفاريز وأعمدة جدارية ودرابزين، ودرج كبير بثلاثة أشواط ومساطح، أعمدة بداية ذهبية وفوانيس وأحواض زهر ═══ */
const V16 = (() => { const TL = new THREE.TextureLoader(), V2 = THREE.Vector2;
  const tex = (p, srgb) => { const t = TL.load(p, undefined, undefined, () => console.warn('[v16] لم يُحمَّل ' + p)); t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; t.anisotropy = 4; if (srgb) t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; return t; };
  let MT = null;
  const mats = () => { if (MT) return MT; const cr = tex('landmarks/cream.webp', 1), crn = tex('landmarks/cream_n.webp'), fl = tex('city/flag.webp', 1), fln = tex('city/flag_n.webp');
    MT = [new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.8, 0.8), color: 0xf1eee8, roughness: 0.9 }),          // 0 جدار
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.5, 0.5), color: 0xddd3c2, roughness: 0.84 }),           // 1 أفاريز وقواعد
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: fl, normalMap: fln, normalScale: new V2(0.7, 0.7), color: 0xf3f0ea, roughness: 0.88 }),           // 2 بلاط المصاطب
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: cr, normalMap: crn, normalScale: new V2(0.35, 0.35), color: 0xfaf7f1, roughness: 0.62 }),         // 3 درجات (أفتح وأنعم)
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0xc9a043, roughness: 0.38, metalness: 0.7 }),                                                   // 4 ذهب
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.85 })];                                                               // 5 نبات (ألوان رؤوس)
    return MT; };
  // دمج قطع بمواد متعددة في هندسة واحدة بمجموعات (رسمة لكل مادة) — UV مسقطة حسب الوجه بالإحداثيات المحلية (القطع مكتوبة بإحداثيات المدينة)
  const V3 = THREE.Vector3, _a = new V3(), _b = new V3(), _c = new V3(), _n = new V3();
  const proj = (g, tile) => { g = g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g; const p = g.attributes.position, uv = new Float32Array(p.count * 2);
    for (let i = 0; i < p.count; i += 3) { _a.fromBufferAttribute(p, i); _b.fromBufferAttribute(p, i + 1); _c.fromBufferAttribute(p, i + 2); _n.subVectors(_c, _b).cross(new V3().subVectors(_a, _b)).normalize();
      const ax = Math.abs(_n.x), ay = Math.abs(_n.y), az = Math.abs(_n.z);
      for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const q = [_a, _b, _c][k]; let u, v; if (ay >= ax && ay >= az) { u = q.x; v = q.z; } else if (ax >= az) { u = q.z; v = q.y; } else { u = q.x; v = q.y; } uv[(i + k) * 2] = u / tile; uv[(i + k) * 2 + 1] = v / tile; } }
    g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(uv, 2)); if (!g.attributes.normal) g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; };
  const merge = (parts, nm) => { const by = Array.from({ length: nm }, () => []); for (const [g, mi, keep] of parts) { let q = g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g; if (!keep) q = proj(q, mi === 2 ? 3.2 : mi === 3 ? 2.2 : 3.4); if (!q.attributes.color) { const c = new Float32Array(q.attributes.position.count * 3).fill(1); q.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(c, 3)); } by[mi].push(q); }
    let n = 0; for (const L of by) for (const g of L) n += g.attributes.position.count; const P = new Float32Array(n * 3), N = new Float32Array(n * 3), U = new Float32Array(n * 2), C = new Float32Array(n * 3), out = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); let o = 0;
    by.forEach((L, mi) => { const s = o; for (const g of L) { P.set(g.attributes.position.array, o * 3); N.set(g.attributes.normal.array, o * 3); U.set(g.attributes.uv.array, o * 2); C.set(g.attributes.color.array, o * 3); o += g.attributes.position.count; } if (o > s) out.addGroup(s, o - s, mi); });
    out.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3)); out.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(N, 3)); out.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(U, 2)); out.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(C, 3));
    out.computeBoundingBox(); out.computeBoundingSphere(); return out; };
  // شريط أسطواني بارتفاعات لكل زاوية (وجه الجدار) — UV: طول القوس × الارتفاع
  const band = (ox, oz, R, a0, a1, yb, yt, tile, out) => { const n = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(Math.abs(a1 - a0) * R / 1.5)), P = [], U = [], I = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const a = a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / n, x = ox + R * Math.cos(a), z = oz + R * Math.sin(a), y0 = typeof yb === 'function' ? yb(x, z) : yb, y1 = typeof yt === 'function' ? yt(x, z) : yt, u = a * R / tile;
      P.push(x, y0, z, x, y1, z); U.push(u, y0 / tile, u, y1 / tile); if (i) { const k = i * 2; out ? I.push(k - 2, k - 1, k, k, k - 1, k + 1) : I.push(k - 2, k, k - 1, k, k + 1, k - 1); } }
    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(P, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(U, 2)); g.setIndex(I); g.computeVertexNormals(); return g; };
  const annulus = (ox, oz, r0, r1, a0, a1, y, tile, down) => { const n = Math.max(2, Math.ceil(Math.abs(a1 - a0) * r1 / 1.6)), P = [], U = [], I = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= n; i++) { const a = a0 + (a1 - a0) * i / n, c = Math.cos(a), s = Math.sin(a); for (const r of [r0, r1]) { const x = ox + r * c, z = oz + r * s; P.push(x, typeof y === 'function' ? y(x, z) : y, z); U.push(x / tile, z / tile); }
      if (i) { const k = i * 2; I.push(k - 2, k - 1, k, k, k - 1, k + 1); } }
    const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(P, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(U, 2)); g.setIndex(I); g.computeVertexNormals();
    const nn = g.attributes.normal; if ((nn.getY(0) < 0) !== !!down) { for (let i = 0; i < nn.count; i++) nn.setY(i, -nn.getY(i)); const ix = g.index.array; for (let i = 0; i < ix.length; i += 3) { const t = ix[i + 1]; ix[i + 1] = ix[i + 2]; ix[i + 2] = t; } } return g; };
  const box = (w, h, d, x, y, z, ry) => { const g = new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d); if (ry) g.rotateY(ry); return g.translate(x, y + h / 2, z); };
  // درابزين: قاعدة الدرابزين (بالوستر) — مخروط/مخرطة بسيطة (٦ أوجه)
  let BAL = null; const baluster = () => BAL || (BAL = (() => { const pts = [[0.13, 0], [0.13, 0.08], [0.07, 0.12], [0.1, 0.32], [0.05, 0.52], [0.075, 0.62], [0.11, 0.66], [0.11, 0.72]].map(q => new THREE.Vector2(q[0], q[1]));
    const g = proj(new THREE.LatheGeometry(pts, 6), 2.2); const c = new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 3).fill(1); g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(c, 3)); SHARED.add(g); return g; })());
  let BALL = null; const balLo = () => BALL || (BALL = (() => { const pts = [[0.12, 0], [0.07, 0.12], [0.085, 0.4], [0.06, 0.56], [0.11, 0.68], [0.11, 0.72]].map(q => new THREE.Vector2(q[0], q[1]));
    const g = proj(new THREE.LatheGeometry(pts, 4), 2.2); const c = new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 3).fill(1); g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(c, 3)); SHARED.add(g); return g; })());
  let URN = null; const urn = () => URN || (URN = (() => { const pts = [[0.42, 0], [0.42, 0.12], [0.24, 0.2], [0.22, 0.32], [0.5, 0.55], [0.58, 0.86], [0.52, 0.98], [0.56, 1.04], [0.0, 1.04]].map(q => new THREE.Vector2(q[0], q[1]));
    const parts = [[proj(new THREE.LatheGeometry(pts, 10), 2.2), 1]];
    const bush = [], col = [[0.2, 0.42, 0.14], [0.16, 0.36, 0.12], [0.86, 0.36, 0.52], [0.95, 0.82, 0.92], [0.92, 0.3, 0.32]];
    for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) { const a = i * 2.39, r = i ? 0.28 + 0.12 * (i % 2) : 0, s = i ? 0.3 : 0.42, g = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(s, 0).translate(Math.cos(a) * r, 1.15 + (i ? 0.1 + 0.08 * (i % 3) : 0.22), Math.sin(a) * r), c = new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 3), k = col[i % 2];
      for (let j = 0; j < c.length; j += 3) { c[j] = k[0]; c[j + 1] = k[1]; c[j + 2] = k[2]; } g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(c, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 2), 2)); bush.push([g, 5, true]); }
    for (let i = 0; i < 14; i++) { const a = i * 1.71, r = 0.22 + 0.2 * ((i * 7) % 3) / 2, g = new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.075, 0).translate(Math.cos(a) * r, 1.3 + 0.12 * (i % 3), Math.sin(a) * r), c = new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 3), k = col[2 + i % 3];
      for (let j = 0; j < c.length; j += 3) { c[j] = k[0]; c[j + 1] = k[1]; c[j + 2] = k[2]; } g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(c, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 2), 2)); bush.push([g, 5, true]); }
    const geo = merge([...parts, ...bush], 6); SHARED.add(geo); return geo; })());
  let NEWEL = null; const newel = () => NEWEL || (NEWEL = (() => { const p = [[box(0.86, 0.22, 0.86, 0, 0, 0), 1], [box(0.7, 1.05, 0.7, 0, 0.22, 0), 0], [box(0.9, 0.16, 0.9, 0, 1.27, 0), 1],
      [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.16, 0.24, 0.16, 10).translate(0, 1.51, 0), 4], [new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.22, 12, 8).translate(0, 1.78, 0), 4], [new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.06, 0.26, 8).translate(0, 2.1, 0), 4]];
    const geo = merge(p, 6); SHARED.add(geo); return geo; })());

  function build(L, ctx) { const { cx, cz, H, add, lamps, D2R } = ctx, q = WORLD.hill, T = q.t; if (!T) return; const t0 = performance.now();
    const ox = q.x - cx, oz = q.z - cz, Lv = q.level, H0 = (x, z) => LIB.height(x, z, true), M = mats(), parts = [], PI = Math.PI;
    const LV = [Lv, Lv - 4, Lv - 8], RR = [T.r0, T.r1, T.r2], GW = T.cw + 0.75;   // GW: نصف عرض فتحة الدرج في الدرابزين
    const base = (x, z) => H0(x + cx, z + cz) - 0.7;
    // الزوايا بنظام المدينة المحلي (x = R cos a، z = R sin a): الجنوب a = π/2
    const gapA = R => Math.asin(Math.min(1, GW / R));
    const segs = R => { const g = gapA(R); return [[PI / 2 + g, PI / 2 + 2 * PI - g]]; };            // كل الحلقة ما عدا فتحة الدرج
    const solid = (x, z, w, d, yaw, top) => { const b = topOf(solidBox(x + cx, z + cz, w, d, yaw), top); L.obst.push(b); L.solid.push(b); };
    const balPos = [], urnPos = [], newelPos = [];
    for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) { const R = RR[k], yt = LV[k], yb = k < 2 ? LV[k + 1] : base;
      // وجه الجدار (كامل الحلقة: الدرج يقف أمامه) + قاعدة بارزة + إفريز وغطاء
      parts.push([band(ox, oz, R, 0, 2 * PI, yb, yt - 0.42, 3.4, true), 0, true]);
      parts.push([band(ox, oz, R + 0.22, 0, 2 * PI, k < 2 ? yb : (x, z) => base(x, z), (x, z) => (k < 2 ? yb : base(x, z) + 0.7) + 0.75, 2.6, true), 1, true]);
      parts.push([annulus(ox, oz, R, R + 0.22, 0, 2 * PI, (x, z) => (k < 2 ? yb : base(x, z) + 0.7) + 0.75, 2.6), 1, true]);
      parts.push([band(ox, oz, R + 0.26, 0, 2 * PI, yt - 0.44, yt - 0.14, 2.6, true), 1, true], [annulus(ox, oz, R, R + 0.26, 0, 2 * PI, yt - 0.44, 2.6, true), 1, true]);
      parts.push([band(ox, oz, R + 0.42, 0, 2 * PI, yt - 0.14, yt + 0.06, 2.6, true), 1, true], [annulus(ox, oz, R + 0.26, R + 0.42, 0, 2 * PI, yt - 0.14, 2.6, true), 1, true]);
      // أعمدة جدارية بارزة كل ٩° (بعيداً عن الدرج)
      for (let a = 4.5; a < 360; a += 9) { const ar = a * D2R, x = ox + (R + 0.12) * Math.cos(ar), z = oz + (R + 0.12) * Math.sin(ar); if (Math.sin(ar) > 0 && Math.abs(x - ox) < GW + 1.2) continue;
        const y0 = k < 2 ? yb : base(x, z), g = box(1.05, yt - 0.42 - y0, 0.34, 0, y0, 0); g.rotateY(-ar + PI / 2).translate(x, 0, z); parts.push([g, 1]); }
      // بلاط المصطبة: من الحلقة الأعلى (أو سور القصر للهضبة) حتى حافة الجدار
      const rIn = k === 0 ? 29.4 : RR[k - 1] - 0.05; parts.push([annulus(ox, oz, rIn, R + 0.42, 0, 2 * PI, yt + 0.035, 3.2), 2, true]);
      // الدرابزين على حافة المصطبة (فتحة الدرج جنوباً)
      const Rb = R - 0.38;
