    const mat = atlas().mat; let tris = 0, nm = 0;
    for (const k in CH) { const Bq = CH[k]; if (!Bq.P.length) continue; const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(Bq.P, 3)); g.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(Bq.N, 3)); g.setAttribute('uv', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(Bq.U, 2)); g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(Bq.C, 3));
      g.translate(-cx, 0, -cz); g.computeBoundingSphere(); const m = new THREE.Mesh(g, mat); m.castShadow = true; m.receiveShadow = true; m.userData.v18 = 1; L.det.add(m); tris += Bq.P.length / 9; nm++; if (Bq.R) try { V20F(Bq, m, mat); } catch (e) { console.warn('[v20] far', e); } }
    // ── بيوت السكان (أبواب حقيقية على الأزقة): ~٤٠ بيتاً كما كان عدد السكان سابقاً ──
    homesC.sort((p, q) => Math.atan2(p.u.mid[1] - cz, p.u.mid[0] - cx) - Math.atan2(q.u.mid[1] - cz, q.u.mid[0] - cx)); const NH = 40, step = Math.max(1, homesC.length / NH), CAPS = [2, 3, 3, 2, 4, 3, 2, 3];
    for (let i = 0; i < NH && Math.floor(i * step) < homesC.length; i++) { const { u, d } = homesC[Math.floor(i * step)], door = [d.c[0] + d.n[0] * 1.4, d.c[1] + d.n[1] * 1.4], cap = CAPS[i % CAPS.length];
      L.houses.push({ x: (u.mid[0] + u.bm[0]) / 2, z: (u.mid[1] + u.bm[1]) / 2, door, def: { role: cap >= 4 ? 'بيت كبير' : cap === 3 ? 'بيت متوسط' : 'بيت صغير', w: u.W, d: u.dep, h: u.yT - u.yg, doorSide: '+z', doorDist: u.dep / 2, cap }, key: 'v18', cap, yaw: Math.atan2(d.n[0], d.n[1]), ring: 'ABC'[u.b], T: { W: u.W, D: u.dep } }); }
    L.v18 = { cx, cz, strips, rads, units: live, bays, arches, unitAt };
    ST.build = { units: live.length, dropped: units.length - live.length, alleys: nAl, strips: strips.length, sides: nSide, arches: arches.length, meshes: nm, tris: Math.round(tris), homes: L.houses.filter(h => h.key === 'v18').length, drop: DR, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) };
    console.info('[v18] أزقة وبيوت متلاصقة', JSON.stringify(ST.build)); return ST.build; }

  // ───────── (٤) زينة الأزقة: براميل وجرار وصناديق وفوانيس بحجم حقيقي (×١٫٢٥) على طول الجدران، وبسطات على جانبي الشارع الرئيسي ─────────
  function dress(L, ctx) { const t0 = performance.now(), V = L.v18; if (!V || !window.V17 || !V17.geos) return null; const { cx, cz, H } = ctx, rnd = rs(5151), G = V17.geos(), SC = 1.25;
    const cl = [[], []], stalls = [], lan = [], used = [], arches = V.arches, near = (x, z, r) => used.some(q => Math.hypot(q[0] - x, q[1] - z) < r);
    if (!G.lan) G.lan = V17.lanternGeo();
    const solid = (x, z, w, d, yaw, top) => { const b = topOf(solidBox(x, z, w, d, yaw), top); L.obst.push(b); L.solid.push(b); };
    for (const by of V.bays) { if (by.door) continue; const [bx, bz] = by.c, n = by.n, yaw = Math.atan2(n[0], n[1]), y = H(bx, bz) - 0.02, lw = by.laneW || (by.kind === 'front' ? by.u.laneW : 0);
      if (!lw || lw < 4.6) continue; if (arches.some(a => Math.hypot(a[0] - bx, a[1] - bz) < 4.5)) continue; if (Math.hypot(bx - cx, bz - cz) < 70) continue;
      if (by.isMain) { if (by.len > 2.4 && !near(bx, bz, 4.8) && rnd() < 0.8) { used.push([bx, bz]); /* v35: بسطات الشارع الرئيسي محذوفة */ } continue; }
      const narrow = lw < 5.2, pr = narrow ? 0.36 : by.u.plazaF ? 0.45 : 0.82; if (by.end && rnd() < 0.5) continue;
      if (rnd() < pr && !near(bx, bz, narrow ? 6 : 3.7)) { const k = rnd() < 0.5 ? 0 : 1, lx = -n[1], lz = n[0], ox = lx * 0.3 * SC, oz = lz * 0.3 * SC; cl[k].push([bx - cx, y, bz - cz, yaw, SC]); used.push([bx, bz]);
        solid(bx + ox + n[0] * 0.45 * SC, bz + oz + n[1] * 0.45 * SC, 2.3 * SC, 0.9 * SC, yaw, y + 1.05 * SC); }
      else if (rnd() < 0.3 && !lan.some(q => Math.hypot(q[0] + cx - bx, q[2] + cz - bz) < 6)) lan.push([bx - cx, y, bz - cz, yaw, 1.3]); }
    V17.dyn(L, G.cA, cl[0]); V17.dyn(L, G.cB, cl[1]); V17.dyn(L, G.stall, stalls); V17.dyn(L, G.lan, lan); L.lan35 = lan.map(q => [q[0] + cx, q[2] + cz, q[1], q[3]]); { const mm = []; for (const by of V.bays) { if (!by.isMain || Math.hypot(by.c[0] - cx, by.c[1] - cz) < 60) continue; if (mm.some(q => Math.hypot(q[0] - by.c[0], q[1] - by.c[1]) < 13)) continue; mm.push([by.c[0], by.c[1], H(by.c[0], by.c[1]), Math.atan2(by.n[0], by.n[1])]); } L.main35 = mm; }   /* v35: ضوء دافئ من بيوت الشارع الرئيسي ليلاً */
    ST.dress = { clA: cl[0].length, clB: cl[1].length, stalls: stalls.length, lanterns: lan.length, ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; return ST.dress; }
  // ───────── (٥) ملاحة الناس: شبكة إشغال ١م للمدينة كلها + A* بخلايا ١٫٥م (تمرّ في أزقة ٤٫٨م) ─────────
  function nav(L) { const t0 = performance.now(), V = L.v18; if (!V) return; const n = Math.ceil(2 * (ANX50.on ? 257 : CITY_R) + 24), x0 = V.cx - n / 2, z0 = V.cz - n / 2, a = new Uint8Array(n * n), clr = 0.46;
    for (const o of L.obst) { const e = o[2] + clr, i0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(o[0] - e - x0)), i1 = Math.min(n - 1, Math.ceil(o[0] + e - x0)), j0 = Math.max(0, Math.floor(o[1] - e - z0)), j1 = Math.min(n - 1, Math.ceil(o[1] + e - z0));
      for (let j = j0; j <= j1; j++) for (let i = i0; i <= i1; i++) { const k = j * n + i; if (a[k]) continue; if (sdist(o, x0 + i + 0.5, z0 + j + 0.5) < clr) a[k] = 1; } }
    L.nav18 = { n, x0, z0, a }; ST.nav = Math.round(performance.now() - t0); }
  function astar(x0, z0, x1, z1, ob, ctx) { const L = ctx.L, G = L.nav18, R = 30, N = 61, C = 1.5, NN = N * N, ng = ctx.ng, road = ctx.road;
    const wx = i => x0 + (i - R) * C, wz = j => z0 + (j - R) * C, st = new Uint8Array(NN), HH = new Float32Array(NN), CO = new Float32Array(NN);
    const blocked = k => { if (st[k]) return st[k] === 2; const x = wx(k % N), z = wz((k / N) | 0), h = LIB.height(x, z); HH[k] = h; CO[k] = road ? road(x, z) : 1;
      let b = h < 0.3 || (ng && Math.hypot(x - ng[0], z - ng[1]) < ng[2]) || (ctx.nk && NPCK.sd(x, z) < 0.6);
      if (!b) { const gi = Math.floor(x - G.x0), gj = Math.floor(z - G.z0); if (gi >= 0 && gj >= 0 && gi < G.n && gj < G.n) b = G.a[gj * G.n + gi] === 1;
        else for (const o of ob) { if (Math.abs(x - o[0]) < o[2] + 1 && Math.abs(z - o[1]) < o[2] + 1 && sdist(o, x, z) < 0.9) { b = true; break; } } }
      st[k] = b ? 2 : 1; return b; };
    const cl = q => Math.max(0, Math.min(N - 1, q)), gi = cl(Math.round((x1 - x0) / C) + R), gj = cl(Math.round((z1 - z0) / C) + R), gk = gj * N + gi, inside = Math.abs(x1 - x0) < R * C - 2 && Math.abs(z1 - z0) < R * C - 2;
    const sk = R * N + R, g = new Float32Array(NN).fill(1e9), par = new Int32Array(NN).fill(-1), closed = new Uint8Array(NN), hk = [], hf = [];
    const push = (k, f) => { hk.push(k); hf.push(f); let i = hk.length - 1; while (i > 0) { const p = (i - 1) >> 1; if (hf[p] <= hf[i]) break; let t = hk[p]; hk[p] = hk[i]; hk[i] = t; t = hf[p]; hf[p] = hf[i]; hf[i] = t; i = p; } };
    const pop = () => { const k = hk[0], lk = hk.pop(), lf = hf.pop(); if (hk.length) { hk[0] = lk; hf[0] = lf; let i = 0; for (;;) { const l = 2 * i + 1, r = l + 1; let m = i; if (l < hk.length && hf[l] < hf[m]) m = l; if (r < hk.length && hf[r] < hf[m]) m = r; if (m === i) break; let t = hk[m]; hk[m] = hk[i]; hk[i] = t; t = hf[m]; hf[m] = hf[i]; hf[i] = t; i = m; } } return k; };
    const hz = k => { const dx = Math.abs(k % N - gi), dz = Math.abs(((k / N) | 0) - gj); return Math.max(dx, dz) + 0.414 * Math.min(dx, dz); };
    const mk = k => { const p = []; for (let c = k; c !== -1 && c !== sk; c = par[c]) p.push([wx(c % N), wz((c / N) | 0)]); p.reverse(); return p; };
    blocked(sk); g[sk] = 0; push(sk, hz(sk)); let best = sk, bd = 1e9, it = 0;
    while (hk.length && it++ < 6000) { const k = pop(); if (closed[k]) continue; closed[k] = 1;
      if (k === gk && inside) { const p = mk(k); if (!p.length) p.push([x1, z1]); else p[p.length - 1] = [x1, z1]; return p; }
      const td = Math.hypot(wx(k % N) - x1, wz((k / N) | 0) - z1); if (td < bd) { bd = td; best = k; }
      const i = k % N, j = (k / N) | 0;
      for (let di = -1; di <= 1; di++) for (let dj = -1; dj <= 1; dj++) { if (!di && !dj) continue; const ni = i + di, nj = j + dj; if (ni < 0 || nj < 0 || ni >= N || nj >= N) continue; const nk = nj * N + ni;
        if (closed[nk]) continue; if (blocked(nk) && nk !== gk) continue; if (di && dj && (blocked(j * N + ni) || blocked(nj * N + i))) continue; if (Math.abs(HH[nk] - HH[k]) > 1.4) continue;
        const ngv = g[k] + (di && dj ? 1.414 : 1) * (CO[nk] || 1) * (dj < 0 && ctx.nk ? NPCK.upCost(wx(ni), wz(nj)) : 1) + Math.abs(HH[nk] - HH[k]) * 0.5; if (ngv < g[nk]) { g[nk] = ngv; par[nk] = k; push(nk, ngv + hz(nk)); } } }
    if (inside) return null; if (best === sk) return null; const p = mk(best); return p.length ? p : null; }

  // ── للبعيدين (غير مرئيين): خطوة جشعة على شبكة الملاحة بدل الخط المستقيم عبر البيوت، وفكّ أي عالق داخل جدار ──
  function blk(L, x, z) { const G = L.nav18; if (!G) return false; const i = Math.floor(x - G.x0), j = Math.floor(z - G.z0); if (i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= G.n || j >= G.n) return false; return G.a[j * G.n + i] === 1; }
  function inside(L, x, z, r) { for (const o of L.solid) { if (Math.abs(x - o[0]) > o[2] + r || Math.abs(z - o[1]) > o[2] + r) continue; if (sdist(o, x, z) < r) return true; } return false; }
  function unstick(n, L) { if (!blk(L, n.x, n.z) || !inside(L, n.x, n.z, 0.05)) return false; const G = L.nav18, i0 = Math.floor(n.x - G.x0), j0 = Math.floor(n.z - G.z0);
    for (let r = 1; r <= 10; r++) { let bd = 1e9, bi = -1, bj = -1; for (let dj = -r; dj <= r; dj++) for (let di = -r; di <= r; di++) { if (Math.max(Math.abs(di), Math.abs(dj)) !== r) continue; const i = i0 + di, j = j0 + dj; if (i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= G.n || j >= G.n || G.a[j * G.n + i]) continue; const d = di * di + dj * dj; if (d < bd) { bd = d; bi = i; bj = j; } }
      if (bi >= 0) { n.x = G.x0 + bi + 0.5; n.z = G.z0 + bj + 0.5; n.path = null; ST.unstick = (ST.unstick || 0) + 1; return true; } } return false; }
  function farMove(n, L, gx, gz, s) { let left = s; n.fs = n.fs || (Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : -1);
    while (left > 1e-3) { const st = Math.min(1, left), dx = gx - n.x, dz = gz - n.z, dl = Math.hypot(dx, dz); if (dl < 0.5) break; const a0 = Math.atan2(dx, dz); let moved = false;
      for (const da of [0, 0.6, -0.6, 1.2, -1.2, 1.9, -1.9, 2.6, -2.6]) { const a = a0 + da * n.fs, nx = n.x + Math.sin(a) * st, nz = n.z + Math.cos(a) * st; if (blk(L, nx, nz)) continue; n.x = nx; n.z = nz; moved = true; break; }
      if (!moved) { n.fs = -n.fs; break; } left -= st; } }
  return { build, dress, nav, astar, atlas, ST, T, tuv, farMove, unstick, blk }; })();
window.V18 = V18;

const DENSE16 = (() => { let ROOF = null;
  const roofGeo = () => ROOF || (ROOF = (() => { const mk = (parts) => { const gs = parts.map(([g, c]) => { g = g.index ? g.toNonIndexed() : g; const col = new Float32Array(g.attributes.position.count * 3); for (let i = 0; i < col.length; i += 3) { col[i] = c[0]; col[i + 1] = c[1]; col[i + 2] = c[2]; } g.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(col, 3)); g.deleteAttribute('uv'); return g; });
      let n = 0; gs.forEach(g => n += g.attributes.position.count); const P = new Float32Array(n * 3), N = new Float32Array(n * 3), C = new Float32Array(n * 3); let o = 0; gs.forEach(g => { P.set(g.attributes.position.array, o * 3); N.set(g.attributes.normal.array, o * 3); C.set(g.attributes.color.array, o * 3); o += g.attributes.position.count; });
      const out = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); out.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3)); out.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(N, 3)); out.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(C, 3)); out.computeBoundingSphere(); SHARED.add(out); return out; };
    const tank = mk([[new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.55, 0.55, 1.0, 10).translate(0, 1.0, 0), [0.22, 0.22, 0.24]], [new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.58, 0.58, 0.08, 10).translate(0, 1.54, 0), [0.3, 0.3, 0.32]], ...[[-0.35, -0.35], [0.35, -0.35], [-0.35, 0.35], [0.35, 0.35]].map(([x, z]) => [new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.07, 0.5, 0.07).translate(x, 0.25, z), [0.25, 0.22, 0.2]])]);
    const dome = mk([[new THREE.CylinderGeometry(1.15, 1.2, 0.5, 14).translate(0, 0.25, 0), [0.93, 0.91, 0.87]], [new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.15, 14, 7, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI / 2).translate(0, 0.5, 0), [0.95, 0.94, 0.91]], [new THREE.ConeGeometry(0.06, 0.4, 6).translate(0, 1.85, 0), [0.75, 0.6, 0.25]]]);
    const awn = mk([[new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.4, 0.06, 1.1).rotateX(0.32).translate(0, 0, 0.5), [0.13, 0.22, 0.45]], [new THREE.BoxGeometry(2.4, 0.22, 0.04).translate(0, -0.27, 1.03), [0.85, 0.7, 0.3]]]);
    const line = mk([[new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.02, 0.02, 1).translate(0, 0, 0), [0.3, 0.28, 0.25]], ...[-0.35, -0.12, 0.12, 0.35].map((z, i) => [new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.02, 0.45, 0.22).translate(0, -0.24, z), [[0.92, 0.9, 0.85], [0.75, 0.3, 0.28], [0.2, 0.35, 0.6], [0.85, 0.75, 0.45]][i]])]);
    return { tank, dome, awn, line }; })());
  function build(L, ctx) { const t0 = performance.now(), { cx, cz, H, blockedAt, lamps, D2R, pcx, pcz } = ctx, PI = Math.PI;
    const MK = ['mixH1', 'mixH2', 'mixH4', 'mixH5', 'mixFood', 'mixSmith'].filter(k => MODELS[k] && MODELS[k].house), WT = { mixH1: 0.25, mixH2: 0.22, mixH4: 0.2, mixH5: 0.23, mixFood: 0.06, mixSmith: 0.04 };
    if (!MK.length || !window.__LOD16_ON) return; let seed = 16016; const rnd = () => (seed = (seed * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647;
    const pick = () => { let t = rnd() * MK.reduce((a, k) => a + WT[k], 0); for (const k of MK) { t -= WT[k]; if (t <= 0) return k; } return MK[0]; };
    const RAD = [0, 45, 90, 135, 180, 225, 270, 315], WIDE = { 270: 5.8 }, CR = CITY_R, MQ = [cx + 92.5 * Math.cos(165 * D2R), cz - 92.5 * Math.sin(165 * D2R)];
    const gates = (L.gates || []).map(g => [g.x, g.z]);
    // شبكة إشغال ١م: 1 = ممنوع/مبنى، 2 = فناء أمام باب (زقاق مشترك)
    const N = 2 * Math.ceil(CR) + 2, G0 = Math.ceil(CR) + 1, OC = new Uint8Array(N * N), cell = (x, z) => { const i = Math.floor(x - cx) + G0, j = Math.floor(z - cz) + G0; return i < 0 || j < 0 || i >= N || j >= N ? -1 : j * N + i; };
    const RESx = (ctx.RES || []).filter(q => !q.r);   // مناطق محجوزة (السوق، الإسطبلات، ساحات التدريب) — التل يُفحص بنصف قطره الجديد
    const resHit = (x, z, m) => RESx.some(q => Math.abs(x - q.x) < q.hx + m && Math.abs(z - q.z) < q.hz + m);
    const blockedBase = (x, z) => { const dx = x - cx, dz = z - cz, R = Math.hypot(dx, dz); if (R > CR - 10.5 || R < 40) return 1;
      if (resHit(x, z, 1.5) || Math.hypot(x - MQ[0], z - MQ[1]) < 25 || Math.hypot(x - pcx, z - pcz) < WORLD.hill.t.r2 + 5 || (Math.abs(x - pcx) < 9 && z > pcz && z < pcz + WORLD.hill.t.foot + 14)) return 1;
      for (const g of gates) if (Math.hypot(x - g[0], z - g[1]) < 24) return 1;
      if (Math.abs(R - 72) < 4.2 || Math.abs(R - 118) < 3.7) return 2;                                   // الطرق: يُسمح عليها بفناء الباب فقط
      const ang = (Math.atan2(-dz, dx) / D2R + 360) % 360;
      for (const a of RAD) { const da = Math.abs(((ang - a + 540) % 360) - 180) * D2R; if (da < PI / 2 && Math.abs(Math.sin(da)) * R < (WIDE[a] || 3.8)) return 2; }
      return 0; };
    for (let j = 0; j < N; j++) for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) OC[j * N + i] = blockedBase(cx + i - G0 + 0.5, cz + j - G0 + 0.5);
    const disc = (x, z, r, v) => { for (let a = -r; a <= r; a++) for (let b = -r; b <= r; b++) if (a * a + b * b <= r * r + 0.5) { const c = cell(x + a, z + b); if (c >= 0 && OC[c] < v) OC[c] = v; } };
    for (const q of lamps) disc(q[0], q[1], 2, 1); for (const q of Object.values(L.poi || {}).flat()) disc(q.x, q.z, 2, 1); for (const q of L.houses) if (q.door) disc(q.door[0], q.door[1], 3, 1);
    // كل المصادمات الموجودة (بيوت، آبار، دكك، أسوار، التل...) بهامش
    for (const s of L.solid) { const e = Math.ceil(s[2] + 1); for (let a = -e; a <= e; a++) for (let b = -e; b <= e; b++) { const x = s[0] + a, z = s[1] + b; if (sdist(s, x, z) < 0.35) { const c = cell(x, z); if (c >= 0) OC[c] = 1; } } }
    // أزقة عرضية متعرجة: لكل حلقة سماكتها ~١٤م إزاحة زاوية مختلفة، وساحات صغيرة
    for (let rb = 40; rb < CR; rb += 14) { const off = rnd() * 30, stp = 24 + rnd() * 10; for (let a = off; a < 360; a += stp) { const ar = a * D2R; for (let rr = rb; rr < rb + 14; rr += 0.7) { const t = (rr - rb) / 14, aw = ar + (t - 0.5) * 0.06; disc(cx + rr * Math.cos(aw), cz - rr * Math.sin(aw), 1, 1); } } }
    for (let k = 0; k < 26; k++) { const a = rnd() * 2 * PI, rr = 50 + rnd() * 80; disc(cx + rr * Math.cos(a), cz - rr * Math.sin(a), 3, 1); }
    const placed = [], out = [], props = { tank: [], dome: [], awn: [], line: [] };
    const foot = (x, z, w, d, yaw, yd, test) => { // test: 0 = فحص، 1 = تعليم. البصمة بهامش ٠٫١ والفناء أمام الباب بعمق yd
      const c = Math.cos(yaw), s = Math.sin(yaw), hw = w / 2, hd = d / 2;
      for (let u = -hw; u <= hw + 0.01; u += 0.7) for (let v = -hd; v <= hd + yd + 0.01; v += 0.7) { const uu = Math.min(u, hw), vv = Math.min(v, hd + yd), x1 = x + uu * c + vv * s, z1 = z - uu * s + vv * c, ce = cell(x1, z1), yard = vv > hd;
        if (ce < 0) return false; if (!test) { if (yard ? OC[ce] === 1 : OC[ce] !== 0) return false; } else if (yard) { if (!OC[ce]) OC[ce] = 2; } else OC[ce] = 1; } return true; };
    let tries = 0;
    for (const [SMIN, SRNG, YD, STEP] of [[0.9, 0.25, 2.2, 1.0], [0.74, 0.14, 1.6, 0.6]]) {   // جولة ثانية أصغر تملأ الفجوات
    for (let rr = 44; rr < CR - 11; rr += STEP) { const n = Math.floor(2 * PI * rr / STEP), a0 = rnd() * 2 * PI;
      for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) { const ar = a0 + i / n * 2 * PI, x = cx + rr * Math.cos(ar), z = cz - rr * Math.sin(ar), c0 = cell(x, z); if (c0 < 0 || OC[c0]) continue;
        const k = pick(), M = MODELS[k], s = SMIN + rnd() * SRNG, w = M.w * s, d = M.d * s, inward = rnd() < 0.5; tries++;
        for (const fw of inward ? [0, PI, PI / 2, -PI / 2] : [PI, 0, -PI / 2, PI / 2]) { const yaw = Math.atan2(cx - x, cz - z) + fw + (rnd() - 0.5) * 0.05;
          if (foot(x, z, w, d, yaw, YD, 0)) { foot(x, z, w, d, yaw, YD, 1); const sy = s * (0.95 + rnd() * 0.35); out.push({ k, x, z, yaw, s, sy });
            const top = H(x, z) - 0.1 + M.h * sy, fr = rnd() - 0.5, pr = rnd(), fx = Math.cos(yaw), fz = -Math.sin(yaw), bx = Math.sin(yaw), bz = Math.cos(yaw);
            if (pr < 0.3) props.tank.push([x + fx * w * 0.22 * fr - bx * d * 0.2, 0, z + fz * w * 0.22 * fr - bz * d * 0.2, yaw, 1, out.length - 1]);
            else if (pr < 0.38) props.dome.push([x - bx * d * 0.12, 0, z - bz * d * 0.12, 0, 0.8 + rnd() * 0.4, out.length - 1]);
            if (rnd() < 0.35) props.awn.push([x + bx * (d / 2 + 0.05), H(x, z) + 2.5, z + bz * (d / 2 + 0.05), yaw, 1]);
            break; } } } } }
    try { L.v17fronts = V17.snap(out, props, { cx, cz, OC, cell }); } catch (e) { console.warn('[v17] snap', e); }
    const CNT = { tries }; L.cnt16 = CNT; if (/occ16/.test(location.search)) window.__OCC16 = { N, OC: Array.from(OC) };
    // البيوت: نسخ عبر LOD16 + مصادم بسطح (يمكن الوقوف على السطح)
    const m4 = new THREE.Matrix4(), Q = new THREE.Quaternion(), P3 = new THREE.Vector3(), S3 = new THREE.Vector3(), UP = new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0);
    for (const q of out) { const M = MODELS[q.k], y = H(q.x, q.z) - 0.1; m4.compose(P3.set(q.x - cx, y, q.z - cz), Q.setFromAxisAngle(UP, q.yaw), S3.set(q.s * (q.mir ? -1 : 1), q.sy, q.s));
      const h1 = Math.abs(Math.sin(q.x * 12.9898 + q.z * 78.233) * 43758.5453) % 1, h2 = Math.abs(Math.sin(q.x * 39.346 + q.z * 11.135) * 24634.6345) % 1;
      LOD16.addInst(L, M.geo, M.mat, M, 1, m4.clone(), [0.92 + 0.12 * h1, 0.92 + 0.1 * h2, 0.9 + 0.1 * (1 - h1)]);
      const b = topOf(solidBox(q.x, q.z, M.w * q.s * 0.94, M.d * q.s * 0.94, q.yaw), y + M.h * q.sy); L.obst.push(b); L.solid.push(b); q.m4 = m4.clone(); }
    // تفاصيل السطح على السطح الحقيقي فقط (شعاع للأسفل على هندسة البيت؛ سطح أفقي وإلا تُحذف — لا شيء يطفو)
    { const rc = new THREE.Raycaster(), tm = new THREE.Mesh(), dn = new THREE.Vector3(0, -1, 0), o3 = new THREE.Vector3(), nm = new THREE.Matrix3();
      for (const nmK of ['tank', 'dome']) props[nmK] = props[nmK].filter(p => { const q = out[p[5]]; if (!q) return false; const M = MODELS[q.k]; tm.geometry = M.geo; tm.material = M.mat; tm.matrixAutoUpdate = false; tm.matrix.copy(q.m4); tm.matrixWorld.copy(q.m4);
        const rr = p[4] * (nmK === 'dome' ? 1.15 : 0.6), y0 = H(q.x, q.z) - 0.1, hs = [];
        for (const [ox, oz] of [[0, 0], [rr, 0], [-rr, 0], [0, rr], [0, -rr]]) { rc.set(o3.set(p[0] - cx + ox, y0 + M.h * q.sy + 6, p[2] - cz + oz), dn); const hit = rc.intersectObject(tm, false)[0]; if (!hit || !hit.face) return false; nm.getNormalMatrix(q.m4); const ny = hit.face.normal.clone().applyMatrix3(nm).normalize().y; if (ny < 0.85) return false; hs.push(hit.point.y); }
        if (Math.max(...hs) - Math.min(...hs) > 0.12 || hs[0] < y0 + M.h * q.sy * 0.45) return false; p[1] = hs[0] - 0.02; return true; }); }
    // تفاصيل الأسطح (نسخ مكررة؛ مادة بألوان الرؤوس)
    const RG = roofGeo(), RM = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.8 });
    for (const [nm, arr] of Object.entries(props)) { if (!arr.length) continue; const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(RG[nm], RM, arr.length);
      arr.forEach((p, i) => im.setMatrixAt(i, m4.compose(P3.set(p[0] - cx, p[1], p[2] - cz), Q.setFromAxisAngle(UP, p[3]), S3.set(p[4], p[4], p[4])))); im.castShadow = window.__QUALITY === 'high'; im.receiveShadow = true; im.computeBoundingSphere(); L.det.add(im); }
    L.dense16 = { cnt: CNT, n: out.length, props: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(props).map(([k, v]) => [k, v.length])), ms: Math.round(performance.now() - t0) }; console.info('[v16] تكثيف المدينة', JSON.stringify(L.dense16)); }
  return { build }; })();

/* ═══ v16b: حقول طبيعية خارج السور بدل المربعات الصفراء — خطوط زرع (أخضر/ذهبي ناضج/تربة محروثة) بحواف ناعمة وتلوين متفاوت، خامة رسم واحدة لكل نوع ═══ */
const V16FIELD = (() => { const mk = (kind, seed) => { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = 512; const g = c.getContext('2d'); let s = seed; const r = () => (s = (s * 16807) % 2147483647) / 2147483647;
    const P = { green: [[86, 112, 52], [64, 92, 40], [104, 92, 60]], gold: [[196, 168, 92], [170, 140, 70], [122, 100, 62]], earth: [[118, 92, 64], [96, 74, 52], [138, 112, 80]] }[kind];
    g.fillStyle = `rgb(${P[2]})`; g.fillRect(0, 0, 512, 512);
    for (let y = 0; y < 512; y += 8) { const cl = P[(y / 8) % 2 ? 1 : 0]; for (let x = 0; x < 512; x += 4) { const v = 0.82 + r() * 0.3; g.fillStyle = `rgb(${cl.map(q => Math.round(q * v)).join(',')})`; g.fillRect(x, y + 1, 4, 5 + (r() < 0.2 ? 1 : 0)); } }
    for (let i = 0; i < 900; i++) { const v = 0.7 + r() * 0.5, cl = P[(r() * 3) | 0]; g.fillStyle = `rgba(${cl.map(q => Math.round(q * v)).join(',')},0.5)`; g.fillRect(r() * 512, r() * 512, 2 + r() * 6, 2 + r() * 6); }
    for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) { const x = r() * 512, y = r() * 512, rr = 40 + r() * 90, gr = g.createRadialGradient(x, y, 0, x, y, rr); gr.addColorStop(0, `rgba(${P[2]},0.35)`); gr.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); g.fillStyle = gr; g.fillRect(0, 0, 512, 512); }
    const id = g.getImageData(0, 0, 512, 512), d = id.data; for (let y = 0; y < 512; y++) for (let x = 0; x < 512; x++) { const e = Math.min(x, y, 511 - x, 511 - y) / 26 + (r() - 0.5) * 0.5; d[(y * 512 + x) * 4 + 3] = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, e * 255)); } g.putImageData(id, 0, 0);
    const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace; t.anisotropy = 8;
    return new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: t, transparent: true, alphaTest: 0.04, depthWrite: false, roughness: 0.95, polygonOffset: true, polygonOffsetFactor: -2, polygonOffsetUnits: -2 }); };
  let M = null, k = 0; return () => { if (!M) M = [mk('green', 11), mk('gold', 23), mk('earth', 37), mk('green', 41), mk('gold', 53), mk('earth', 67), mk('green', 79)]; return M[k++ % M.length]; }; })();
function buildCity(v) {                                   // المدينة الصغيرة (R=150) بالمخطط: θ من الشرق عكس عقارب الساعة، الشمال z سالب
  const r = rng(v.seed), g = new THREE.Group(), det = new THREE.Group(), farG = new THREE.Group(), cx = v.x, cz = v.z, H = (x, z) => LIB.height(x, z), D2R = PI / 180;
  const P = (rad, deg) => [cx + rad * Math.cos(deg * D2R), cz - rad * Math.sin(deg * D2R)];
  const HL = WORLD.hill, pcx = HL.x, pcz = HL.z, HR = HL.r2, onHill = (x, z, m) => Math.hypot(x - pcx, z - pcz) < HR + (m || 0), PP2 = (rad, deg) => [pcx + rad * Math.cos(deg * D2R), pcz - rad * Math.sin(deg * D2R)];   // v8: القصر على التل شمالاً
  const L = { g, det, farG, houses: [], obst: [], solid: [], npcs: [], work: [], wood: [], stalls: [], gatePos: [], gateFace: [], alarm: 0, ga: 0, animals: [], sus: null, big: true, patrol: [143, 143], gates: [], t0: 0,
    poi: { plaza: [], well: [], bench: [], cross: [], stall: [], field: [], wood: [] }, stat: {} };
  v.live = L; g.add(det, farG); scene.add(g); const tb = performance.now(), cnt = {}, groups = {}, SEC = 8, circ = [], GA = [0, 90, 180, 270];
  const add = (key, x, z, yaw, sx, sy, sz) => { const a = Math.atan2(z - cz, x - cx), sec = ((Math.floor((a + PI) / (2 * PI) * SEC) % SEC) + SEC) % SEC, id = key + '|' + sec;
    (groups[id] = groups[id] || []).push([x, z, yaw, sx, sy === undefined ? sx : sy, sz === undefined ? sx : sz]); cnt[key] = (cnt[key] || 0) + 1; };
  const cTop = (key, x, z, sy, raw) => H(x, z) - 0.1 + (raw ? cityRaw(key).by : geoTop(cityTpl(key))) * sy;   // v10: سطح نسخة المدينة (نفس موضع ومقياس النسخة)
  const chain = (x, z, w, d, yaw, top) => { const b = solidBox(x, z, w, d, yaw, 0.05); if (top !== undefined) b.top = top; L.obst.push(b); L.solid.push(b); return [[x, z, Math.min(w, d) / 2]]; };   // صندوق موجّه بالبصمة الحقيقية
  const put = (key, rad, deg, s, outward, ring) => { if (window.V18 && /^nh/.test(key)) return;      // موضع بالمخطط؛ الباب (+z) نحو المركز أو نحو الخارج
    let yj = 0; if (!LAND[key]) { rj(); rj(); rj(); }   /* v17: صفوف مستقيمة بلا إزاحة عشوائية */
    const D = cDef(key), [x, z] = P(rad, deg), w = D.w * s, d = D.d * s; if (!LAND[key] && blockedAt(x, z, 0.3 * Math.max(D.w, D.d) * s)) return;
    const HR = HREP_MAP[key] ? houseRep(key) : null, yaw = Math.atan2(cx - x, cz - z) + (outward ? PI : 0) + yj, dw = rotL(0, 1, yaw), dd = (HR ? HR.front : D.doorDist) * s, door = [x + dw[0] * (dd + 1.4), z + dw[1] * (dd + 1.4)];
    const M0 = MODELS[key], dR = HR ? HR.d * s : M0 && M0.geo ? (M0.bb.max.z - M0.bb.min.z) * D.w / (M0.bb.max.x - M0.bb.min.x) * s : d;
    add(key, x, z, yaw, s); const hb = topOf(houseBox(cityTpl(key), x, z, yaw, s, 0.02), cTop(key, x, z, s)); L.obst.push(hb); L.solid.push(hb); const cs0 = [[x, z, Math.min(w, dR) / 2]]; L.houses.push({ x, z, door, def: D, key, cap: D.cap || 0, yaw, ring, T: { W: w, D: d } }); if (DBG) circ.push({ cs: cs0, x, z, dw, dd }); };
  const PK = { L: [['nhA', 1.15], ['nhD', 1.2], ['nhB', 1.15], ['nhF', 1.25], ['nhC', 1.2], ['nhE', 1.25]], M: [['nhE', 1.1], ['nhB', 1.1], ['nhF', 1.15], ['nhD', 1.1], ['nhC', 1.1], ['nhA', 1.05]], S: [['nhF', 1.0], ['nhE', 1.05], ['nhC', 1.0], ['nhB', 1.0], ['nhD', 1.0], ['nhA', 0.95]] };
  let ki = 0; const pk = c => PK[c][ki++ % PK[c].length];
  // ── تخطيط v6: سوق بيضاوي جنوب القصر، إسطبلان قرب البوابة الجنوبية، ساحتا تدريب أمام الثكنتين ──
  const MKX = cx, MKZ = cz + 18;                                                         // مركز السوق: ساحة مستطيلة بين الحلقتين ٧٢ و١١٨ جنوب القصر (بلا تماس مع أي طريق مرسوم)
  const STABLE_AT = [[cx - 24, cz + 128], [cx + 24, cz + 128]];   /* v37: الإسطبل v6 أطول (٣٠م): مركزه أقرب للطريق الجنوبي حتى يبقى داخل الحلقة ١٤٣ */
  const BARN_AT = (() => { const th = 242.8, q = P(131, th); return { x: q[0], z: q[1], t: [Math.sin(th * D2R), Math.cos(th * D2R)] }; })();   /* v37: الحظيرة v2 داخل السور قرب الإسطبل الغربي */                          // الإسطبلان بين الحلقتين ١١٨ و١٤٣ على جانبي الطريق الجنوبي
  const YARDS = [19.5, 160.5].map(deg => { const [yx, yz] = P(131, deg); return { x: yx, z: yz, yaw: Math.atan2(cx - yx, cz - yz) }; });   // ساحة تدريب بجانب كل ثكنة
  const RES = [{ x: MKX, z: MKZ, hx: 50, hz: 25, lamp: false }, { x: pcx, z: pcz, r: HR + 8, lamp: true }, ...STABLE_AT.map(q => ({ x: q[0], z: q[1], hx: 16.5, hz: 10, lamp: true })), { x: BARN_AT.x, z: BARN_AT.z, r: 19, lamp: true }, ...YARDS.map(q => ({ x: q.x, z: q.z, hx: 11, hz: 11, lamp: true }))];   // مناطق ممنوع فيها بيوت/أشجار/دكك
  const blockedAt = (x, z, m, lampOnly) => RES.some(q => (!lampOnly || q.lamp) && (q.r ? Math.hypot(x - q.x, z - q.z) < q.r + m : Math.abs(x - q.x) < q.hx + m && Math.abs(z - q.z) < q.hz + m));
  const LAND = { cTemple: 1, cInn: 1, cBarracks: 1, cFort: 1 }, rj = rng(v.seed + 777);   // rj: مولّد مستقل للإزاحة الخفيفة (لا يغيّر عشوائية بقية المدينة)
  // ── القصر (~40×28) وسوره الداخلي r=36 (وحدات الحزمة) بفتحة جنوباً ──
  if (!PAL3D) PMQ.palace(L, { pcx, pcz, cx, cz }); /* PMQ: القصر الجديد (بدل cCastle) */
  const WS = cityRaw('wlS'), NI = Math.round(2 * PI * 36 / (WS.bx * 0.7)); for (let i = 0; i < (PAL3D ? 0 : NI); i++) { const deg = (i + 0.5) / NI * 360; if (Math.abs(((deg - 270 + 540) % 360) - 180) < 8) continue; const [x, z] = PP2(36, deg), ch = 2 * PI * 36 / NI;
    add('wlS', x, z, (deg + 90) * D2R, (ch + 0.15) / WS.bx, 0.7, 0.7); const c = topOf(solidBox(x, z, ch + 0.4, 2.2, (deg + 90) * D2R), cTop('wlS', x, z, 0.7, 1)); L.obst.push(c); L.solid.push(c); }
  // ── الحلقات: أ (r=86)، ب (r=104)، ج (r=132) ──
  for (let k = 0; k < 24; k++) { const deg = k * 15; if (deg % 45 === 0) continue; if (deg === 165) PMQ.mosque(L, RES, { cx, cz }, P(92.5, deg)); /* PMQ: الجامع الجديد (بدل cTemple) */ else if (deg === 15) put('cInn', 86, deg, 0.9, false, 'A'); else { const [key, s] = pk('L'); put(key, 86, deg, s, false, 'A'); } }
  for (let k = 0; k < 24; k++) { const [key, s] = pk('M'); put(key, 104, 7.5 + 15 * k, s, true, 'B'); }
  for (let k = 0; k < 36; k++) { const deg = k * 10, m = deg % 45, dist = Math.min(m, 45 - m); if (dist <= 6) continue;
    if (deg === 10 || deg === 170) put('cBarracks', 132, deg, 1, false, 'C'); else if (deg === 260 || deg === 280) put('cFort', 132, deg, 1, false, 'C'); else { const [key, s] = pk('S'); put(key, 132, deg, s, false, 'C'); } }
  const WELLD = [53, 127, 233, 307];
  for (const deg of WELLD) { const [x, z] = P(122.5, deg), wy = Math.atan2(cx - x, cz - z); add('pWell', x, z, wy, 1.3); const c = topOf(solidBox(x, z, 3.0, 2.6, wy), cTop('pWell', x, z, 1.3, 1)); L.obst.push(c); L.solid.push(c); }
  // ── السور من وحدات الحزمة: ربعان بين البوابات، ١٢ برجاً، وأربع بوابات (برجان ±9.5م، فتحة ~3.9م، تُغلق ليلاً) ──
  const gdeg = d => Math.min(...GA.map(q => Math.abs(((d - q + 540) % 360) - 180)));
  const WSG = 13.7 / CITY_WR * 180 / PI;
  const KS = 2.3, GW = a => (a === 270 ? KS : 1), WSGa = a => (5.5 * GW(a) + 8.2) / CITY_WR * 180 / PI;   // v6: البوابة الجنوبية (270°) أعرض ٢٫٣× (فتحة ≈ ٩ م)؛ الشمالية والشرقية والغربية ثانوية بلا تغيير
  for (const a of GA) { const NQ = CITY2 ? 27 : 19, w0 = WSGa(a), w1 = WSGa((a + 90) % 360), step = (90 - w0 - w1) / NQ; for (let k = 0; k < NQ; k++) { const deg = a + w0 + (k + 0.5) * step, [x, z] = P(CITY_WR, deg), ch = 2 * CITY_WR * Math.sin(step * D2R / 2); if (PAL3D && ANX50.cut(deg)) continue;
    add('wlS', x, z, (deg + 90) * D2R, (ch + 0.25) / WS.bx, 1.3, 1.3); const c = topOf(solidBox(x, z, ch + 0.3, 3.6, (deg + 90) * D2R), cTop('wlS', x, z, 1.3, 1)); L.obst.push(c); L.solid.push(c); } }
  for (let i = 0; i < 12; i++) { const deg = 15 + i * 30, [x, z] = (PAL3D && (deg === 75 || deg === 105)) ? ANX50.tw(deg) : P(CITY_WR, deg), rd = deg % 90 === 45; add(rd ? 'wlR' : 'wlQ', x, z, (deg + 90) * D2R, rd ? 0.85 : 1.1); const c = topOf(rd ? [x, z, 2.5] : solidBox(x, z, 7.6, 7.6, (deg + 90) * D2R), cTop(rd ? 'wlR' : 'wlQ', x, z, rd ? 0.85 : 1.1, 1)); L.obst.push(c); L.solid.push(c); }
  GA.forEach((a, gi) => { const [gx, gz] = (PAL3D && a === 90) ? [cx, cz + ANX50.A.zN] : P(CITY_WR, a), tg = [-Math.sin(a * D2R) * -1, 0]; const tx = -Math.sin(a * D2R), tz = -Math.cos(a * D2R), yaw = (a + 90) * D2R, closeC = [];
    let gmO, gmC;
    for (const s of [-1, 1]) { const kk = GW(a), tw = 5.5 * kk + 4, px = gx + tx * s * tw, pz = gz + tz * s * tw; add('wlQ', px, pz, yaw, 1.1); const c = topOf(solidBox(px, pz, 7.6, 7.6, yaw), cTop('wlQ', px, pz, 1.1, 1)); L.obst.push(c); L.solid.push(c); const f = topOf(solidBox(gx + tx * s * 3.72 * kk, gz + tz * s * 3.72 * kk, 3.55 * kk, 3.6, yaw), cTop('wlG', gx, gz, 1.3, 1)); L.obst.push(f); L.solid.push(f); }
    { const mk = key => { const m = C15.geo[key] ? new THREE.Mesh(C15.geo[key], C15.mats) : new THREE.Mesh(cityRaw(key).geo, HM1); m.castShadow = window.__QUALITY === 'high'; m.position.set(gx - cx, H(gx, gz) - 0.1, gz - cz); m.rotation.y = yaw; m.scale.set(1.3 * GW(a), 1.3, 1.3); m.receiveShadow = true; det.add(m); return m; }; gmO = mk('wlG'); gmC = mk('wlC'); gmC.visible = false; }
    for (const s of (GW(a) > 1 ? [-3.6, -1.8, 0, 1.8, 3.6] : [-1.3, 0, 1.3])) closeC.push([gx + tx * s, gz + tz * s, 1.4]);
    const inn = (PAL3D && a === 90) ? [cx, cz + ANX50.A.zN + 8] : P(CITY_WR - 8, a); for (const s of [-1, 1]) { L.gatePos.push([inn[0] + tx * s * (GW(a) > 1 ? 6 : 3.5), inn[1] + tz * s * (GW(a) > 1 ? 6 : 3.5)]); L.gateFace.push(Math.atan2(-Math.cos(a * D2R), Math.sin(a * D2R))); }

  try { const gts = (L.gates || []).map(q => [q.x, q.z]), ctx17 = { cx, cz, H, onHill, gates: gts, mk: { x: MKX, z: MKZ, hx: 50, hz: 25 } };
    const b17 = V17.build(L, L.v17rm, ctx17), d17 = window.V18 && L.v18 ? V18.dress(L, ctx17) : V17.dress(L, ctx17); console.info('[v17] المدينة المرتبة', JSON.stringify({ snap: V17.ST.snap, build: b17, dress: d17 })); } catch (e) { console.warn('[v17] build', e); }

  // ── v15: نخيل على جانبي الشوارع الشعاعية (نسخ مكررة، لا مصادمات، ظل على الجودة العالية فقط) ──
  { if (!C15.palm) { const parts = [], col = [], V3 = THREE.Vector3, TH = 6.6, NS = 7;
      const lean = y => 0.55 * Math.pow(y / TH, 2);
      for (let i = 0; i < NS; i++) { const y0 = i * TH / NS, y1 = (i + 1) * TH / NS, r0 = 0.27 - 0.1 * i / NS, r1 = 0.27 - 0.1 * (i + 1) / NS;
        const g = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(r1, r0 * 1.06, y1 - y0, 7, 1, true).toNonIndexed(); const p = g.attributes.position;
        for (let k = 0; k < p.count; k++) { const yy = p.getY(k) + (y0 + y1) / 2; p.setXYZ(k, p.getX(k) + lean(yy), yy, p.getZ(k)); }
        const sh = i % 2 ? 0.86 : 1; parts.push([g, [0.46 * sh, 0.34 * sh, 0.22 * sh], [0.36 * sh, 0.26 * sh, 0.17 * sh]]); }

        for (let s = 0; s <= N; s++) { const t = s / N, w = 0.62 * Math.sin(Math.PI * Math.min(1, t * 1.1)) + 0.05, cxp = top.x + dx * L * t, cyp = top.y + up * t * 1.6 - 2.4 * t * t, czp = top.z + dz * L * t;
          P.push([cxp - dz * w, cyp - 0.18 * w, czp + dx * w], [cxp, cyp + 0.12, czp], [cxp + dz * w, cyp - 0.18 * w, czp - dx * w]); }
        const pos = []; for (let s = 0; s < N; s++) { const a = P[s * 3], b = P[s * 3 + 1], c = P[s * 3 + 2], d = P[s * 3 + 3], e = P[s * 3 + 4], g2 = P[s * 3 + 5]; pos.push(...a, ...d, ...b, ...b, ...d, ...e, ...b, ...e, ...c, ...c, ...e, ...g2); }
        const g = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); g.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(pos, 3)); g.computeVertexNormals(); const gg = 0.85 + 0.15 * ((f * 7) % 3) / 2;
        parts.push([g, [0.30 * gg, 0.52 * gg, 0.16 * gg], [0.18 * gg, 0.34 * gg, 0.1 * gg]]); }

      for (const [g, cTop, cBot] of parts) { const p = g.attributes.position; P.set(p.array, o * 3); Nn.set(g.attributes.normal.array, o * 3);
        let ymin = 1e9, ymax = -1e9; for (let k = 0; k < p.count; k++) { ymin = Math.min(ymin, p.getY(k)); ymax = Math.max(ymax, p.getY(k)); }
        for (let k = 0; k < p.count; k++) { const t = ymax > ymin ? (p.getY(k) - ymin) / (ymax - ymin) : 1; for (let q = 0; q < 3; q++) C[(o + k) * 3 + q] = cBot[q] + (cTop[q] - cBot[q]) * t; } o += p.count; }
      const pg = new THREE.BufferGeometry(); pg.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(P, 3)); pg.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(Nn, 3)); pg.setAttribute('color', new THREE.BufferAttribute(C, 3)); pg.computeBoundingSphere(); SHARED.add(pg);
      C15.palm = { geo: pg, mat: new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.86, side: THREE.DoubleSide }) }; }

    if (window.__V16_TREES) for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) { const deg = k * 45, [ex, ez] = P(10, deg), ux = (ex - cx) / 10, uz = (ez - cz) / 10, txp = -uz, tzp = ux, off = deg === 270 ? 9.5 : 6.3;
      for (const r of [40, 55, 70, 85, 99]) for (const s of [-1, 1]) { if (deg === 270 && r < 75) continue; const x = cx + ux * r + txp * off * s, z = cz + uz * r + tzp * off * s;
        if (Math.hypot(x - pcx, z - pcz) < HR + 7) continue; pos.push([x, z]); } }

    if (!pos.length) pos.push([pcx, pcz]);   // v16: نسخة واحدة مخفية تحت القصر بدل شبكة فارغة

  // ── رصف ساحة السوق (مستطيل) وأرض ساحتي التدريب (v6) ──
  { const NX = 14, NZ = 4, QP2 = [], QI2 = []; for (let k = 0; k <= NZ; k++) for (let i = 0; i <= NX; i++) { const x = MKX - 38 + 76 * i / NX, z = MKZ - 13 + 26 * k / NZ; QP2.push(x - cx, H(x, z) + 0.2, z - cz); }

    const pg2 = new THREE.BufferGeometry(), pn2 = new Float32Array(QP2.length); for (let i = 1; i < pn2.length; i += 3) pn2[i] = 1; pg2.setAttribute('position', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(QP2, 3)); pg2.setAttribute('normal', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pn2, 3)); pg2.setIndex(QI2);
